The dry-run service **reuses the Phase 16E `integration_hardening` readiness** for payment + logistics status (no duplicated provider logic) and reads settings + kill-switch + sandbox + Claim-Vault coverage + team roles to build a 12-gate matrix (lead/customer data, calling outcome flow, order creation, confirmation flow, payment readiness [live blocked], shipment readiness [live blocked], delivery/RTO, WhatsApp automation [blocked], Vapi/AI calling [blocked], Claim Vault seed [warning if demo], team roles, safety state). A dry-run records `gate_results` / `blocked_reasons` / `safety_snapshot` and always sets `provider_actions_attempted=false` + `provider_actions_blocked=true`. **Every Phase 16F path is internal/dry-run only — it NEVER creates a live Razorpay/PayU payment link, captures/refunds, books a live Delhivery AWB, sends WhatsApp/Meta Cloud, places a Vapi call, calls any AI/LLM provider, enqueues a business Celery job, or mutates `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState`** — asserted by a defensive test patching `queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `trigger_call_for_lead` / `razorpay_client.create_payment_link` / `delhivery_client.create_awb` (all `assert_not_called`). Linked Lead / Customer / Order / imported-campaign rows are referenced, never mutated. The Director review/sign-off endpoint force-locks the live-provider-gate checklist item to "NOT approved". - **Phase 16F (2026-05-30) — Controlled Internal Pilot Readiness + End-to-End Dry Run IMPLEMENTED + PUSHED (VPS production verification pending).** Internal pilot/dry-run command center; reuses the Phase 16E readiness service. **Internal/dry-run only — no live provider call, no live automation, no Phase 15 safety-shell change.**
  1. **Pilot readiness** (`GET /api/v1/pilot/readiness/`, auth) — a read-only composite: safety snapshot (AI Paused / Sandbox OFF / Sync Live / live-actions-locked, reusing `integration_hardening.safety_summary`), broad-automation flag snapshot (AI_CALLING / WHATSAPP_* / MCP / WHATSAPP_PROVIDER / VAPI_MODE), Razorpay + PayU + Delhivery readiness (reusing `integration_hardening.payment_logistics_readiness`), Claim Vault production-seed status (reusing `compliance.coverage.build_coverage_report` — warning if demo-only), team-role presence, data counts, a **12-gate matrix** (lead/customer data, calling outcome flow, order creation, confirmation flow, payment readiness [live blocked], shipment readiness [live blocked], delivery/RTO, WhatsApp automation [blocked], Vapi/AI calling [blocked], Claim Vault seed, team roles, safety state), a blocked-live-actions list, and the Director sign-off checklist keys.
  2. **Dry-run create + evaluate** (`POST /api/v1/pilot/dry-runs/`, director/admin) — creates a `PilotDryRun` (scenario ∈ fresh_lead / imported_campaign / existing_order / payment_logistics / full_lifecycle), evaluates the gate matrix, and persists `gate_results` / `blocked_reasons` / `safety_snapshot` + a verdict (passed / warning / blocked). It ALWAYS sets `provider_actions_attempted=false` + `provider_actions_blocked=true`. Provider live-gate blocks are the EXPECTED safe state (they do NOT fail the dry-run); a provider gate showing *warning* (e.g. automation flag ON) blocks the verdict. Optional references to an existing Lead / Customer / Order / imported campaign / queue item are validated to exist and stored — never mutated.
  3. **Dry-run detail + review** (`GET /api/v1/pilot/dry-runs/<id>/`; `POST .../review/`, director/admin) — detail returns the full gate results + decisions; review records a `PilotDecision` (reviewed / approved_for_next_phase / deferred / blocked) with a note + sign-off checklist. The review **force-locks `live_provider_gate_not_approved=true`** so the internal surface can never authorise a live action.

- **Phase 16E (2026-05-28) — Payment / Logistics Integration Hardening implemented.** Hardening + controlled-readiness phase (NOT broad live execution). **Internal/read-only — no live provider call, no live automation, no Phase 15 safety-shell change.**
  1. **Payment readiness layer.** New `apps.integration_hardening.services.payment_logistics_readiness()` computes, from settings (presence checks only — never secret values) + the runtime kill switch + sandbox state: **Razorpay** {mode mock/test/live-gated, configured, liveEnabled, liveGateRequired, liveGatePresent, status, blockedReasons, safeActions}; **PayU** = **unavailable** (no adapter — only a `Payment.Gateway.PAYU` enum + a `payu_mock_*` fallback in `apps.payments.services`; documents the missing merchant-key/salt requirement; **no PayU SDK / third-party dependency added**); plus a `safety_summary()` (AI Paused / Sandbox OFF / live-actions-locked) and `recent_events()` (recent Payment + Shipment rows, AWB/gateway-ref masked to last-6, no PII).
  2. **Logistics readiness layer.** `delhivery_readiness()` reports mode (mock/test/live-gated/unavailable), configured (token + base URL presence), live blocked without a Director gate, and that live HTTP shipment creation is refused.
  3. **`ShipmentViewSet.create()` hardened** (`apps/shipments/views.py`). Before: always called `create_mock_shipment` (an alias for `create_shipment`) which silently delegated to the Delhivery adapter and *would* hit the live production network when `DELHIVERY_MODE=live`. After: explicit mode dispatch — `mock` → deterministic AWB (no network, unchanged); `test` → Delhivery **staging** adapter (existing Phase 2C capability + test preserved); `live` → **HTTP 409 `live_delhivery_booking_blocked` "Live Delhivery booking blocked — Director live gate required."**; unknown → HTTP 400. The HTTP endpoint can no longer book a live production AWB; the controlled CLI-only Phase 7G-Live gate (untouched) remains the only live path.
- **Backend (16E):** new app `apps.integration_hardening` (`services.py`, `permissions.py` (`IsAuthenticatedReadOnly`), `views.py` (2 read-only APIViews), `urls.py`, `apps.py`; **NO models, NO migration**). Registered in `INSTALLED_APPS`; mounted at `/api/v1/integrations/`. Hardened `apps/shipments/views.py` (`ShipmentViewSet.create` + `_delhivery_mode` helper). **No change to the Razorpay/Delhivery adapters (already mode-safe), no `.env*` edit, no existing payment/shipment gate model touched.**
- **Endpoints (16E):** `GET /api/v1/integrations/payment-logistics/readiness/` (auth; payment + logistics readiness + safety summary + order-workflow gates) and `GET /api/v1/integrations/payment-logistics/recent-events/?limit=N` (auth; masked recent payment + shipment records). Both read-only. **No POST test-payment / test-shipment endpoint was added** (per the directive's "if test actions are unsafe/unclear, return read-only readiness only") — the existing safe mock flows (`POST /api/payments/links/`, `POST /api/shipments/`) are unchanged except for the explicit-mode shipment hardening.
- **Frontend (16E):** new page `frontend/src/pages/PaymentLogistics.tsx` (`/operations/payment-logistics` — safety banner, AI-Paused/Sandbox/live-locked chips, Razorpay + PayU + Delhivery readiness cards with mode/status/blocked-reasons/safe-actions, order-workflow gate rows, recent internal events, loading/error/empty states, NO live action button); route in `App.tsx`; sidebar entry (Operations group, Wallet icon); new types in `types/domain.ts`; 2 `api` methods + mock fixtures.
- **Tests (16E):** `backend/tests/test_phase16e_payment_logistics.py` — 13 tests (readiness auth, provider status without secrets, PayU unavailable, live Razorpay + live Delhivery blocked without gate, mock shipment still works, live shipment HTTP 409 + `create_awb` not called + no Shipment row, test-mode routes to staging SDK (not production), recent-events masking, defensive no-provider-call + safety-state-unchanged). `frontend/src/test/phase16e_payment_logistics.test.tsx` — 7 tests. **Verification: backend 13 + 22 + 17 + 30 = 82 passed; collect-only clean across 108 files; `makemigrations --check` + `manage.py check` clean; frontend 328/328, lint 0 errors, build green.**
- **Safety (16E):** no live Razorpay/PayU link/capture/refund, no live PayU API, no live Delhivery AWB, no WhatsApp/Meta Cloud, no Vapi, no AI/LLM provider, no business Celery enqueue, no `RuntimeKillSwitch`/`SandboxState` change, no `.env*` edit; secrets surfaced as presence booleans only. **PRODUCTION VERIFIED + CLOSED at `36395f6` (VPS browser validation passed). - **Phase 16D (2026-05-28) — Uploaded Customer Data Campaigns + Calling Lifecycle implemented** (subsequently PRODUCTION VERIFIED — see the verification entry above). The Director changed the post-16C priority from "Payment / Logistics Integration Hardening" to uploaded-customer-data workflow; Payment / Logistics is re-sequenced to Phase 16E (since shipped + verified at `36395f6`). **Internal-only — no provider call, no live automation, no Phase 15 safety-shell change.**
  1. **Data upload / dataset management.** `POST /api/v1/imports/datasets/upload/` (director/admin) accepts a CSV text blob (`{name, csv, sourceLabel?, problemCategory?, originalFilename?}`; ~5MB cap, 5000-row cap), auto-detects column headers (wide alias map: name/customer, phone/mobile/contact/whatsapp, problem/disease/category, city, state, notes/remark, product/medicine, source, old status, last order date), and writes one `ImportedDataset` + N `ImportedDataRow` rows. **No Lead/Customer/Order is created at upload.** CSV-only (Python `csv` module — no new dependency; XLSX intentionally out of scope).
  2. **Validation + dedup (phone-only).** Per row: missing name/phone → `missing_required`; phone that doesn't normalize to 10 digits → `invalid_phone`; normalized phone already seen in the file → `duplicate_in_file`; normalized phone matching an existing Lead (`crm.find_lead_by_phone`) or Customer → `duplicate_existing`; else `valid`. Email is NOT a dedup key (same email + different phone → both valid). Reuses the canonical `apps.crm.services.normalize_phone` / `find_lead_by_phone`.
  3. **Calling campaign + queue.** `POST /api/v1/imports/datasets/<id>/create-campaign/` (director/admin) creates an `ImportedCallingCampaign` + one `ImportedCallQueueItem` (status `pending`) per VALID row. Campaign carries denormalized counters (total/pending/completed/interested/not_interested/callback/wrong_number/order_created).
  4. **Call outcome recording.** `POST /api/v1/imports/queue/<id>/outcome/` (director/admin/operations) records a manual outcome; maps to queue status + increments attempts; `medical_emergency` sets `escalation_flag="medical_emergency"` (UI warning), `angry_escalation` sets `senior_review`. No provider is contacted.
  5. **Order creation from imported data.** `POST /api/v1/imports/queue/<id>/create-order/` (director/admin/operations) requires an `interested` item, creates an **internal** `Order` (stage `Order Punched`) via `apps.orders.services.create_order` (pure DB insert), best-effort links/creates a Lead, flips the item to `order_created`, marks the row `imported`. The existing confirmation/order lifecycle continues from there. **No payment/courier/WhatsApp side effect.**
  6. **Dashboard.** `GET /api/v1/imports/overview/` returns dataset count, valid contacts, duplicates, invalid, active campaigns, pending calls, interested rate, orders created.

- **Phase 16B-Hotfix-2 (2026-05-27) — Phone-only Lead Uniqueness + Orders Responsive Pipeline SHIPPED.** Fixes the 2 remaining browser-validation issues after Hotfix-1. **No migration, no env edit, no Phase 15 safety-shell change, no provider side-effect.**
  1. **Lead uniqueness is now phone-only.** Root cause: `_check_lead_duplicate` (in `backend/apps/crm/services.py`) blocked on **both** phone AND email — so a Director who changed the phone but kept the same email was wrongly blocked as "Duplicate email". Fix: email is now optional metadata, **NOT** a uniqueness key. New `normalize_phone()` helper produces a canonical 10-digit key (strips spaces/dashes/brackets/dots/plus; collapses `+91XXXXXXXXXX` / `91XXXXXXXXXX` / `0XXXXXXXXXX` / bare `XXXXXXXXXX` → `XXXXXXXXXX`). New `find_lead_by_phone()` narrows candidates via the `crm_lead_phone_idx` (`phone__contains=key`) then confirms by normalizing each candidate in Python so formatting differences collapse to the same key. `_check_lead_duplicate(phone=…)` no longer takes/uses `email`. `create_lead` raises `LeadDuplicateError(field_name="phone")` only on a normalized-phone match; **same email + different phone now creates a new Lead**; same normalized phone + different email is still blocked. CSV import dedup is likewise phone-only + normalized (in-CSV and against-DB), tracking a `seen_phones_in_csv` set of normalized keys (email tracking removed). The `LeadViewSet.create` 409 response now returns `detail: "Duplicate phone blocked — existing lead found."`, `field: "phone"`, `duplicate_field: "phone"`, `existingLeadId` + `existing_lead_id` (no full PII). **`NewLeadModal` always shows "Duplicate phone blocked — existing lead found."** and never an "email" duplicate message; modal stays open on the phone duplicate, closes on success.
  2. **Orders Pipeline — no horizontal scrolling.** Root cause: `frontend/src/pages/Orders.tsx` rendered the 10 stages in an `overflow-x-auto` + `flex min-w-max` horizontal kanban that forced left/right scrolling. Fix: replaced with a responsive **wrapped grid** `grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4 min-w-0` (`data-testid="orders-stage-grid"`) — stages wrap into rows (1 col mobile / 2 tablet / 3 desktop / 4 on 2xl), each column `min-w-0` so card content truncates instead of forcing a page-level horizontal scrollbar. The "Manage status" per-card affordance + the safe-copy hint banner ("Internal status update only — no WhatsApp / payment / courier action.") + the `OrderDetailSheet` transition flow are all preserved.
- **Backend changes (Hotfix-2):** `backend/apps/crm/services.py` (`normalize_phone` + `find_lead_by_phone` + `_phone_digits` helpers; `_check_lead_duplicate` phone-only; `create_lead` drops email from dedup; `import_leads_csv` phone-only normalized dedup; `LeadDuplicateError` docstring), `backend/apps/crm/views.py` (409 response message + field fixed to "phone"). **No model change, no migration** — phone-only is a pure service-layer change (the Lead model never had a unique email constraint; email was only enforced in the service layer).
- **Frontend changes (Hotfix-2):** `frontend/src/components/leads/NewLeadModal.tsx` (phone-only duplicate message, reads `existingLeadId`/`existing_lead_id`), `frontend/src/pages/Orders.tsx` (responsive wrapped stage grid). **Phase 15 safety shell untouched.**
- **Tests (Hotfix-2):** backend — replaced the email-duplicate test with `test_create_lead_same_email_different_phone_is_allowed` + `test_create_lead_same_phone_different_email_is_blocked` + `test_normalize_phone_collapses_indian_formats` + `test_create_lead_phone_duplicate_across_formats_is_blocked` + `test_post_leads_same_email_new_phone_succeeds` + `test_import_leads_csv_same_email_different_phone_creates` + `test_import_leads_csv_phone_duplicate_across_formats`; **backend Phase 16B suite now 30 passed**. Frontend — updated the duplicate test to assert "Duplicate phone blocked — existing lead found." + never "email" + added a same-email/new-phone success test + 2 Orders responsive-layout tests (wrapped grid present, no `min-w-max` horizontal scroller, Manage-status + safe banner preserved); **frontend 298/298 passed**. Lint 0 errors; build green; `makemigrations --check` clean; `manage.py check` clean.

- **Phase 16B-Hotfix-1 (2026-05-27) — Customer Lifecycle UI Validation Fixes SHIPPED (frontend-only).** Fixes the 4 browser-validation issues found after Phase 16B. **No backend code, no migration, no env edit, no Phase 15 safety-shell change, no provider side-effect.**
  1. **Duplicate lead now correctly blocked in the UI.** Root cause: `safeMutate` (in `frontend/src/services/api.ts`) caught the backend's legitimate HTTP 409 duplicate response and **fell back to the optimistic mock** (`optimisticLead(payload)`), so the modal received a fake "created" Lead and showed a success toast. Fix: new exported `ApiError` class (carries `httpStatus` + parsed `body`) + `isApiError()` predicate. `safeMutate` now parses a real HTTP error RESPONSE body and throws `ApiError` instead of substituting the optimistic mock; only a genuine network/offline failure (where `fetch` itself rejects → not an `ApiError`) still falls back (offline resilience preserved, asserted in tests). `NewLeadModal` now checks `isApiError(err) && err.httpStatus === 409`, reads the structured `{field, existingLeadId}` body, shows the exact message **"Duplicate lead blocked — existing lead found."**, renders the inline duplicate banner with the existing Lead id, keeps the modal **open**, and fires **no** created-success toast. Backend duplicate semantics were already correct (409); only the frontend masking was wrong.
  2. **Leads CRM table S.N. column.** `frontend/src/pages/Leads.tsx` now has a first `S.N.` column rendering `idx + 1` over the **current filtered/search result set** (display index, NOT the database id). Empty-state `colSpan` bumped 7 → 8. Re-indexes when filters/search change (asserted in tests).
  3. **Customer 360 right-rail overflow fixed.** `frontend/src/pages/Customers.tsx`: outer grid → `lg:grid-cols-[300px_minmax(0,1fr)]` + `min-w-0`; inner tabs/right-rail grid → `lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_340px]` + `min-w-0` on the grid, the profile column, and the `Tabs`. `TabsList` made `flex-wrap h-auto` so the 8 tab triggers wrap instead of pushing width. The `minmax(0,1fr)` + `min-w-0` combination stops wide tab content (tables / long timelines) from forcing horizontal page overflow; on smaller widths the 340px summary rail stacks below the profile. The Phase 16B tab tables already carry `overflow-x-auto` so a wide table scrolls within its card.
  4. **Orders transition discoverability.** `frontend/src/pages/Orders.tsx`: added a visible hint banner above the kanban (`data-testid="orders-transition-hint"`) carrying the exact safe copy **"Internal status update only — no WhatsApp / payment / courier action."**, plus a per-card "Manage status" affordance (`ArrowRightLeft` icon, opacity-on-hover) so operators can see that clicking a card opens the detail sheet with safe internal stage-transition buttons (the Phase 16B `OrderDetailSheet` already existed; this only makes it discoverable). Transition still calls the existing `api.transitionOrder` → `POST /api/orders/{id}/transition/`; success refreshes the list; failure shows an error toast; no provider / WhatsApp / payment / courier method fires (asserted in tests).
- **Tests (Phase 16B-Hotfix-1):** **+8 new frontend tests** in `frontend/src/test/phase16b_hotfix1_validation.test.tsx` (safeMutate throws `ApiError` on 409 + still falls back on network failure; Leads S.N. header + per-row serial + filtered re-index; Orders hint banner + card-opens-detail + transition-calls-api-and-refreshes-with-no-provider-calls + transition-error-toast). The existing Phase 16B duplicate test was rewritten to reject with a real `ApiError(409, {...})`, assert the "Duplicate lead blocked" toast, assert NO success toast, and assert the modal stays open. Verification: frontend **295/295 passed** (Phase 16B baseline 287 + 8 new); backend Phase 16B suite still **24/24** (no backend change); lint 0 errors; build green; `makemigrations --check` clean; `manage.py check` clean.

No WhatsApp / Razorpay / PayU / Delhivery / Vapi / AI-provider call, no Celery enqueue, no `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` change, no `.env*` edit.

- **Phase 16B (2026-05-27) — Customer Lifecycle UI Backbone SHIPPED.** First post-Phase-15M business-execution phase. Wires existing backend services to frontend action surfaces + adds Lead consent fields + Customer 360 timeline + CSV lead import. **6 implementation areas:**
  1. **Confirmation queue wire-up** — `frontend/src/pages/Confirmation.tsx` Confirmed / Rescue needed / Cancelled buttons now call the existing `POST /api/orders/{id}/confirm/` endpoint (backed by `apps.orders.services.record_confirmation_outcome`). Loading state per button, success / error toast, list refresh after each action. Toast-only stubs removed.
  2. **Customer 360 tab hydration** — new `GET /api/customers/{id}/timeline/` endpoint (read-only, admin-only) returns `{calls, orders, payments, shipments}` in a single round-trip. `frontend/src/pages/Customers.tsx` Calls / Orders / Payments / Delivery tabs now hydrate from this endpoint with per-tab loading / error / empty states. Hardcoded mock call timeline removed.
  3. **Order kanban transition buttons** — `frontend/src/pages/Orders.tsx` detail sheet now exposes safe internal stage transitions (NEW_LEAD → INTERESTED → PAYMENT_LINK_SENT → ORDER_PUNCHED → CONFIRMATION_PENDING) via the existing `POST /api/orders/{id}/transition/` endpoint. Confirmation Pending → Confirmation Queue page (no bypass). Dispatched / Delivered / RTO deliberately NOT exposed (those require their own backend workflows).
  4. **Manual Create Lead form** — new `frontend/src/components/leads/NewLeadModal.tsx` replaces toast-only "New Lead" button. Fields: name + phone (required) + email + source + disease/category + state + city + notes + 3 consent checkboxes (default off). Submits to existing `POST /api/leads/`. 409 duplicate response surfaces inline duplicate banner with existing Lead id.
  5. **CSV Lead Import** — new endpoint `POST /api/leads/import-csv/` (`apps.crm.views.LeadImportCsvView`) parses CSV with safe header alias map (15 aliases), enforces `name` + `phone` required columns, returns summary `{totalRows, createdCount, duplicateCount, errorCount, createdLeadIds, rowErrors, truncatedErrorList}`. Phone digits in error rows masked to last-4. Duplicates within CSV OR against existing Leads are **skipped, never overwritten**. New `frontend/src/components/leads/LeadImportModal.tsx` (paste OR file-upload) replaces toast-only "Import" button.
  6. **Lead consent fields + duplicate detection** — new migration `crm.0004_phase16b_lead_consent_fields` adds `consent_call`, `consent_whatsapp`, `consent_marketing` (default `false`), `email`, `notes`, `disease_category` to `Lead` model + cheap indexes on `phone` + `email`. `apps.crm.services.create_lead` raises typed `LeadDuplicateError`; `LeadViewSet.create` returns HTTP 409 with `{duplicate, field, existingLeadId}` payload. `apps.crm.services.import_leads_csv` enforces same dedup. The Meta Lead Ads webhook ingest path passes `skip_dedup=True` because it has its own `meta_leadgen_id` idempotency model. **NOTE — superseded by Hotfix-2 (`00c3295`): the original Phase 16B build detected duplicates on phone OR email; Hotfix-2 made lead uniqueness PHONE-ONLY (normalized). - **Phase 12C (2026-05-18) — Post-Call WhatsApp Follow-up Automation V1 (Director-triggered queue-only) SHIPPED.** Next link in the Tier-4 calling stack after Phase 12B. When the classifier marks a `CallOutcomeRecord` as `connected_converted` (customer agreed → wants payment link) or `connected_callback` (customer asked us to call back), Phase 12C queues a WhatsApp follow-up suggestion. **The actual WhatsApp send is NEVER triggered automatically** — Phase 12C only writes a draft-status `Phase7ELiveBRealCustomerSendGate` row via the existing `apps.whatsapp.phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send.prepare_gate(...)` entrypoint at the operator's CLI request; the Director still owns the approve + execute via the existing Phase 7E-Live-B CLI commands (`PHASE7E_LIVE_B_REAL_CUSTOMER_SEND_ENABLED=true` runtime env, `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true`, kill switch, structured 15-min UTC window, `--confirm-phase7e-live-b-real-customer-send` flag — none of which Phase 12C bypasses). **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `apps.whatsapp.phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send.prepare_gate` is keyword-only with signature `(*, target_phone, target_customer_name, template_name, template_params=None, operator_name)` and accepts the **logical** template key (e.g. `"payment_reminder"`, not the Meta name `"nrg_payment_reminder"`); `APPROVED_TEMPLATE_NAMES = {"confirmation_reminder", "delivery_reminder", "rto_rescue", "reorder_reminder", "payment_reminder", "usage_explanation"}` (5 logical keys + 1); `nrg_payment_reminder` and `nrg_confirmation_reminder` both use the `{{1}} {{2}}` body shape with positional vars `customer_name` + `context`; `CallOutcomeRecord` has `OneToOneField(Call, on_delete=CASCADE)` and `review_status` choices `{pending, approved, applied, skipped}`; `Customer` model requires explicit `id` (CharField primary key) — no auto-generated id; Phase 12B classifier already runs daily at 07:00 IST so a 26h window catches all newly-classified outcomes from the previous day. **Implementation decisions** based on discovery: new model `PostCallFollowUpQueue` with one-to-one FK on `CallOutcomeRecord` (CASCADE — outcome deletion cascades) to enforce idempotency at the DB layer; denormalize `lead_id` + `lead_phone_last4` (last-4 masked only — never full E.164); 2 follow-up types, 7 statuses (`pending → needs_customer_setup → gate_prepared → dispatched | skipped | gate_prep_failed | sandbox_skipped`); `phase7e_gate_id` is an opaque IntegerField pointer (not a real FK to avoid app-coupling); `prepare_gate_for_follow_up` wraps the lazy `from apps.whatsapp.phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send import prepare_gate` call in a try/except so any failure from Phase 7E-Live-B (raise OR `ok=False`) records `gate_prep_failed` without raising; Customer lookup walks `phone=` first then `phone__endswith=last4` fallback (matches Phase 10B Hotfix-1 pattern); the `_CONTEXT_TEXT` Hinglish phrases deliberately do NOT embed a `payment_url` — Director adds the actual link via Phase 10C before sending so the warm follow-up step stays truly safe even if the gate is approved by mistake. **New model** (migration `calls.0008_phase12c_post_call_follow_up_queue`): `call_outcome` OneToOne FK on `CallOutcomeRecord` (on_delete=CASCADE); `lead_id` CharField(32); `lead_phone_last4` CharField(4) — never full E.164; `follow_up_type` (2 choices: `payment_reminder` / `callback_confirmation`); `status` (7 choices, default `pending`); `phase7e_gate_id` IntegerField nullable; `customer_found` BooleanField; `operator_note` TextField; `dispatched_at` DateTimeField nullable; `dispatched_by` CharField(120) (used as `skipped_by` for skipped rows); `metadata` JSONField (stores `outcome_not_yet_applied`, `skip_reason`, `gate_prepared_at`, `error`, `blockers` etc); `created_at` + `updated_at`; 3 indexes (`status`, `follow_up_type`, `-created_at`). **New service module** `apps.calls.post_call_followup` exports: `TRIGGER_OUTCOMES = {"connected_converted": "payment_reminder", "connected_callback": "callback_confirmation"}` (only these two outcomes qualify; every other outcome is a silent no-op), `_FOLLOW_UP_TEMPLATE` dict mapping follow_up_type → Phase 7E-Live-B logical key (`payment_reminder` → `"payment_reminder"`; `callback_confirmation` → `"confirmation_reminder"`), `_CONTEXT_TEXT` Hinglish phrases matching the `{{1}} {{2}}` template shape, `identify_follow_up_candidates(hours=26)` (QuerySet filter: `detected_outcome IN TRIGGER_OUTCOMES` + `follow_up__isnull=True` + `classified_at >= now-hours`; ordered oldest-first), idempotent `create_follow_up_entry(*, call_outcome, sandbox=False)` (returns `(existing, False)` if a row already exists for the outcome; refuses non-trigger outcomes with `PostCallFollowUpStateError`; sets `metadata.outcome_not_yet_applied=True` + `outcome_review_status` flag when `call_outcome.review_status != "applied"` so the Director sees that classifying-then-immediately-following-up is happening out of order; writes `call_followup.queued` audit), `bulk_identify_and_queue(hours=26, sandbox=False) -> {total_found, queued, already_existed, errors, sandbox}` (per-row exceptions caught and counted — one bad row never aborts the sweep), `prepare_gate_for_follow_up(*, follow_up_id, operator_name, sandbox=False) -> dict` (lazily imports Phase 7E-Live-B `prepare_gate`, looks up Customer via `Customer.objects.filter(phone=lead.phone).first()` then `phone__endswith=last4` fallback, wraps the call in try/except so any exception → `gate_prep_failed` status without raising; handles 4 deterministic outcomes: `sandbox_skipped` when `sandbox=True` (does NOT even call Phase 7E-Live-B), `needs_customer_setup` when no `Customer` row exists for the phone, `gate_prep_failed` when Phase 7E-Live-B raises OR returns `ok=False` with blockers, `gate_prepared` on success — persists the returned `gateId` on the queue row + writes `call_followup.gate_prepared` audit; refuses non-{pending,needs_customer_setup} status with `PostCallFollowUpStateError`; refuses blank operator), `mark_dispatched(*, follow_up_id, operator_name, note="") -> PostCallFollowUpQueue` (`gate_prepared → dispatched`; raises `PostCallFollowUpStateError` on wrong status; writes `call_followup.dispatched` audit), `skip_follow_up(*, follow_up_id, operator_name, reason="") -> PostCallFollowUpQueue` (`pending / needs_customer_setup / gate_prepared → skipped`; refuses `dispatched` or already-`skipped`; writes `call_followup.skipped` audit), `get_followups_summary() -> {total, by_status, by_follow_up_type}`. `PostCallFollowUpStateError` exception class for every transition refusal. **4 new CLI commands** in `apps/calls/management/commands/`: `python manage.py list_post_call_followups [--status STATUS] [--type TYPE] [--hours N=72] [--limit N=100] [--json]` (read-only Director review surface — masked phone last-4 only; never full E.164), `python manage.py prepare_post_call_followup_gate <follow_up_id> --operator-name NAME [--json]` (calls `prepare_gate_for_follow_up`; sandbox auto-detected via `apps.ai_governance.sandbox.is_sandbox_enabled`; prints next-step `approve_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate` hint on success; exits 1 with `REFUSED: <reason>` on state error), `python manage.py mark_followup_dispatched <follow_up_id> --operator-name NAME [--note TEXT] [--json]` (records what Director already did via Phase 7E-Live-B execute; exits 1 from non-`gate_prepared`), `python manage.py skip_post_call_followup <follow_up_id> --operator-name NAME [--reason TEXT] [--json]`. **New Celery task** `apps.calls.tasks.queue_post_call_followups_daily(hours=26)` runs at **08:30 IST** (env-shiftable via new settings `POST_CALL_FOLLOWUP_DAILY_HOUR=8` / `_MINUTE=30` / `_HOURS=26`); runs 90 minutes after the Phase 12B classifier at 07:00 IST so the freshly-classified outcomes get queued the same morning; refuses with `call_followup.daily_queue.blocked` audit when the Postgres-safe runtime kill switch is off OR sandbox mode is active (sandbox refusal is deliberate — we don't queue real-customer follow-ups from synthetic data); success writes `call_followup.daily_queue.completed`. **Beat schedule now carries 13 daily entries** (12 from Phase 12B + the new `post-call-followup-daily`). **3 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/calls/`: `followups/?status=&type=&limit=N` (paginated list, capped 200), `followups/<int:pk>/` (detail), `followups/summary/` (camelCased aggregate — `total`, `byStatus`, `byFollowUpType`); POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. URL ordering: `followups/summary/` registered BEFORE the dynamic `<int:pk>` route. Auth admin/director/owner/superuser only. **9 new audit kinds (each ≤ 64 chars)**: `call_followup.queued`, `call_followup.gate_prepared`, `call_followup.needs_customer_setup`, `call_followup.gate_prep_failed`, `call_followup.dispatched`, `call_followup.skipped`, `call_followup.sandbox_skipped`, `call_followup.daily_queue.completed`, `call_followup.daily_queue.blocked`. All audit payloads carry phone last-4 only — never full E.164. **Phase 12C NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER makes a call, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi, NEVER edits `.env.production`** — asserted across every path by patching all 4 outbound entrypoints (`queue_template_message`, `send_freeform_text_message`, `trigger_call_for_lead`, `create_shipment`) AND Phase 7E-Live-B `prepare_gate` in the sandbox path. The lifecycle `bulk_identify_and_queue → prepare → mark_dispatched` flow only adds: (a) `PostCallFollowUpQueue` rows, and (b) ONE Phase 7E-Live-B draft-status gate row per successful `prepare_gate_for_follow_up` — every other table row count is unchanged (asserted on Customer/Lead/Order/Payment/Shipment). Phase 12B regression: `test_beat_schedule_has_call_outcome_classification_daily` relaxed from `len == 12` to `len >= 12` for forward-compat. **46 new backend tests** in `tests/test_post_call_followup.py` cover: identify-candidates filter (converted/callback included, unclear excluded, existing follow-up excluded, window respected via `classified_at` past-30h push-back); idempotent `create_follow_up_entry`; refuses non-trigger outcomes; `outcome_not_yet_applied` flag set when `review_status != applied`; `TRIGGER_OUTCOMES` map size = 2; `bulk_identify_and_queue` counts + idempotent re-sweep; `prepare_gate_for_follow_up` happy path with Phase 7E-Live-B mocked + template_name + template_params shape verified + `gateId` persisted + `call_followup.gate_prepared` audit; callback path uses `confirmation_reminder` template; no-customer marks `needs_customer_setup` + Phase 7E-Live-B NOT called; sandbox marks `sandbox_skipped` + Phase 7E-Live-B NOT called; Phase 7E-Live-B exception marks `gate_prep_failed` (no raise); Phase 7E-Live-B `ok=False` with blockers marks `gate_prep_failed`; refuses wrong-status entry; requires non-blank operator; missing-row raises; `mark_dispatched` happy + refuses-pending + requires-operator; `skip_follow_up` from pending/gate_prepared/refuses-dispatched/refuses-already-skipped; Celery happy + kill-switch blocked + sandbox blocked; all 4 CLI commands (list + prepare success + prepare refused-exit-1 + mark dispatched + skip); API anonymous blocked + admin list/detail/summary + 404 + 405; defensive integration (no outbound on queue path / sandbox prepare does not even create Phase 7E-Live-B gate row / full lifecycle from queue → gate_prepared → dispatched does not mutate Customer/Lead/Order/Payment/Shipment row counts AND all 4 outbound entrypoints stay `assert_not_called`); `get_followups_summary` mixed-status counts; beat schedule sanity (`post-call-followup-daily` registered, total ≥ 13). Verification baseline after the new tests is **2730 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (46 new Phase 12C tests).

- **Phase 12B (2026-05-18) — Call Outcome Classifier V1 (deterministic, recommendations-only) SHIPPED.** Closes the Tier-4 calling loop started by Phase 12A. Post-execute, Vapi webhooks land Call + CallTranscriptLine rows via the Phase 2D path; Phase 12B reads them deterministically (zero LLM call) and **suggests** a `Lead.status` update — Director reviews + approves + manually applies via separate CLI. **V1 has NO auto-apply path.** **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `Lead.Status` has 8 choices (`New / AI Calling Started / Interested / Callback Required / Payment Link Sent / Order Punched / Not Interested / Invalid`); no dedicated Lead status-setter service — direct ORM `.save(update_fields=["status"])` is the pattern; `AiCallCampaignGate.leads_attempted` is JSONField storing lead IDs only (not call IDs); `Call.lead_id` is `CharField(max_length=32, db_index=True)` (denormalized reference, NOT a real FK) — Lead lookup uses `Call.objects.filter(lead_id=lead.id)`; `CallTranscriptLine` has `(active_call FK, call FK, order PositiveIntegerField, who CharField(40), text TextField)` ordered by `order`; SQLite (test DB) does NOT support JSON `contains` lookup, so `leads_attempted__contains=[lead_id]` must be a Python-side scan. **Implementation decisions** based on discovery: new model `CallOutcomeRecord` in `apps.calls.models` with one-to-one FK on `Call` to enforce idempotency at the DB layer; `apply_outcome_updates` mutates `Lead.status` only via direct ORM `.save(update_fields=["status"])` inside `transaction.atomic()`; campaign-gate linkage on the classified record is best-effort via a Python scan of the latest 50 `AiCallCampaignGate.leads_attempted` lists (engine-agnostic, cheap because Phase 12A campaigns are bounded at `AI_CALLING_MAX_PER_CAMPAIGN=20` leads); customer-side classifier uses the same permissive `{customer, user, human}` case-insensitive substring match Phase 11B introduced; the standalone 2-char `ha` is intentionally NOT in CONVERSION_SIGNALS because it false-matches inside `raha` / `raha hoon` (only `haan` is in the list); cascade priority is **rejection → conversion → callback → unclear** because Hinglish negations like `nahi chahiye` / `mujhe nahi` embed the conversion keyword `chahiye`/`lena` inside an explicit refusal — an explicit "no" must always beat an incidental "yes" keyword match. **New model** (migration `calls.0007_phase12b_call_outcome_record`): `call` OneToOne FK on `Call` (on_delete=CASCADE; enforces idempotency); `campaign_gate` FK on `AiCallCampaignGate` (nullable; on_delete=SET_NULL); `lead_id` CharField (denormalized); `current_lead_status` CharField (snapshot at classify time); `detected_outcome` (6 choices); `suggested_lead_status` CharField (blank for unclear/not-connected/no-transcript); `confidence` (high/medium/low); `evidence` JSONField; `review_status` (pending → approved → applied / skipped); `applied_at` / `applied_by` / `classified_at` / `scoring_version="deterministic_v1"`; 3 indexes (`review_status`, `detected_outcome`, `-classified_at`). **Outcome → Lead.status mapping**: converted → `Payment Link Sent`; callback → `Callback Required`; not_interested → `Not Interested`; unclear / not_connected / no_transcript → empty (apply skips blank suggestions). **Three deterministic V1 signal lists** in `apps.calls.outcome_classifier`: `CONVERSION_SIGNALS` 18 phrases (`haan`, `bilkul`, `zaroor`, `le lunga`, `le lungi`, `order`, `khareed`, `buy`, `payment`, `link bhejo`, `confirm`, `lena hai`, `chahiye`, `mangwa`, `mangwana`, `ok bhai`, `theek hai`, `kar lete hain`); `CALLBACK_SIGNALS` 16 phrases (`baad mein/me`, `call karo/karein`, `phir baat`, `thodi der`, `abhi busy/nahi`, `kal`, `shaam ko`, `subah`, `raat ko`, `ghar aake`, `callback`, `call back`, `later`); `REJECTION_SIGNALS` 15 phrases (`nahi chahiye`, `nahi lena`, `mujhe nahi`, `interested nahi`, `not interested`, `no`, `nahi`, `band karo`, `mat karo`, `number hatao`, `dnd`, `remove`, `koi zaroorat nahi`, `paise nahi`, `mehnga`). **Pure classifier `_classify_text`** (no DB access, exposed at module level so tests exercise the rule cascade directly without staging Calls): Missed/Failed/Queued → `not_connected` (high confidence); zero transcript lines → `no_transcript` (low); rejection signal found → `connected_not_interested` (high); else conversion signal found → `connected_converted` (high if multiple signals matched, else medium); else callback signal found → `connected_callback` (medium); else → `connected_unclear` (low). Cascade order is FIRMLY `rejection → conversion → callback → unclear` (asserted in `test_classify_call_rejection_beats_callback` AND `test_classify_call_not_interested_on_rejection_signal` which previously expected `nahi chahiye` to map to not_interested even though `chahiye` is in CONVERSION). **Main entrypoint** `classify_call(call_id) -> CallOutcomeRecord` — idempotent (returns existing record on re-run without writing a second audit row); populates `current_lead_status` from `Lead.objects.filter(pk=call.lead_id).first()`; populates `campaign_gate` via in-Python scan of `AiCallCampaignGate.objects.order_by("-executed_at")[:50]` matching `lead_id in (gate.leads_attempted or [])`; raises `ValueError` on missing call. `classify_campaign_calls(campaign_gate_id)` loops every `lead_id` in `gate.leads_attempted`, resolves the most recent Call via `Call.objects.filter(lead_id=lid).order_by("-created_at").first()`, counts `skipped_no_call` separately (lead with no Call row yet — the Vapi webhook hasn't landed; the daily sweep picks it up later). `classify_recent_calls(hours=24)` sweeps `Call.objects.filter(created_at__gte=cutoff).filter(outcome_record__isnull=True)`. **`apply_outcome_updates(*, operator_name, outcome_record_ids=None, sandbox=False) -> dict`** is the ONLY path that mutates `Lead.status`. Wrapped in `transaction.atomic()` so the `Lead.status` flip and `record.review_status="applied"` move together or not at all. Raises `ValueError` on blank `operator_name`. Filters to `review_status=APPROVED` rows; skips blank `suggested_lead_status` (counted as `skipped_blank`); when `sandbox=True` skips the actual Lead mutation but records the intent in a `call_outcome.applied` audit with `sandbox=True` + `skipped=True` (counted as `skipped_sandbox`); on success writes `call_outcome.applied` audit carrying `previous_lead_status` + `applied_lead_status` + `applied_by`. Per-row exceptions are caught and counted as `errors` — one bad row never aborts the whole sweep. **`approve_record(*, outcome_record_id, operator_name)`** transitions `pending → approved` and stamps `applied_by` (reviewer capture); raises `ValueError` on missing record or non-pending status. NEVER mutates Lead.status. **4 new CLI commands** in `apps/calls/management/commands/`: `python manage.py classify_call_outcomes [--call-id ID] [--campaign-id N] [--hours N=24] [--dry-run] [--json]` (default = recent-calls sweep; `--dry-run` skips persistence + audit emission; `--call-id` classifies one specific call; `--campaign-id` classifies all leads in a Phase 12A campaign), `review_call_outcomes [--status pending] [--campaign-id N] [--limit N=50] [--json]` (READ-ONLY Director review surface — pretty-print shows masked signal counts only, never raw customer text), `approve_call_outcome <outcome_record_id> --operator-name NAME [--json]` (pending → approved; prints next-step hint; NEVER mutates Lead.status), `apply_call_outcome_updates [--outcome-record-id N ...] --operator-name NAME --confirm-outcome-apply [--json]` (THE ONLY MUTATING CLI; refuses with exit 1 if `--confirm-outcome-apply` missing — enforced at the CLI layer; sandbox auto-detected via `apps.ai_governance.sandbox.is_sandbox_enabled` and propagated to the service which skips the actual Lead.status mutation while recording the intent). **New Celery task** `apps.calls.tasks.classify_call_outcomes_daily(hours=26)` runs at **07:00 IST** (env-shiftable via new settings `CALL_OUTCOME_CLASSIFICATION_DAILY_HOUR=7` / `_MINUTE=0` / `_HOURS=26`); 26h window so late Vapi webhooks from the prior evening are caught (overlaps with Phase 12A's morning calling start); refuses with `call_outcome.daily_classification.blocked` audit when (a) the Postgres-safe runtime kill switch is off OR (b) sandbox mode is active (sandbox refusal is deliberate — governance-state rows shouldn't be derived from synthetic data); success writes `call_outcome.daily_classification.completed`. **Beat schedule now has 12 daily entries** (11 from Phase 11C + the new `call-outcome-classification-daily`). **4 new audit kinds (each ≤ 64 chars)**: `call_outcome.classified` (per-call), `call_outcome.applied` (per-record; carries `previous_lead_status` + `applied_lead_status`), `call_outcome.daily_classification.completed`, `call_outcome.daily_classification.blocked`. **3 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/calls/`: `outcomes/?review_status=&outcome=&campaign_gate_id=&limit=N` (paginated, capped 200), `outcomes/<int:pk>/` (detail with full `evidence` JSON + camelCased fields), `outcomes/summary/` (`total` / `pendingCount` / `approvedCount` / `appliedCount` / `skippedCount` / `byOutcome` map). URL ordering: `summary/` registered BEFORE the dynamic `<int:pk>` route. POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. Auth admin/director/owner/superuser only. **Phase 12B NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER makes a call, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery, NEVER edits `.env.production`** — asserted across every path by patching all 4 outbound entrypoints (`queue_template_message`, `send_freeform_text_message`, `trigger_call_for_lead`, `create_shipment`) and checking `assert_not_called`. **`Lead.status` is mutated ONLY by `apply_outcome_updates`** with non-blank `--operator-name` + `--confirm-outcome-apply` flag + `review_status="approved"` + non-blank `suggested_lead_status` + sandbox-off. Sandbox skips the mutation but records intent. Asserted: classify path leaves `Customer / Lead / Order / Payment` row counts constant; apply path leaves `Lead` row count constant (only the `status` field flips) and `Customer / Order / Payment` counts constant. Phase 12A regression: gate test `test_beat_schedule_unchanged_at_11` relaxed from `== 11` to `>= 11`; Phase 11C `test_beat_schedule_has_caio_audit_daily` relaxed identically. Phase 9E + 11A/B/C + 12A regression: 183/183 still green after the relaxation. **40 new backend tests** in `tests/test_call_outcome_classifier.py` cover: every outcome rule (`not_connected` for Missed / Failed; `no_transcript` for Completed-empty; converted for conversion signal; not-interested for rejection signal; callback for callback signal; unclear when no signals); rejection-beats-callback for embedded `nahi`; rejection-beats-conversion for `nahi chahiye` containing `chahiye`; confidence levels (single-signal medium, multi-signal high); idempotency (second call returns existing record + only 1 audit row); missing-call ValueError; `OUTCOME_TO_LEAD_STATUS` map covers every enum value; Lead.status snapshot at classify; best-effort campaign-gate linkage via the in-Python scan (works on SQLite where JSON contains is unsupported); `classify_campaign_calls` summary including `skipped_no_call`; `apply_outcome_updates` happy path with audit + `applied_by` capture; blank-suggestion skip; sandbox-skip; approved-only filter (pending rows NEVER applied even with explicit ID list); specific-id filter (`outcome_record_ids=[r1.pk]` applies r1 but leaves r2 in approved); empty-operator ValueError; `approve_record` happy path + non-pending refusal; all 4 CLI commands (dry-run no DB write / persisted single-call / review list / approve / apply happy path / apply missing-confirm exits 1); Celery task happy path + kill-switch blocked + sandbox blocked (both routes refused before any DB write); API (anon blocked + admin list/detail/summary + 404 + 405); defensive integration (classify path leaves `Customer/Lead/Order/Payment` constant; apply path leaves `Lead` row count constant, mutates only the `status` field, leaves `Customer/Order/Payment` constant); beat schedule sanity (`call-outcome-classification-daily` registered, total = 12); `get_outcomes_summary` mixed-status counts. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2684 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (40 new Phase 12B tests).

- **Phase 12A (2026-05-18) — AI Calling Campaign Gate V1 (Director-approved Vapi outbound) SHIPPED.** First **Tier-4** piece. Phase 9E Calling Team Leader's first VPS run showed an 18% overall connection rate (Vaani-3 AI at 44% — best performer); Phase 12A wraps the existing `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` (Phase 2D) in a Director-approved campaign gate so AI outbound calls land on real Leads safely. **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `trigger_call_for_lead(*, lead: Lead, by_user: User, purpose: str = "sales_call") -> Call` is keyword-only, raises `VapiClientError` on adapter failure inside `@transaction.atomic` (Call row rolls back on raise); `Lead.status` has 8 choices (`New / AI Calling Started / Interested / Callback Required / Payment Link Sent / Order Punched / Not Interested / Invalid`); Lead has `phone` but **NO DND/opt-out field** (Customer has `consent_call` but the gate operates on Leads); Lead has **NO `last_called_at`/`call_count`** so frequency must be derived via `Call.objects.filter(lead_id=lead.id, created_at__gte=now-24h).exists()`; `VAPI_MODE` env var supports `mock`/`test`/`live` (default `mock`); `VAPI_ASSISTANT_ID` and `VAPI_PHONE_NUMBER_ID` env vars exist at settings.py lines 620-621; `AiCallCampaignGate` did NOT exist anywhere (fresh model); Phase 7E-Live-B gate pattern uses `draft → approved → executed/failed/cancelled` (Phase 12A adds an `executing` intermediate state because a campaign loops over multiple leads); Phase 7E-Live-B uses 15-min UTC window (Phase 12A uses 30-min per spec — `validate_within_director_window(max_window_seconds=1800)`). **Implementation decisions**: new model `AiCallCampaignGate` in `apps.calls.models` + migration `calls.0006_phase12a_ai_call_campaign_gate`; 6-status lifecycle (`draft → approved → executing → completed | failed | cancelled`); default allowed Lead stages `{New, AI Calling Started, Interested, Callback Required}` (anything in `BLOCKED_STAGES = {Payment Link Sent, Order Punched, Not Interested, Invalid}` is refused outright at prepare time even if Director explicitly tries to include it via `--stage`); single active campaign at a time (refuses prepare if any gate in `{draft, approved, executing}` exists); frequency cutoff configurable via `AI_CALLING_FREQUENCY_LIMIT_HOURS=24`. **New settings** (env-driven, defaults LOCKED off — `.env.production` is NEVER edited): `AI_CALLING_ENABLED=false`, `AI_CALLING_MAX_PER_CAMPAIGN=20`, `AI_CALLING_FREQUENCY_LIMIT_HOURS=24`. **New service module** `apps.calls.ai_calling_gate` exports `prepare_campaign_gate / approve_campaign_gate / execute_campaign_gate / inspect_campaign_gate / cancel_campaign_gate / AiCallCampaignGateError`. **`prepare_campaign_gate(*, operator_name, stage_filter=None, max_leads=None, ai_assistant_id="", operator_note="")`** — refuses on active-campaign-exists (`active_campaign_exists` code) or on stage_filter containing any blocked stage (`stage_filter_includes_blocked_stage`); defaults `stage_filter` to `DEFAULT_ALLOWED_STAGES` when empty; defaults `ai_assistant_id` to `settings.VAPI_ASSISTANT_ID`; clamps `max_leads` to `AI_CALLING_MAX_PER_CAMPAIGN`; queries `Lead.objects.filter(status__in=effective_stages).exclude(phone="")`, applies the frequency filter via a `Call.objects.filter(lead_id__in=..., created_at__gte=cutoff).values_list("lead_id", flat=True)` set lookup, takes the first `effective_max` eligible IDs ordered `-created_at`, writes a draft `AiCallCampaignGate` with `leads_selected=[lead_ids]`, sandbox flag propagated, and writes `ai_calling.campaign.prepared` audit row. **`approve_campaign_gate(*, gate_id, operator_name, intent, director_signoff)`** — requires non-empty `intent` (`intent_required`); refuses on non-draft gate (`gate_not_draft`); parses sign-off via `apps.saas.utc_window.parse_director_signoff_window` (missing markers → `director_signoff_missing_utc_window`); validates window via `validate_within_director_window(max_window_seconds=1800)` and tolerates the future-window blocker `now_outside_director_signoff_utc_window_before_start` alone (Director can approve a window that starts in the future) — every other validation failure (malformed timestamps, window > 30 min, end ≤ start, stale window > 24h old, window already ended) raises `director_signoff_window_invalid`; persists parsed `window_start_utc` / `window_end_utc` (the `ParsedWindow` dataclass uses these attribute names — not `start`/`end`) onto the gate + flips `recorded_signoff_window_valid=True`; writes `ai_calling.campaign.approved` audit. **`execute_campaign_gate(*, gate_id, operator_name, confirm_ai_calling=False)`** is the ONLY CLI path that may dispatch real Vapi calls. Refusal order (each writes `ai_calling.campaign.refused` audit with the blocker code): `gate_not_approved` (gate is in wrong status), `confirm_ai_calling_campaign_flag_required` (missing `--confirm-ai-calling-campaign`), `ai_calling_not_enabled` (env flag not set to `"true"`), `runtime_kill_switch_disabled` (Postgres-safe `-pk` ordering pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` row always wins), `approval_window_not_recorded` (gate has no parsed window), `approval_window_invalid_at_execute` (now ∉ [start, end] or window malformed). On entering the loop the task flips `status → executing` and records `vapi_mode_at_execute` + `sandbox` for audit replay. For each lead in `leads_selected`: re-validates stage against `gate.stage_filter` (skip code `stage_no_longer_eligible`); re-runs the 24h frequency check (skip `recently_called`); respects `_sandbox_active()` (skip `sandbox_skip` — NO Vapi call); respects `VAPI_MODE in {mock, test}` (skip `vapi_not_live_skip` — NO real Vapi call); else lazy-imports `from apps.calls import services` (so tests patching the symbol at this path resolve to the same callable the live path uses) and calls `services.trigger_call_for_lead(lead=lead, by_user=<synthetic user>, purpose="sales_call")`. Per-lead Vapi exceptions are caught and counted as `vapi_error` skips — one failure NEVER kills the campaign loop. Each per-lead outcome writes either `ai_calling.campaign.lead.dispatched` (phone masked last-4 only) or `ai_calling.campaign.lead.skipped` (with `reason` field). Final gate status flips to `completed`; summary audit `ai_calling.campaign.executed` carries `calls_attempted` / `calls_dispatched` / `calls_skipped` / `skip_reasons` map / `vapi_mode_at_execute` / `sandbox` / `duration_ms`. **`inspect_campaign_gate(gate_id)`** is strictly read-only — returns gate details + per-lead masked phone (last-4 only) + current Lead.status + recently-called boolean; never mutates anything. **`cancel_campaign_gate(*, gate_id, operator_name, reason="")`** allowed from `draft` or `approved` only; refused with `gate_not_cancellable` after execution starts. **5 new CLI commands**: `python manage.py prepare_ai_calling_campaign [--stage STAGE ...] [--max-leads N=20] [--assistant-id ID] --operator-name NAME [--operator-note TEXT] [--json]`, `inspect_ai_calling_campaign <gate_id> [--json]`, `approve_ai_calling_campaign <gate_id> --operator-name NAME --intent TEXT --director-signoff "BEGIN_UTC=...Z END_UTC=...Z" [--json]`, `execute_ai_calling_campaign <gate_id> --operator-name NAME --confirm-ai-calling-campaign [--json]`, `cancel_ai_calling_campaign <gate_id> --operator-name NAME [--reason TEXT] [--json]`. All 5 commands print a structured `REFUSED [<code>]: <message>` to stderr and exit 1 on `AiCallCampaignGateError`. **7 new audit kinds (each ≤ 64 chars)**: `ai_calling.campaign.prepared`, `ai_calling.campaign.approved`, `ai_calling.campaign.executed`, `ai_calling.campaign.cancelled`, `ai_calling.campaign.refused`, `ai_calling.campaign.lead.dispatched`, `ai_calling.campaign.lead.skipped`. **3 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/calls/`: `campaigns/?limit=N` (paginated list, capped 200), `campaigns/latest/` (most recent — 404 when empty), `campaigns/<int:pk>/` (detail). URL ordering: `latest/` registered BEFORE the dynamic `<int:pk>` route. Response shape camelCased (`aiAssistantIdLast4` — never the full Vapi assistant id; `recordedSignoffWindowStartUtc/EndUtc/Valid`; `vapiModeAtExecute`; `sandbox`; `callsAttempted/Dispatched/Skipped`). POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. **Beat schedule UNCHANGED at 11 entries** — Phase 12A is Director-triggered, not automated (V2 may add an automated calling sweep). **Phase 12A NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery, NEVER edits `.env.production`**. Asserted in the defensive safety test by patching all four outbound entrypoints (`queue_template_message`, `send_freeform_text_message`, `trigger_call_for_lead`, `create_shipment`) across every non-live path and asserting `trigger_call_for_lead.assert_not_called()` in: prepare / inspect / approve / execute under sandbox / execute under `VAPI_MODE=mock` / execute under `VAPI_MODE=test` / execute window-expired / execute kill-switch-off / cancel. In the **explicit live-path test** (`AI_CALLING_ENABLED=true` + `VAPI_MODE=live` + window inside + kill switch on + `--confirm-ai-calling-campaign`), `trigger_call_for_lead` IS called once per eligible lead while `queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `create_shipment` stay `assert_not_called` and `Order`/`Payment`/`Lead` row counts stay constant. **Cannot un-make a Vapi call** — there is no rollback path; individual call results land on `Call` rows via the existing Phase 2D Vapi webhook flow. **39 new backend tests** in `tests/test_ai_calling_gate.py` cover: every prepare outcome (happy / active-campaign refusal / frequency filter excludes recent / default stage filter excludes blocked stages / explicit stage_filter rejects blocked / max_leads clamp / empty-phone exclusion); every approve outcome (happy inside-window / future-window allowed / non-draft refusal / missing markers / window > 30 min / empty intent); every execute outcome (`AI_CALLING_ENABLED=false` / missing confirm / kill switch off / window expired / mock skip / test skip / sandbox skip / live mode dispatches per eligible lead / stage changed since prepare / `trigger_call_for_lead` exception counted as `vapi_error` skip / recently-called lead skipped at execute time); cancel from draft / approved / completed-refused; inspect shape; all 5 CLI commands (happy + refusal exit-1); API anonymous blocked + admin list/latest/detail + 404 + 405; full defensive integration (mock mode — `trigger_call_for_lead` not called, Customer/Order/Payment/Lead row counts unchanged; live mode — `trigger_call_for_lead` called once but `Order`/`Payment` row counts unchanged); beat schedule sanity (still 11). Verification baseline after the new tests is **2644 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (39 new Phase 12A tests).

- **Phase 11D (2026-05-16) — Learning Loop Gate V1 (Director-approved, human-reviewed paper trail) SHIPPED.** Closes the nd.md learning chain (*transcript → QA scoring → compliance review → CAIO audit → CEO/approved workflow before changing live prompts/playbooks*) by adding the **gate** at the "approved workflow" step. V1 is a **paper trail** system with NO auto-execution: CAIO (Phase 11C) auto-creates `LearningProposal` rows from RED findings, Director reviews via CLI, approves or rejects, then manually implements the change outside the platform and runs the implement command to record what was done. **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `LearningProposal` model did NOT exist anywhere (closest pre-existing thing was `apps.learning_engine.LearningRecording` which is a separate call-recording staging table — no collision); `PromptVersion` EXISTS in `apps.ai_governance.models` and Phase 11D MUST NOT mutate it; `ApprovalRequest` EXISTS in `apps.ai_governance.models` with status `{pending, approved, rejected, auto_approved, blocked, escalated, expired}` and modes `{auto, auto_with_consent, approval_required, director_override, human_escalation}` — **deliberately separate** from `LearningProposal` because ApprovalRequest is for AI-proposed business actions through `APPROVAL_MATRIX` (auto-approval possible) while LearningProposal is for human-reviewed learning-change requests (always Director, no auto-execute); `apps/learning/` did NOT exist (fresh app); `CaioAuditSnapshot.severity` is `CharField(choices=Green/Amber/Red)`, `agent_anomaly_flags` is `JSONField(default=dict)` mapping agent_name → list of anomaly codes, `weak_learning_indicators` is `JSONField(default=list)` of code strings. **Implementation decisions** based on discovery: new app `apps.learning` (registered in `INSTALLED_APPS`, mounted at `/api/v1/learning/` in `config/urls.py`); keep separate from `ApprovalRequest` per discovery rationale above; idempotency via service-level pending-duplicate check, not DB UniqueConstraint, so re-running CAIO daily doesn't pile up duplicate proposals while still allowing multiple non-overlapping proposals of the same type from different source agents. **New model** `LearningProposal` (migration `learning.0001_initial`): `source_agent` CharField(80) (e.g. `"caio_v1"`, `"director"`), `proposal_type` (5 choices: `compliance_remediation` / `script_review` / `knowledge_gap` / `process_improvement` / `agent_coaching`), `title` CharField(200), `status` (5 choices: `pending` / `approved` / `rejected` / `implemented` / `cancelled`; default `pending`; indexed), `impact_scope` (3 choices: `low` / `medium` / `high`; default `medium`), `evidence` JSONField (IDs + codes only — NEVER customer names or PII; CAIO writes `{caio_snapshot_id, severity, compliance_risk_call_count, compliance_risk_agent_labels[:10]}` etc.), `proposed_change_text` TextField (**INTERNAL ONLY — what Director should consider changing; NEVER customer-facing**), `director_decision` (pending/approved/rejected) + `director_note` + `reviewed_by` + `reviewed_at`, `implementation_note` + `implemented_at` + `implemented_by`, FK `caio_snapshot → CaioAuditSnapshot` (`on_delete=SET_NULL`), `created_at` + `updated_at`; 3 indexes on `status` / `proposal_type` / `-created_at`. **New service module** `apps.learning.service` ships pure functions: `create_proposal(*, source_agent, proposal_type, title, proposed_change_text, impact_scope='medium', evidence, caio_snapshot) -> (LearningProposal, bool)` — idempotent: if a PENDING proposal with the same `(source_agent, proposal_type)` already exists, returns `(existing, False)` without creating a duplicate; otherwise creates row + writes `learning.proposal.created` audit row whose payload carries `phase="11D"`, `proposal_id`, `proposal_type`, `status`, `source_agent`, `impact_scope`, `title[:120]`, `caio_snapshot_id` (truncated, no PII); validates non-blank `proposed_change_text`, valid `proposal_type` and `impact_scope`. `approve_proposal(*, proposal_id, operator_name, director_note='') -> LearningProposal` — PENDING → APPROVED; raises `LearningProposalStateError` on non-pending or missing proposal; writes `learning.proposal.approved` audit. `reject_proposal(...)` — PENDING → REJECTED; writes `learning.proposal.rejected`. `implement_proposal(*, proposal_id, operator_name, implementation_note) -> LearningProposal` — APPROVED → IMPLEMENTED; **enforces non-blank `implementation_note` at the service layer** so the CLI cannot bypass the requirement; raises `LearningProposalStateError` with the message *"implementation_note cannot be blank — Director must record what was actually done."* on empty notes; writes `learning.proposal.implemented` audit carrying `implementation_note_excerpt[:240]`; **this NEVER auto-applies any change** — Director already implemented manually, this only records what was done. `cancel_proposal(*, proposal_id, operator_name, reason='')` — PENDING or APPROVED → CANCELLED; refuses if IMPLEMENTED or REJECTED; writes `learning.proposal.cancelled`. `get_pending_proposals()`, `get_proposals_by_status(status)`, `get_proposals_summary()` (returns `{pending, approved, rejected, implemented, cancelled, high_impact_pending, total}`). `create_proposals_from_audit(snapshot, *, sandbox=False) -> {created_count, reused_count, skipped, proposals[]}` — CAIO integration helper. **Sandbox guard**: when `sandbox=True`, writes `learning.proposal.skipped_sandbox` audit and returns without creating ANY proposal rows (governance evaluation still runs, but production-changing proposals never land in sandbox runs). **Auto-creation rules** (all idempotent via the pending-duplicate check): compliance violation in any `agent_anomaly_flags` value OR `compliance_risk_call_count > 0` → high-impact `compliance_remediation` proposal with `proposed_change_text` referencing the forbidden-phrase list + Master Blueprint §26 + Claim Vault; `declining_call_quality` in `weak_learning_indicators` → medium-impact `script_review`; `no_recent_calls` → medium-impact `process_improvement`; `all_agent_utterances_missing` → medium-impact `agent_coaching`. **CAIO integration**: `run_caio_audit_agent_daily` in `apps/caio/tasks.py` now calls `create_proposals_from_audit(snapshot, sandbox=snapshot.sandbox)` after `snapshot.save()` and BEFORE the `caio.audit.snapshot.created` audit row is written; failures are caught defensively (logger.exception + zero-count summary) so a proposal-creation bug never breaks the CAIO audit chain. The task result dict now carries a top-level `learning_proposals` field with the `{skipped, created_count, reused_count}` summary. **3 new CLI commands** in `apps/learning/management/commands/`: `python manage.py list_learning_proposals [--status STATUS] [--type TYPE] [--limit N=20] [--json]` (read-only Director review surface; sorts created_at DESC; prints id / status / impact_scope / proposal_type / source_agent / reviewed_by / title; never mutates), `python manage.py review_learning_proposal <proposal_id> --decision approved|rejected --operator-name NAME [--note TEXT] [--json]` (calls `approve_proposal` / `reject_proposal`; on success prints next-step hint for `implement_learning_proposal`; on `LearningProposalStateError` prints `REFUSED: <reason>` to stderr and exits 1), `python manage.py implement_learning_proposal <proposal_id> --operator-name NAME --implementation-note TEXT [--json]` (records Director's manual implementation; refuses with exit 1 on blank `--implementation-note` at the CLI level AND the service level — defence in depth; this command **DOES NOT auto-apply anything**). **4 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/learning/`: `proposals/?status=&type=&limit=N` (paginated list, capped 200), `proposals/pending/` (shortcut for `status=pending`), `proposals/<int:pk>/` (detail with full `evidence` + `proposed_change_text` + `implementation_note`), `proposals/summary/` (camelCased aggregate counts — `pending`, `approved`, `rejected`, `implemented`, `cancelled`, `highImpactPending`, `total`); POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. URL ordering: `summary/` + `pending/` both registered BEFORE the dynamic `<int:pk>` route. Auth: admin / director / owner / superuser only (anonymous → 401/403). Response shape is camelCased (`sourceAgent`, `proposalType`, `proposedChangeText`, `directorDecision`, `directorNote`, `reviewedBy`, `reviewedAt`, `implementationNote`, `implementedAt`, `implementedBy`, `caioSnapshotId`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt`) for direct frontend consumption. **Phase 11D NEVER auto-implements any change** — every transition requires either an explicit Director CLI call or, for CAIO auto-creation, the pending-duplicate guard. **Phase 11D NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`** (asserted in tests by seeding a baseline `PromptVersion` row and verifying its count stays constant across the full CAIO → approve → implement flow), NEVER touches Vapi prompt config, NEVER triggers WhatsApp / makes a call / dispatches a shipment / mutates `Customer` / `Order` / `Payment` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`, NEVER reads or writes `ApprovalRequest` (Phase 4C surface stays completely untouched), NEVER edits `.env.production`. Asserted in the defensive safety test by patching `queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `trigger_call_for_lead` / `create_shipment`, snapshotting `Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` AND `PromptVersion` row counts before/after a full **CAIO daily run → proposal created → approve → implement** lifecycle, and asserting every count stays constant + every outbound entrypoint `assert_not_called`. **Beat schedule unchanged at 11 entries** — Phase 11D adds NO new beat task; proposals are created on demand by the existing `caio-audit-daily` task at 14:00 IST. **6 new audit kinds (all ≤ 64 chars):** `learning.proposal.created`, `learning.proposal.approved`, `learning.proposal.rejected`, `learning.proposal.implemented`, `learning.proposal.cancelled`, `learning.proposal.skipped_sandbox`. **35 new backend tests** in `tests/test_learning_loop_gate.py` cover: `create_proposal` (new + audit; duplicate pending reused via idempotency; blank text rejected; unknown type rejected), every transition (`approve` / `reject` / `implement` / `cancel`) with status guards + audit row + blank-note rejection at service level, `create_proposals_from_audit` (compliance_violation → high-impact remediation; clean snapshot → no proposals; idempotent re-run; sandbox → skipped audit + zero rows; declining_call_quality → script_review; no_recent_calls → process_improvement; all_agent_utterances_missing → agent_coaching), end-to-end CAIO task integration (seeds Phase 11B `CallQualityScore` with `compliance_violation` flag, runs `run_caio_audit_agent_daily`, asserts result carries `learning_proposals.created_count >= 1` AND a `compliance_remediation` proposal exists in PENDING), all 3 CLI commands (list with status filter; review approve + reject + already-decided exits 1; implement happy path + blank-note exits 1 at the CLI level), all 4 API endpoints (anonymous blocked + admin reads list/pending/detail/summary + 404 + 405), full defensive integration (`Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` / **`PromptVersion`** counts unchanged across CAIO → approve → implement + every outbound entrypoint `assert_not_called`), beat schedule sanity (still 11 entries; `caio-audit-daily` registered). Phase 11C regression: 34/34 still green after wiring the new hook. On the local dev DB (Phase 11A migration applied), running `python manage.py list_learning_proposals --status pending` after seeding one PENDING `compliance_remediation` proposal returned `#1 pending high compliance_remediation src=caio_v1 reviewed=- title: Compliance violation detected in recent calls` — confirming the Director review surface works end-to-end. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2605 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (35 new Phase 11D tests).

- **Phase 11C (2026-05-16) — CAIO Audit Agent V1 (recommendations-only governance layer, deterministic, no LLM) SHIPPED.** Third link in the calls-observability chain after Phase 11A (transcript ingestion) and Phase 11B (call quality scoring), and the first **governance** layer that audits both the Phase 9 business agents (including CEO AI Orchestration) and the Phase 11 calls pipeline. Per Master Blueprint §26 #2 hard stop: *"CAIO Agent never executes business actions. Monitor / audit / suggest only."* — Phase 11C is built strictly to that contract. **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `AgentRun.Agent.CAIO = "caio"` already exists at [apps/ai_governance/models.py:79](backend/apps/ai_governance/models.py#L79) so no `ai_governance` migration is needed; `apps/caio/` does NOT exist anywhere (fresh app). The five Phase 9 snapshot models surface different shapes — `CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` carries `business_health_score` / `health_tier` / `cross_cutting_alerts` / `top_3_priorities` / `agent_status_summary` / `alerts`; `CfoFinancialSnapshot` carries `alerts` / `revenue_30d` / `pending_amount` / `paid_count`; `DataAnalystSnapshot` carries `alerts` / `call_to_confirmed_rate` / `call_count_30d`; `CallingTeamLeaderSnapshot` carries `alerts` / `connection_rate_30d` / `transcript_backlog_count` / `call_count_7d`; `RtoRiskSnapshot` is per-order (not cohort) with `risk_tier` per row — to get cohort-level high/critical counts CAIO aggregates `RtoRiskSnapshot.objects.filter(created_at__gte=now-24h)`. Phase 11A `get_backlog_overview(window_days=30)` returns `{backlog_count, total_calls_in_window, ingested_count, oldest_backlog_at, newest_backlog_at, top_backlog, ...}`; Phase 11B `get_scoring_overview(window_days=30)` returns `{total_scored, backlog_count, avg_composite, low_compliance_count, top_flags, avg_by_agent, ...}`. **Implementation decisions** based on discovery: new app `apps.caio` (registered in `INSTALLED_APPS`, mounted at `/api/v1/caio/` in `config/urls.py`); reuse `AgentRun.Agent.CAIO` enum unchanged; reuse Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe `RuntimeKillSwitch` `-pk` ordering pattern for the daily task's kill switch (cloned from `apps.agents.ceo_orchestration.tasks._kill_switch_blocked`); read Phase 9 snapshots via direct `Model.objects.order_by("-snapshot_at").first()` per agent (RTO Prevention uses `-created_at` because it has no `snapshot_at`); read Phase 11A / 11B via the existing read-only overview helpers — never call Vapi directly. **New model fields** (migration `caio.0001_initial`): `snapshot_at`, `window_days` (default 30), `severity` (`green`/`amber`/`red`), `compliance_risk_call_count`, `compliance_risk_agent_labels` JSON, `transcript_backlog_count`, `call_quality_trend` (`up`/`flat`/`down`/`no_data`), `agent_data_gaps`, `agent_data_gap_names` JSON, `agent_anomaly_flags` JSON dict, `weak_learning_indicators` JSON list, `ceo_audit_notes` JSON list, `recommendation_text` (multi-line internal Director briefing, NEVER customer-facing), `audited_agents` JSON list, FK `agent_run → AgentRun` (`on_delete=SET_NULL`), `sandbox` bool, lifecycle timestamps; 2 indexes on `snapshot_at` + `severity`. **New service module** `apps.caio.service` ships pure functions: `gather_compliance_risk(window_days)` (reads Phase 11B `CallQualityScore` rows, filters in Python for `"compliance_violation" in flags`, groups by `agent_label`), `gather_transcript_backlog(window_days)` (delegates to Phase 11A `get_backlog_overview` — never calls Vapi), `gather_call_quality_trend(window_days)` (compares avg composite this 7d vs prior 7d with ±5% band — `TREND_UP_PCT=0.05` / `TREND_DOWN_PCT=-0.05` / `TREND_MIN_SAMPLES=3` per side), `gather_agent_snapshots()` (loads latest from each of the 5 Phase 9 agents, marks any whose latest is > 48h old — `AGENT_STALE_HOURS=48` — as `stale_agents`; `RtoRiskSnapshot` falls back to `created_at` since it has no `snapshot_at`), `compute_agent_anomaly_flags(snapshots)` (per-agent anomaly map lifted from each snapshot's `alerts` field — CFO codes `{revenue_drop_24h, rto_spike, high_pending_payments, low_order_volume}`, Calling codes `{low_connection_rate, high_transcript_backlog, no_calls_today, agent_concentration_risk}`, Data Analyst codes `{conversion_drop, lead_volume_drop, dead_end_calls, geographic_concentration_shift}` plus a `funnel_conversion_low` heuristic that fires when `call_to_confirmed_rate < 0.10 AND call_count_30d >= 5`, CEO codes `{ceo_health_critical, ceo_health_poor, data_gap}` from the `health_tier` + alerts, RTO `high_rto_risk_orders` when the rolling 24h cohort has any high/critical-tier row), `compute_weak_learning_indicators(...)` (rule-based detection of learning gap codes — `transcript_ingestion_stalled` if transcript_backlog > 20; `no_calls_being_scored` if quality backlog > 0; `all_agent_utterances_missing` if more than 50% of scored calls in window had `zero_agent_utterances` flag with minimum 4 calls; `declining_call_quality` if trend == down; `no_recent_calls` if Calling Team Leader `call_count_7d == 0`), `audit_ceo_ai(ceo_snapshot)` (structured observation strings: `"CEO health tier is {tier} (score N). Top priorities: ..."`, `"data_gap_count: N agent(s) missing"` when CEO alerts contain `data_gap`, plus one line per `agent_status_summary` entry whose status is `alert` or `missing`; `None` snapshot returns `["CEO AI snapshot not found - data gap"]`), `compute_severity(...)` (cascade: `red` when `compliance_risk_count > 0` OR `agent_data_gaps >= AGENT_DATA_GAP_RED_THRESHOLD=3`; `amber` when weak learning is non-empty OR any agent anomaly fires OR transcript_backlog > 20 OR trend == down; else `green`), `generate_recommendation_text(...)` (multi-line internal Director briefing — never customer-facing — covering severity rationale, compliance summary, transcript backlog, trend, data gaps, agent anomalies, weak learning indicators, CEO audit notes, and "Recommended Director actions (no auto-execution)" with explicit recommendations like `"Review call quality logs; provide coaching to flagged agent(s)"` / `"Check Celery beat is running on VPS; verify the N stale agent(s) ran their daily task"` / `"Urgent review required - see compliance_risk_agents + agent_data_gap_names above"`), and `build_snapshot(window_days=30) -> CaioAuditSnapshot` (assembles the snapshot instance WITHOUT persisting). **New Celery task** `apps.caio.tasks.run_caio_audit_agent_daily(window_days=30)` scheduled at **14:00 IST** via the new beat key `caio-audit-daily` (env-shiftable via `CAIO_AUDIT_DAILY_HOUR=14` / `_MINUTE=0` / `_WINDOW_DAYS=30`); runs 1 hour after Phase 9F CEO Orchestration at 13:00 IST so it reads the fresh CEO snapshot. The task refuses with `caio.audit.daily_run.blocked` audit when the Postgres-safe runtime kill switch is off (same `-pk` Postgres-safe helper as every Phase 9-11 task). **Sandbox mode does NOT block CAIO** (governance must run even in sandbox to catch hallucinations / weak learning during sandboxed dry-runs); instead `snapshot.sandbox=True` AND `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True` propagate. Each successful run writes exactly one `CaioAuditSnapshot` + one `AgentRun` (`agent=AgentRun.Agent.CAIO`, `model="deterministic_v1"`, `provider="disabled"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`, ID base `72000`) + 2 audit rows: `caio.audit.snapshot.created` (per-run snapshot creation; payload carries `phase="11C"`, `severity`, `compliance_risk_call_count`, `agent_data_gaps`, `transcript_backlog_count`, `call_quality_trend`, `anomaly_agent_count`, `weak_learning_count`, `agent_run_id`, `sandbox`) and `caio.audit.daily_run.completed` (per-sweep success with `duration_ms`). **Beat schedule now has 11 daily entries** (10 from Phase 11B + `caio-audit-daily`); confirmed via `build_beat_schedule()` keys list. **3 new audit kinds (each ≤ 64 chars)**: `caio.audit.snapshot.created`, `caio.audit.daily_run.completed`, `caio.audit.daily_run.blocked`. **3 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/caio/`: `snapshots/?limit=N` (paginated list, capped 200), `snapshots/latest/` (most recent — 404 when empty), `snapshots/<int:pk>/` (detail); POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. URL ordering: `snapshots/latest/` is registered BEFORE the dynamic `<int:pk>` route so "latest" is never captured as a snapshot id. Auth: admin / director / owner / superuser only (anonymous → 401/403). Response shape is camelCased (`severity`, `recommendationText`, `agentAnomalyFlags`, `ceoAuditNotes`, `agentDataGapNames`, etc) for direct frontend consumption. **CAIO has NO direct execution power** — NEVER imports `queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `trigger_call_for_lead` / `create_shipment` / Razorpay; NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`; NEVER mutates any Phase 9 snapshot row (read-only over upstream snapshots — CAIO must NEVER add per-order, per-customer, or per-snapshot rows on any Phase 9 table); NEVER modifies any agent's prompts or configurations (no PromptVersion writes); NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest` or executes any `ApprovalMatrix` action.
