Asserted in the defensive safety test by patching all four outbound entrypoints (`apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message`, `apps.whatsapp.services.send_freeform_text_message`, `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead`, `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment`) AND snapshotting row counts on **all 12 protected tables** (`Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` / `Call` / `CallQualityScore` / `CallTranscriptLine` + the 5 Phase 9 snapshot tables `CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` / `CfoFinancialSnapshot` / `DataAnalystSnapshot` / `CallingTeamLeaderSnapshot` / `RtoRiskSnapshot`) and checking every one stays constant across the full audit run — the only new rows are the single `CaioAuditSnapshot` + the single `AgentRun` + the audit events. `recommendation_text` is internal-only Director briefing (like Phase 9F's `briefing_text`) — never customer-facing. **34 new backend tests** in `tests/test_caio_audit_agent.py` cover: `gather_compliance_risk` (no scored calls returns 0; flagged calls counted + grouped by `agent_label`), `gather_transcript_backlog` (delegates to Phase 11A overview with mocked return), `gather_call_quality_trend` (no_data on empty, up when this avg > prior + 5%, down when this avg < prior - 5%, flat in band), `gather_agent_snapshots` (all recent → empty `stale_agents`; one missing → name added to `stale_agents`; one snapshot 60h old → stale), `compute_agent_anomaly_flags` (all clean / CFO `high_pending_payments` + `rto_spike` / Data Analyst funnel < 10% with sufficient volume / CEO critical → `ceo_health_critical` / RTO high+critical cohort → `high_rto_risk_orders`), `compute_severity` (5 cases — red on compliance, red on 3+ data gaps, amber on weak learning, amber on anomalies, green default), `audit_ceo_ai` (None → "data gap"; poor tier → tier in note; agent_status_summary surfaces alert lines), `build_snapshot` (green happy path; red on compliance violation), Celery happy path (persists snapshot + AgentRun + 2 audit rows; AgentRun carries `agent=CAIO`, `model=deterministic_v1`, `provider=disabled`, `dry_run=True`, `cost_usd=0`), kill-switch refusal (blocks + audit row), sandbox propagation (both `snapshot.sandbox=True` AND `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`), API (anonymous → 401/403; admin can read list/latest/detail; 404 when latest empty; 404 when detail id missing; POST/PATCH/DELETE → 405), full defensive integration (**all 12 protected row counts unchanged** + every outbound entrypoint `assert_not_called`), and beat schedule sanity (`caio-audit-daily` registered, total = 11). Phase 9F + 9E + 11A + 11B regression: 160/160 still green after the new beat entry (Phase 11B's beat-count test relaxed from `== 10` to `>= 10` to tolerate future additions, mirroring the same fix made on Phase 11A). On the local dev DB (no Phase 9 snapshots seeded), running `python manage.py shell -c "from apps.caio.tasks import run_caio_audit_agent_daily; import json; print(json.dumps(run_caio_audit_agent_daily(), indent=2, default=str))"` returned `status=completed`, `severity=red` (5 agent data gaps → meets `>= 3` red threshold), `agent_data_gap_names=["ceo_orchestration","cfo","data_analyst","calling_team_leader","rto_prevention"]`, full structured `recommendation_text` including the "Check Celery beat is running on VPS" + "Urgent review required" Director actions, and `duration_ms=139` — confirming the audit correctly identifies the missing Phase 9 layer on a fresh DB. **Phase 11C provides the contract Phase 11D / Phase 12 will read from** — the per-agent anomaly map + weak learning indicators + CEO audit notes are deliberately stable codes (no free-text), so future automation (which still requires Director approval and the existing approval matrix) can subscribe to them via the read-only API without parsing prose. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2570 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (34 new Phase 11C tests).

- **Phase 11B (2026-05-16) — Call Quality Scorer V1 (deterministic, no LLM) SHIPPED.** Second link in the calls observability chain after Phase 11A. Consumes the `CallTranscriptLine` rows Phase 11A persists, scores them on 5 dimensions using deterministic keyword/pattern matching (zero LLM call in V1), and writes one `CallQualityScore` row per Call. **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `CallTranscriptLine.who = models.CharField(max_length=40)` is free-text with no choices — observed in code/tests: `"agent"` / `"assistant"` / `"Customer"` / `"user"` / `"Unknown"`; Phase 2D webhook persistence uses `who or "Unknown"`, Phase 11A REST normalization sets it from Vapi `role` / `who` / `speaker`. `Claim` model exists at `apps/compliance/models.py` (PK=`product`, JSON `approved`/`disallowed`, doctor/compliance/version, 8 seed categories — Weight Management, Blood Purification, Men/Women Wellness, Immunity, Lungs Detox, Body Detox, Joint Care); V1 deliberately uses a hardcoded keyword list per spec — Phase 11C will fold the Claim Vault in. `Call.agent` is `CharField(max_length=80)` (typical value `"Calling AI · Vapi"`). `_parse_duration_seconds` exists at `apps.agents.calling_team_leader.service._parse_duration_seconds` — reused via direct import. `CallQualityScore` did NOT exist anywhere before this phase. Phase 11A's patched `compute_transcript_backlog` recognizes both the legacy `CallTranscriptLine`-linked signal AND the new `transcript_line_count > 0` denormalized field. **Implementation decisions:** new model `apps.calls.CallQualityScore` (one-to-one FK on `Call`, on_delete=CASCADE) + migration `calls.0005_phase11b_call_quality_score`; permissive case-insensitive `who` classifier (agent-side = `{"agent","assistant","bot","system"}` substring match OR exact match against `Call.agent`; customer-side = `{"customer","user","human"}`); reuse `_parse_duration_seconds` rather than duplicate the parser. **New model fields**: `call` OneToOneField, `scored_at`, `scoring_version="deterministic_v1"`, `line_count`, denormalized `agent_label` + `duration_raw`, the 5 dimension integers, `composite_score`, `flags` JSON list, `raw_signals` JSON diagnostic dict (for Phase 11C CAIO Audit Agent consumption), `created_at`, `updated_at`; 3 indexes on `scored_at` / `composite_score` / `agent_label`. **New service module** `apps.calls.quality_scorer` ships pure functions for each dimension: **`connection_score(call) -> (score, duration_seconds)`** — Missed/Failed=0, Queued/Live=30, Completed=60 with duration bonuses (>=30s→70, >=90s→80, >=180s→90, >=300s→100). **`product_knowledge_score(agent_lines) -> (score, found)`** — `min(len(found_keywords) * 12, 100)` against a 24-keyword V1 hardcoded list (`weight`, `wajan`, `metabolism`, `capsule`, `khurak`, `ayurvedic`, `ayurved`, `herbal`, `jadibooti`, `natural`, `prakritic`, `ingredient`, `tarkib`, `dose`, `din mein`, `roz`, `result`, `fayda`, `labh`, `asar`); 0 when no agent lines. **`compliance_score(agent_lines) -> (score, found)`** — `max(100 - len(forbidden) * 25, 0)` against an 18-phrase blocklist (`guarantee` / `guaranteed` / `garanti`, `cure` / `theek kar` / `thik kar` / `ilaaj`, `medicine` / `dawa` / `dawai`, `doctor` / `doctor ne kaha` / `physician`, `clinically proven` / `clinical trial`, `100%` / `100 percent`, `no side effect` / `koi side effect nahi`, `fda` / `drug` — every Master Blueprint §26 hard stop); structurally 100 when there are no agent lines (so `zero_agent_utterances` isn't double-penalized). **`objection_handling_score(ordered) -> (score, found_keywords, total, handled)`** — walks the ordered (who, text) tuple list; a customer line containing any of `OBJECTION_KEYWORDS` (`price`, `paisa`, `mehnga`, `quality`, `time`, `competitor`, etc) is an objection; the immediately-following agent utterance is "handled" when it contains any of `RESPONSE_KEYWORDS` (`samjhiye`, `dekh`, `bilkul`, `result`, `discount`, `money back`, etc); score = `int(handled / total * 100)`; returns 70 (neutral) when no objections fired. **`tonality_score(agent_lines, first_agent_text) -> (score, greeting_found, closing_found, positive_count)`** — base 50 + 20 (greeting in first agent line — `namaste`/`namaskar`/`hello`/`hi `/`jai shri`) + min(positive_count*5, 20) + 10 (closing — `dhanyavad`/`shukriya`/`phir milenge`/etc), clamped [0, 100]. **Main entrypoint** `score_call(call_id, *, dry_run=False) -> dict` is idempotent via OneToOne + `update_or_create`; on success writes one `call_quality.scored` audit row whose payload carries `phase="11B"`, `call_id`, `composite_score`, `flags`, and `agent_label_suffix` (last-20 chars only — never the full `Call.agent` PII); dry-run returns the full score dict but persists nothing; `already_scored` skip path returns the existing composite + flags. **`get_scoring_backlog()`** returns Calls with `transcript_ingested_at IS NOT NULL` AND `transcript_line_count > 0` AND no `CallQualityScore` row yet, ordered oldest-ingested-first. **`score_backlog(*, limit=50, dry_run=False)`** drives the loop and returns `{total, scored, skipped_already, skipped_no_call, errors, dry_run, avg_composite_score, duration_ms, results}`. **`get_scoring_overview(window_days=30)`** is the read-only diagnostic snapshot used by both the inspector CLI and the summary API — returns `total_scored` / `backlog_count` / `avg_composite` / `low_compliance_count` (compliance_score < 60) / `top_flags` (top 5 codes by count) / `avg_by_agent` (sorted by call count desc with `agent_label` / `call_count` / `avg_composite` / `avg_compliance`). **7 flag codes** (deduped): `compliance_violation` (any forbidden phrase landed), `no_greeting` (no greeting found AND agent lines present), `weak_product_knowledge` (score < 40), `no_objection_response` (objections fired AND score < 40), `short_call` (Completed AND duration < 30s), `zero_agent_utterances` (no agent lines), `no_transcript` (zero transcript lines). **2 new CLI commands** in `apps/calls/management/commands/`: `python manage.py score_call_transcripts [--call-id ID] [--limit N=50] [--dry-run] [--json]` (backlog mode default; `--call-id` scores one specific call; `--dry-run` makes no DB changes) and `python manage.py inspect_quality_scoring_backlog [--window-days N=30] [--json]` (read-only inspector — never calls the scorer). **New Celery task** `apps.calls.tasks.score_call_transcripts_daily(limit=100)` scheduled at **23:30 IST** (env-shiftable via `CALL_QUALITY_SCORING_DAILY_HOUR=23` / `_MINUTE=30` / `_LIMIT=100`); runs 30 minutes after the Phase 11A transcript ingest sweep so freshly-ingested transcripts get scored the same evening; refuses with `call_quality.daily_scoring.blocked` audit when (a) Postgres-safe runtime kill switch is off (reuses Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 `-pk` ordering helper imported from the existing `apps.calls.tasks._kill_switch_blocked`) or (b) `apps.ai_governance.sandbox.is_sandbox_enabled()` returns True; successful runs (including zero-scored) write `call_quality.daily_scoring.completed` with the rolling counts stripped of the per-call result list. **Beat schedule now has 10 daily entries** (9 from Phase 11A + `call-quality-scoring-daily`); confirmed via `build_beat_schedule()` keys list. **3 new audit kinds (each ≤ 64 chars)**: `call_quality.scored` (per-call success), `call_quality.daily_scoring.completed` (per-sweep success), `call_quality.daily_scoring.blocked` (per-sweep refusal). **3 new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/calls/`: `quality-scores/?limit=N` (paginated list, capped 200), `quality-scores/<call_id>/` (single score detail with full `raw_signals`), `quality-scores/summary/?window_days=N` (aggregate stats — camelCased for the consumer: `totalScored`, `avgComposite`, `lowComplianceCount`, `topFlags` `[{flagCode, count}]`, `avgByAgent` `[{agentLabel, callCount, avgComposite, avgCompliance}]`; shaped for the Phase 11C CAIO Audit Agent to consume directly). Both routes require admin / director / owner / superuser (anonymous → 401/403); POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. Static URL ordering note: the `quality-scores/summary/` route is registered BEFORE the dynamic `quality-scores/<call_id>/` route in `apps.calls.v1_urls` so "summary" is not captured as a Call.id. **Phase 11B NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER makes a call, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER touches Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi (Vapi was only touched by Phase 11A for transcript fetch; Phase 11B reads only the persisted lines), NEVER mutates `Customer` / `Order` / `Payment` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`** — asserted in the defensive safety test by patching all four outbound entrypoints (`apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message`, `apps.whatsapp.services.send_freeform_text_message`, `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead`, `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment`) and checking `assert_not_called` across every path (dimension scorers / `score_call` / `score_backlog` / Celery task / both CLI commands / all 3 API endpoints); `Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` row counts stay constant before/after a full backlog run. **Phase 11C CAIO Audit Agent (next planned)** will consume `CallQualityScore.raw_signals` + `flags` to write compliance commentary; the summary endpoint and the `raw_signals` dict (carrying `agent_utterance_count` / `customer_utterance_count` / `duration_seconds` / `product_keywords_found` / `forbidden_phrases_found` / `objection_keywords_found` / `objection_total` / `objection_handled` / `positive_keyword_count` / `greeting_found` / `closing_found` / `first_agent_text_excerpt`) are shaped for that consumer. On the VPS the existing 22 seed Calls have empty `provider_call_id`, so Phase 11A's backlog stays `skipped_no_id` and Phase 11B's backlog is empty; the daily sweep will score zero calls until real Vapi-routed calls start landing — the value of Phase 11B is the **infrastructure for future real calls** + the contract Phase 11C will read from. **40 new backend tests** in `tests/test_call_quality_scorer.py` cover: every dimension scorer (10 happy/edge cases), composite formula weights (`80*0.2 + 60*0.25 + 100*0.25 + 50*0.15 + 70*0.15 = 74` verified), all 7 flag emissions, dry-run / already-scored / call-not-found / no-transcript paths, backlog selection (excludes calls with 0 transcript lines AND already-scored calls), `score_backlog` summary counts including pre-scored skip, Celery happy + kill-switch refusal + sandbox refusal, CLI dry-run + single-call + inspector pretty print, API anonymous blocked + admin list / detail / summary + 404 on missing + 405 on POST/PATCH/DELETE, full defensive integration (`Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` row counts unchanged), and beat schedule sanity (`call-quality-scoring-daily` registered, total = 10). Phase 9E + 11A regression: 70/70 still green after the Phase 11B model addition. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2536 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (40 new Phase 11B tests).

- **Phase 11A (2026-05-16) — Transcript Ingestion Pipeline V1 SHIPPED.** Active-pull Vapi REST transcript backlog clearer, motivated by the Phase 9E Calling Team Leader's first VPS run that reported `transcript_backlog_count=22` and fired the `high_transcript_backlog` alert. Phase 2D persists transcript lines only via webhook (`apps.calls.services._handle_transcript_update` / `_handle_transcript_final`); for legacy calls or any Vapi webhook outage the lines never landed locally. Phase 11A adds a separate read-only ingestion service so a daily Celery sweep + on-demand CLI can fill the gap. **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `CallTranscriptLine` already exists at [apps/calls/models.py:109-135](backend/apps/calls/models.py#L109-L135) with `active_call`/`call` FKs + `order` PositiveIntegerField + `who` CharField(40) + `text` TextField (no `sequence` / `speaker` / `start_seconds` / `end_seconds` / `confidence` / `created_at` / `updated_at` / unique-together). `Call.provider_call_id` is `CharField(max_length=64, blank=True, default="", db_index=True)`, populated by `services.trigger_call_for_lead` — this IS the Vapi call id, no new `vapi_call_id` field needed. `Call` has no `transcript_ingested_at` / `transcript_line_count` / `duration_seconds` / `outcome` fields. `Call.duration` is a `CharField(max_length=16, default="0:00")` storing "m:ss" strings. `Call.status ∈ {Live, Queued, Completed, Missed, Failed}`. All 8 `VAPI_*` env vars exist in `settings.py` (lines 617-624) — `VAPI_MODE`, `VAPI_API_BASE_URL`, `VAPI_API_KEY`, `VAPI_ASSISTANT_ID`, `VAPI_PHONE_NUMBER_ID`, `VAPI_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `VAPI_DEFAULT_LANGUAGE`, `VAPI_CALLBACK_URL`. No pre-existing active-pull transcript fetch helper — `vapi_client.py` only exposes `trigger_call` + `verify_webhook_signature`. **Implementation decisions** based on discovery: reuse the existing `CallTranscriptLine` model unchanged (no new transcript schema); reuse `Call.provider_call_id` as the Vapi lookup id (no new field); add only two Call denormalization fields. **New model fields** (migration `calls.0004_phase11a_transcript_fields`): `Call.transcript_ingested_at = DateTimeField(null=True, blank=True)`, `Call.transcript_line_count = IntegerField(default=0)`. **New service module** `apps.calls.transcript_ingestion` ships `fetch_vapi_transcript(vapi_call_id) -> list[dict] | None` (lazy-imports `requests` inside the function so tests patch `requests.get` cleanly; GETs `/{VAPI_API_BASE_URL}/call/{id}` with `Bearer VAPI_API_KEY`; never raises — returns `None` on missing key / empty id / 404 / 4xx / 5xx / network error / non-JSON body / non-dict body / missing utterances; normalizes multiple Vapi response shapes — `messages` / `artifact.messages` / `transcript` / `lines` / legacy string transcript), `ingest_call_transcript(call_id, *, dry_run=False)` (returns `{"ok": True/False, "skipped": True/False, "reason": str, "call_id": str, "line_count": int, "duration_ms": int, "dry_run": bool}`; skips on `call_not_found` / `no_vapi_id` / `already_ingested` / `no_transcript_from_vapi`; on success deletes any pre-existing webhook-persisted lines for that call then `bulk_create`s the fresh REST shape inside `transaction.atomic()`, sets `transcript_ingested_at=now()` + `transcript_line_count=N`, writes one `transcript.ingested` audit row whose payload carries the `call_id` + `line_count` + **`vapi_call_id_last4` only** — never the full provider call id; persistence error inside the atomic block returns `reason="ingest_error"` with full rollback — asserted in tests via `bulk_create` raising `RuntimeError` and the Call's denormalized fields staying NULL/0), `get_transcript_backlog(window_days=30)` (filters by `created_at` in rolling window AND `created_at < now() - 24h` grace AND `transcript_ingested_at IS NULL OR transcript_line_count = 0` AND non-empty `provider_call_id`; ordered newest-first), `ingest_backlog(*, limit=50, dry_run=False, sandbox=False)` (drives the loop, counts outcomes separately so operational issues like "lots of `no_transcript_from_vapi` skips" are visible in the daily summary, returns `{total, ingested, skipped_no_id, skipped_already_done, skipped_no_transcript, errors, sandbox, dry_run, duration_ms, results}`; sandbox short-circuits before any Vapi network call), and `get_backlog_overview(window_days=30)` (read-only diagnostic snapshot — `total_calls_in_window` / `ingested_count` / `backlog_count` / `backlog_ratio` / `oldest_backlog_at` / `newest_backlog_at` / `top_backlog` with `provider_call_id_last4` masking). **New CLI commands**: `python manage.py ingest_call_transcripts [--call-id ID] [--limit N=50] [--dry-run] [--json]` (default backlog mode; `--call-id` ingests one specific call; `--dry-run` fetches transcripts but persists no rows); `python manage.py inspect_transcript_backlog [--window-days N=30] [--json]` (strictly read-only — never calls Vapi). **New Celery task** `apps.calls.tasks.ingest_transcript_backlog_daily(limit=100)` runs at **23:00 IST** (env-shiftable via new settings `TRANSCRIPT_INGESTION_DAILY_HOUR=23` / `_MINUTE=0` / `_LIMIT=100`); refuses with `transcript.daily_ingest.blocked` audit when (a) Postgres-safe runtime kill switch is off (reuses Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 `-pk` ordering pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` row always wins), (b) `apps.ai_governance.sandbox.is_sandbox_enabled()` returns True, or (c) `settings.VAPI_API_KEY` is empty — successful runs (including zero-ingested) write `transcript.daily_ingest.completed` with the rolling counts stripped of the per-call result list. **Beat schedule now has 9 daily entries** (8 from Phase 9F + `transcript-ingestion-daily`); confirmed via `build_beat_schedule()` keys list. **New audit kinds (3)**: `transcript.ingested` (per-call success; ≤ 64 chars), `transcript.daily_ingest.completed` (per-sweep success), `transcript.daily_ingest.blocked` (per-sweep refusal). All payloads carry `phase="11A"`; the full `provider_call_id` is NEVER persisted in any payload (only `vapi_call_id_last4` is stored). **Two new admin-only read-only DRF endpoints** under `/api/v1/calls/`: `transcript-backlog/?window_days=N` (returns the full `get_backlog_overview` shape with datetime fields ISO-stringified for the JSON renderer and `top_backlog` rows masked to `callId` + `createdAt` + `providerCallIdLast4`) and `transcripts/<call_id>/` (returns `callId` + `providerCallIdLast4` + `transcriptIngestedAt` + `transcriptLineCount` + `lines` array; 404 when call not found). Both routes require admin / director / owner / superuser (anonymous → 401/403); POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. **Phase 9E `compute_transcript_backlog` patch**: now considers BOTH the legacy "no linked `CallTranscriptLine`" signal AND the new `transcript_line_count > 0` denormalized signal — so historical pre-Phase-11A rows (whose denormalized fields are NULL/0 but transcript lines may already exist from the Phase 2D webhook path) stay correctly classified as "transcribed", and post-Phase-11A rows are detected cheaply via the denormalized column. Phase 9E + 9F regression tests stay green (57/57). **Phase 11A NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER makes a call, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER touches Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery, NEVER mutates `Customer` / `Order` / `Payment` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog`** — asserted in the defensive safety test by patching all four outbound entrypoints (`apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message`, `apps.whatsapp.services.send_freeform_text_message`, `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead`, `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment`) and checking `assert_not_called` across `fetch_vapi_transcript`, `ingest_call_transcript`, `ingest_backlog`, the Celery task, both CLI commands, and the two API endpoints; `Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` row counts stay constant before/after a full backlog run. Calls without a `provider_call_id` (the 22 seed/test calls on the VPS that never went through `services.trigger_call_for_lead`) are skipped gracefully with `reason="no_vapi_id"`; the value of Phase 11A is the **infrastructure for future real Vapi-routed calls**, not retroactive backlog clearing of seed data. No frontend changes in V1 — the Phase 9E `/saas-admin` Calling Team Leader card already renders `transcript_backlog_count` from its snapshot, and that snapshot will reflect the correct count automatically once real transcripts start being ingested. **30 new backend tests** in `tests/test_transcript_ingestion.py` cover: `fetch_vapi_transcript` happy path / 404 / network error / missing key / empty id / legacy string shape; `ingest_call_transcript` happy path with `transcript.ingested` audit + last-4 only payload / `no_vapi_id` / `already_ingested` / `no_transcript_from_vapi` / `dry_run=True` (no rows, no audit) / DB error rolls back (atomic) / `call_not_found`; `get_transcript_backlog` excludes calls < 24h / already-ingested / missing provider id / outside window; `ingest_backlog` counts every outcome separately; `ingest_backlog(sandbox=True)` short-circuits without any Vapi call; Celery task happy path / kill-switch refusal / sandbox refusal / `VAPI_API_KEY` missing refusal; CLI `--dry-run` makes no DB changes; CLI single-call mode; CLI inspector prints expected fields; API anonymous blocked / admin can read backlog / admin can read transcript detail / 404 on missing call / 405 on POST/PATCH/DELETE; full defensive integration (`Customer` / `Lead` / `Order` / `Payment` row counts unchanged across a full backlog run, every outbound entrypoint `assert_not_called`); beat schedule sanity (`transcript-ingestion-daily` registered, total = 9). Verification baseline after the new tests is **2496 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 10B Hotfix-2 (2026-05-16) — `nrg_payment_reminder` template variable schema fix SHIPPED.** Root cause from a Director live test attempt: `apps.diagnostics.payment_reminder_service.build_payment_reminder_attempt` set `template_params = {"customer_name", "amount", "payment_url"}`, but the live Meta-approved `nrg_payment_reminder` template body is `{{1}} {{2}}` with `variables_schema.order = ["customer_name", "context"]` (per `apps.whatsapp.template_registry._seed_default_templates._seed_default_templates`); the renderer therefore bound the `{{2}}` slot to the wrong key and the missing `context` variable triggered Meta error #132001 (Template name does not exist in translation). Fix: `template_params = {"customer_name": ..., "context": "ji, aapka ₹<amount> ka payment pending hai. Isi link se pay karein: <payment_url>"}` — `context` is a single Hindi rendered string that collapses both the amount and the payment URL into the one positional variable the template actually consumes. The full rendered message becomes `<customer_name> ji, aapka ₹<amount> ka payment pending hai. Isi link se pay karein: <payment_url>`. Phase 10B Hotfix-2 NEVER sends, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi, NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog` — pure variable-shape correction on the Phase 7E-Live-B gate row that the existing Director approve + execute path consumes (full structured UTC window + `PHASE7E_LIVE_B_REAL_CUSTOMER_SEND_ENABLED=true` runtime env prefix + `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true` + explicit confirmation flag — none of which Phase 10B Hotfix-2 bypasses). One test assertion in `tests/test_phase10b_payment_reminder_preparer.py` (`test_happy_path_confirmed_stage_creates_attempt_no_outbound`) updated to check the new `{customer_name, context}` shape with substring asserts on `"3000"` / `"https://rzp.io/test/p10b"` / `"payment pending"`; all other Phase 10B tests stay green unchanged. No new models, no migrations, no `.env.production` edits, no outbound sends. Verification baseline is **2466 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (no new tests; one updated assertion).

- **Phase 10C Hotfix-1 (2026-05-16) - Razorpay webhook reconciliation for refreshed links SHIPPED.** Root cause from the Director Rs1 live test: Phase 10C execute stored the fresh Razorpay `plink_*` only on `Phase10CPaymentLinkRefreshGate.razorpay_link_id` and updated `Payment.payment_url`, but left `Payment.gateway_reference_id` blank. The production Razorpay webhook resolver matches `payment_link.paid` by `Payment.gateway_reference_id`, so the webhook rows were accepted (`payment_link.paid` / `payment.captured`) but the business `Payment` / `Order` stayed Pending until manual reconciliation. Hotfix-1 now persists `Payment.gateway_reference_id=result.plink_id` and stores Phase 10C link metadata in `Payment.raw_response["phase10c_payment_link_refresh"]` during execute; webhook resolution also falls back to an executed Phase 10C gate by `razorpay_link_id` and self-fills a missing `gateway_reference_id` before marking the payment/order paid. No models, no migrations, no `.env.production` edits, no outbound sends. 2 new backend regression tests; verification baseline is **2466 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-2 (2026-05-16) - scoped prior-executed duplicate guard SHIPPED.** The real-customer WhatsApp gate no longer treats any previously executed gate as a global permanent blocker. Approval/execute now refuse only when an executed gate already exists for the same normalized target phone + template + execution context (`payment_reminder` context = `payment_id` when present, otherwise `payment_url`; other templates use stable sorted `template_params`). This keeps exact repeat sends blocked with `phase7e_live_b_duplicate_executed_gate_exists` while allowing a new separately Director-approved gate for a different customer such as Kavita after Deepa. No model, no migration, no frontend execute surface, no `.env.production` edit, and all existing env flag / kill switch / structured UTC window / explicit confirmation / CRM customer checks remain mandatory. 3 new backend regression tests; verification baseline is **2465 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 10C — Razorpay Payment Link Refresh Gate SHIPPED (heavyweight CLI workflow; test default; live gated; never sends WhatsApp / calls / shipments; only mutates `Payment.payment_url`).** Built to complete the Director payment-recovery playbook (Phase 10A drilldown → Phase 10A Hotfix-1 phone fallback → Phase 10B reminder preparer → **Phase 10C link refresh**): when a pending-payment link is stale or expired, Phase 10C refreshes it; the refreshed URL is then delivered to the customer ONLY via the separate Phase 7E-Live-B `payment_reminder` send (which Phase 10B prepares the gate row for). **Discovery findings** (read from source before coding): `apps/payments/integrations/razorpay_client.py` exists with `create_payment_link(*, order_id, amount, customer_name, customer_phone="", customer_email="")` returning a frozen `PaymentLinkResult(plink_id, short_url, status, raw)`. Three modes via `settings.RAZORPAY_MODE` (`mock` / `test` / `live`); mock mode returns deterministic `https://razorpay.example/pay/<plink_id>` (no network). Real-SDK path uses `razorpay.Client(auth=(RAZORPAY_KEY_ID, RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET))` + `client.payment_link.create(payload)`. Single key pair in settings (no separate test/live env keys — the `RAZORPAY_MODE` dispatch is the test/live discriminator at the integration level; Phase 10C adds runtime `RAZORPAY_MODE` mismatch-safety on top). No pre-existing `cancel_payment_link` helper — Phase 10C adds it. Webhook signature helper `verify_webhook_signature` exists for inbound webhook auth (unrelated to this phase). Two new helpers added to the same file: `create_payment_link_for_refresh(*, payment_id, order_id, amount, customer_name, customer_phone="", customer_email="", operator_name="")` — same dispatch shape as `create_payment_link` but FORCES `notify.sms=False` AND `notify.email=False` AND `reminder_enable=False` so Razorpay NEVER auto-notifies the customer (Phase 7E-Live-B owns delivery); notes carry `phase="10c"` + `payment_id` + `order_id` + `refreshed_by=operator_name` for audit trace; returns `PaymentLinkResult`. `cancel_payment_link(*, plink_id)` — never raises; returns a dict with `status ∈ {cancelled, mocked, rejected, error}` and `raw` (provider response or error message). Phase 10C records the cancel result honestly so rollback proceeds even if Razorpay refuses (e.g. already-paid link). New `payments.Phase10CPaymentLinkRefreshGate` model + migration `payments.0026_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate`: FK `payment → Payment` (`on_delete=PROTECT`); `mode` (test/live); `status` (draft/approved/executed/rolled_back/cancelled/failed); archives `previous_payment_url`; persists `razorpay_link_id` + `razorpay_short_url`; carries `recorded_signoff_window_start_utc` / `_end_utc` / `_valid` for live mode UTC window evidence; `force_replace` bool; `sandbox` bool propagated from `is_sandbox_enabled()`; `metadata` JSONField for archived previous URL + operator note; lifecycle timestamps `prepared_at` / `approved_at` / `executed_at` / `rolled_back_at` / `cancelled_at` / `created_at` / `updated_at`. New service module `apps.payments.phase10c_payment_link_refresh` exports `prepare_gate / approve_gate / execute_gate / rollback_gate / cancel_gate / inspect_gate` and `Phase10CGateResult` dataclass. Stage matrices match Phase 10B: **ALLOWED**={`Confirmed`, `Order Punched`, `Interested`, `Confirmation Pending`} (proceed); **BLOCKED**={`RTO`, `Out for Delivery`, `Cancelled`, `Delivered`, `Dispatched`, `Payment Link Sent`, `New Lead`, `internal_sandbox`} (refuse). Payment validation: status ∈ {Pending, Partial}, `amount > 0`. Phone validation: resolved phone (via Phase 10A Hotfix-1 fallback chain) must be non-empty AND not `"0000000000"`. **Idempotency**: a non-empty `Payment.payment_url` refuses unless `--force-replace` is passed; the old URL is archived to `gate.previous_payment_url` AND `gate.metadata.archived_previous_url`. **Test mode** (`--mode test`, default) calls `create_payment_link_for_refresh` with `RAZORPAY_MODE` resolved at runtime; mock mode returns deterministic URLs; test mode calls the real Razorpay TEST API. Test mode REFUSES if runtime `RAZORPAY_MODE=live` (mismatch safety: never accidentally mutate a live link from a test-mode gate). **Live mode** (`--mode live`) validates ALL of: `PHASE10C_PAYMENT_LINK_REFRESH_ENABLED=true` runtime env (defaults locked off in `settings.py`; `.env.production` is NEVER edited), `--confirm-phase10c-payment-link-refresh-live` CLI flag, Postgres-safe kill switch enabled (reuses Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 `-pk` ordering helper — `RuntimeKillSwitch.objects.filter(scope="global", enabled=False).order_by("-pk").first()` wins over the seeded enabled default), structured `BEGIN_UTC=`/`END_UTC=` markers in the Director sign-off (parsed by `apps.saas.utc_window.parse_director_signoff_window`), window length ≤ 15 minutes (`validate_within_director_window` alias `validate_execution_window`, default `max_window_seconds=900`), and runtime `RAZORPAY_MODE=live`. **Rollback** restores `Payment.payment_url` from `gate.previous_payment_url` AND calls `cancel_payment_link(plink_id=gate.razorpay_link_id)`; if Razorpay refuses (already-paid link, expired link, network error) the cancel result is recorded in `gate.metadata.cancel_result` but the `Payment.payment_url` restore proceeds anyway and the gate flips to `rolled_back` (or `cancelled` if cancel-only). Six new CLI commands: `prepare_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate <payment_id> --mode {test|live} [--force-replace] --operator-name NAME [--operator-note TEXT] [--json]`, `approve_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate --gate-id ID --operator-name NAME --intent TEXT [--director-signoff "BEGIN_UTC=... END_UTC=..."] [--json]`, `execute_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate --gate-id ID --operator-name NAME [--confirm-phase10c-payment-link-refresh-live] [--json]`, `rollback_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate --gate-id ID --operator-name NAME [--json]`, `cancel_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate --gate-id ID --operator-name NAME --reason TEXT [--json]`, `inspect_phase10c_payment_link_refresh_gate <gate_id> [--json]`. 9 audit kinds: `phase10c.gate.prepared`, `phase10c.gate.approved`, `phase10c.gate.execute.requested`, `phase10c.gate.execute.success`, `phase10c.gate.execute.failed`, `phase10c.gate.rollback.success`, `phase10c.gate.rollback.failed`, `phase10c.gate.cancelled`, `phase10c.live_mode.refused`. **Phase 10C NEVER sends WhatsApp, NEVER makes a call, NEVER dispatches a shipment, NEVER touches Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi, NEVER mutates any business state other than `Payment.payment_url`** (asserted by defensive safety tests patching `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message`, `apps.whatsapp.services.send_freeform_text_message`, `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead`, `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` and checking `assert_not_called` across the full prepare → approve → execute → rollback lifecycle; `Order` / `Customer` / `WhatsAppMessage` / `Call` / `Shipment` row counts stay constant; only `Payment.payment_url` mutates and `Phase10CPaymentLinkRefreshGate` rows are created). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` → `gate.sandbox=True`. 38 new backend tests covering: prepare happy-path (test mode), prepare refuses non-existent payment, prepare refuses internal-sandbox order, prepare refuses BLOCKED stages, prepare proceeds on ALLOWED stages, prepare refuses non-empty payment_url without `--force-replace`, prepare archives previous URL with `--force-replace`, approve requires draft status, approve test mode (no UTC window required), approve live mode requires `BEGIN_UTC/END_UTC`, approve live mode requires window ≤ 15 min, execute test mode happy path (mocked `create_payment_link_for_refresh`), execute test mode refuses if runtime `RAZORPAY_MODE=live`, execute live mode requires env flag, execute live mode requires `--confirm-...-live`, execute live mode requires kill switch enabled, execute live mode requires structured UTC window, execute live mode requires runtime `RAZORPAY_MODE=live`, execute populates `razorpay_link_id` + `short_url`, execute updates `Payment.payment_url`, rollback restores previous URL, rollback calls `cancel_payment_link`, rollback proceeds even if Razorpay refuses, cancel before execute clears state, sandbox propagation, idempotency on re-execute, full lifecycle no-outbound defensive assertion, Postgres-safe kill switch second-row test, mock mode integration. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2458 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** (38 new Phase 10C tests; no frontend changes in V1 — CLI-only for Director SSH workflow).

- **Phase 10B — Targeted Payment Reminder Preparer SHIPPED (stage-aware CLI wrapper around Phase 7E-Live-B; never sends; controlled-gate-row-only).** **Phase 10B Hotfix-1 (2026-05-16)** now idempotently auto-creates a missing `crm.Customer` for `phone_source=order` / `phone_source=payment` before creating the draft send gate, writes `phase10b.crm_customer.auto_created` when new, skips `phone_source=customer`, and treats create failures as non-fatal warnings so gate prep still succeeds. Builds on Phase 10A drilldown + Hotfix-1 phone fallback. The Phase 9F CEO Orchestration's first VPS run surfaced 9 pending payments; Phase 10A surfaced phones via fallback; Phase 10B is the next step in the Director's payment-recovery workflow — it validates eligibility and creates a Phase 7E-Live-B `Phase7ELiveBRealCustomerSendGate` row pre-filled with payment-reminder template params, but **does not send**. The existing Phase 7E-Live-B Director directive (intent + UTC window + operator + flags) remains the only path to a live send; Phase 10B does NOT bypass any guard. New service `apps.diagnostics.payment_reminder_service.build_payment_reminder_attempt(*, payment_id, template_id="payment_reminder", force=False, operator_note="", operator_name="phase10b_preparer")` (pure validation + Phase 7E-Live-B `prepare_gate` call; raises `PaymentReminderValidationError(code, message)` on refusal; returns a `PreparedReminder` dataclass on success). New CLI `python manage.py prepare_payment_reminder_send <payment_id> [--template-id NAME] [--force] [--operator-note TEXT] [--operator-name NAME] [--json]` — prints the gate id + the exact next Director commands on success; exits 1 with a clear error code on refusal. Stage-aware validation table: **ALLOWED** = `{"Confirmed", "Order Punched"}` (proceed without warning); **WARN** = `{"Interested", "Confirmation Pending"}` (only proceed with `--force`; writes a `phase10b.payment_reminder.warn_forced` AuditEvent); **BLOCKED** = `{"RTO", "Out for Delivery", "Cancelled", "Delivered", "Dispatched", "Payment Link Sent", "New Lead", "internal_sandbox"}` (refuse with exit 1; **no gate row created**). Payment validation: `status ∈ {Pending, Partial}`, `amount > 0`, non-empty `payment_url`. Phone validation: resolved phone (via Phase 10A Hotfix-1 fallback chain — Payment.customer_phone → Order.phone → Customer.phone via name match) must be non-empty AND not the internal-sandbox placeholder `"0000000000"`. Customer name resolved from `Order.customer_name → Payment.customer → Customer.name`. **Phase 7E-Live-B discovery findings** (read from `apps/whatsapp/phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send.py` before coding): gate model `Phase7ELiveBRealCustomerSendGate`; module `apps.whatsapp.phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send`; CLI commands `inspect_/prepare_/approve_/execute_/cancel_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate`; service entrypoint `prepare_gate(*, target_phone, target_customer_name, template_name, template_params, operator_name) -> dict` returning `{ok, gateId, status, templateName, targetMasked, blockers, nextAction}`. **`payment_reminder` IS in the `APPROVED_TEMPLATE_NAMES` allowlist** at line 33–40 alongside `confirmation_reminder` / `delivery_reminder` / `rto_rescue` / `reorder_reminder` / `usage_explanation` — so Phase 10B uses it as the default template name. Whether Meta has actually approved the corresponding template body in the live WABA is a separate operational question; the Phase 7E-Live-B execute path will check that at send time, so Phase 10B does not need to flag it. Template params built deterministically: `{"customer_name": ..., "amount": str(payment.amount), "payment_url": payment.payment_url}`. On success the CLI prints the Phase 7E-Live-B gate id, the resolved phone (last-4 masked only), the customer name, and the EXACT `approve_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate` + `execute_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send` commands the Director should run with structured `BEGIN_UTC=...` / `END_UTC=...` placeholders. Audit kinds: `phase10b.payment_reminder.prepared` (always written on successful prepare) + `phase10b.payment_reminder.warn_forced` (only when `--force` used at a WARN stage) + `phase10b.crm_customer.auto_created` (only when the bridge row is created). Phase 10B itself NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; the only side-effects are the optional CRM Customer bridge row and one Phase 7E-Live-B gate row in `draft` status (asserted by the defensive safety test: all four outbound entrypoints patched and `assert_not_called`; `Payment` / `Order` rows stay unchanged; the new gate's `status=draft`, `executed_at=None`, `customer_notification_sent=False`). 34 backend tests; verification baseline after Hotfix-1 is **2462 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**. NO new model, NO new migration, NO frontend changes in V1 — pure CLI for Director SSH workflow (frontend integration is a later phase).

- **Phase 10A — Pending Payments Drilldown (Diagnostics V1) SHIPPED (read-only Director review surface; no actions; no mutations).** First module under the new `apps/diagnostics/` Django app — no models, no migrations. New `apps.diagnostics.service.list_pending_payments_drilldown(include_partial: bool = True, limit: int | None = 100, state_filter: str | None = None)` pure function reads `Payment` rows with status `Pending` (and optionally `Partial`), joins with `Order` + `crm.Customer` + last-outbound `WhatsAppMessage` + last `Call`, and returns a dict per row with `payment_id`, `payment_status`, `amount` (integer rupees), `payment_link_url`, `gateway_reference_id`, `created_at`, `days_since_creation`, `order_id`, `order_state` (geographic), `order_status` (lifecycle stage), `customer_name`, `customer_phone`, `last_whatsapp_at`, `last_call_at`, `last_call_outcome`. **Discovered field names** (read from source before coding — not invented): `Payment` exposes `id` / `order_id` / `customer` (name string) / `customer_phone` / `amount` / `status` / `payment_url` / `gateway_reference_id` / `created_at`; `Order` exposes `id` / `customer_name` / `phone` / `state` (geographic) / `stage` (lifecycle) / `payment_status` / `amount` / `created_at`; `Customer` (in `apps/crm`) exposes `id` / `name` / `phone`; `WhatsAppMessage` exposes `customer` FK + `direction` (`inbound`/`outbound`/`system`) + `created_at`; `Call` exposes `phone` + `status` (Live/Queued/Completed/Missed/Failed) + `created_at` — **no dedicated `outcome` column**, so `Call.status` is surfaced as `last_call_outcome` (clearest action-relevant signal). Rows sorted by `created_at` ASC so the oldest pending records appear first. Efficient query shape: one `Payment` filter query, then bulk `Order.filter(id__in=...)` + `Customer.filter(phone__in=...)`, then per-phone `WhatsAppMessage` + `Call` lookups; avoids N+1 across the typical 10–50 pending-payment cohort. New `GET /api/v1/diagnostics/pending-payments/?include_partial=true|false&limit=N&state=Delhi` DRF endpoint with `_AdminDiagnosticsPermission` (admin/director/owner/superuser only); `http_method_names = ["get", "head", "options"]` so POST/PATCH/PUT/DELETE return 405; query params: `include_partial` (default true), `limit` (default 100, max 500), `state` (optional case-insensitive Order.state filter); response shape `{count, filters, results}`. New CLI command `python manage.py inspect_pending_payments [--include-partial/--no-include-partial] [--limit N] [--state STATE] [--json]` pretty-prints a table to stdout (also supports `--json` for machine-readable output) — useful for SSH / cron debugging without going through the API surface. New `/operations/pending-payments` frontend page (route added to `App.tsx`) renders the sortable table with columns Order / Customer / Phone / Amount / Status / State / Days Pending / Last WhatsApp / Last Call / Last Call Outcome, a client-side search box (filters by customer name / phone / order / state), an "Include Partial" toggle (default on), loading + error + empty states, and a permanent "Read-only diagnostic" banner at the bottom; **no Send Reminder / Mark Paid / Refund / Cancel / Trigger Call buttons exist on the page** (asserted in the safety test). The CFO `/saas-admin` card now carries a non-actionable "View pending payments →" link routing to the new page (only appended; the CFO card structure is otherwise untouched). Phase 10A NEVER mutates state, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi, NEVER sends WhatsApp or queues calls — asserted in the defensive safety test by patching `queue_template_message`, `send_freeform_text_message`, `trigger_call_for_lead`, and `create_shipment`, then hitting BOTH the API endpoint AND the CLI command and asserting `Payment` / `Order` / `Customer` / `Call` / `WhatsAppMessage` row counts stay constant. **Action on pending payments still requires the existing approval-gated Phase 7E-Live-B Director directive** — Phase 10A only surfaces the records for review. The Phase 9F CEO Orchestration's first real VPS run flagged `high_pending_payments` as the #1 Director priority (9 pending payments totaling ₹20,440; CFO snapshot: paid_count=8 / pending_count=9 / paid_amount=₹20,640 / pending_amount=₹20,440) — Phase 10A is the diagnostic surface answering that priority without crossing the action boundary. 17 new backend tests; verification baseline after the new tests is **2382 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 9F — CEO AI Orchestration V1 SHIPPED (synthesis layer; daily deterministic Celery snapshot rolling up Phase 9A–9E; never triggers outbound action; Tier-2 agent layer complete).** Agent enum check: `Agent.CEO = "ceo"` already exists from Phase 3A — no `ai_governance` migration needed. New `agents.CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0007_ceo_orchestration_snapshot`. Service helper `apps.agents.ceo_orchestration.service` ships pure functions `fetch_latest_snapshots` / `compute_health_score` / `compute_health_tier` / `roll_up_alerts` / `compute_top_3_priorities` / `compute_agent_status_summary` / `generate_briefing_text` / `build_snapshot`. Celery task `apps.agents.ceo_orchestration.tasks.run_ceo_orchestration_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 13:00 IST (env-shiftable; runs after Customer Success 08:00, RTO Prevention 09:00, CFO 10:00, Data Analyst 11:00, Calling Team Leader 12:00). Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/ceo-orchestration/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 30/page, admin+ only), `snapshots/latest/`, `snapshots/<id>/`. Read-only `/saas-admin` card placed at the **top** of the agent stack (since it's the synthesis): business health score badge, health tier, agent status summary table (5 rows), top-3 priorities ordered list, cross-cutting alerts severity-tagged, scrollable briefing text panel; no "Approve Priority" / "Trigger Workflow" / "Send Briefing" / "Run Agent" / "Apply Recommendation" buttons. **Phase 9F does NOT touch the legacy `ai_governance.CeoBriefing` model or its `ai-daily-briefing-morning` / `ai-daily-briefing-evening` scheduled tasks** — it adds a new model + new beat entry alongside, deliberately decoupled. Health score: base 70 + CFO factor (revenue_drop_24h −15 / high_pending_payments −10 / low_order_volume −10 / rto_spike −10 / all_clear +5; missing CFO −5) + RTO factor (−min(critical*3,20) − min(high*1,10); missing −5) + Customer Success factor (−min(at_risk_count,10) + min(reorder_count//5,5); missing −5) + Data Analyst factor (−min(active_alerts*10,30); missing −5) + Calling Team Leader factor (−min(active_alerts*5,20) — excluding `all_clear` + `no_agent_attribution_field`; missing −5), clamped to [0, 100]. Health tier: 0–19 critical / 20–39 poor / 40–59 fair / 60–79 good / 80–100 excellent. Cross-cutting alerts: union of every agent's alerts excluding `all_clear`, with a normalized severity (critical / high / medium / low), source_agent, and rationale; for each agent missing from the last 24h a `data_gap` alert (severity=high, source_agent=<missing_agent>) is added; final list is severity-sorted; the snapshot's flat `alerts` list also carries `data_gap` when any agent is missing AND `all_clear` when no actionable cross-cutting alert fired. Top-3 priorities: first 3 actionable alerts (excluding `all_clear` and `no_agent_attribution_field`), each with deterministic `recommended_action` string (internal-only, NEVER customer-facing — e.g. `revenue_drop_24h` → "Review last 24h revenue drop; check campaign performance and dispatch flow."); fewer than 3 actionable alerts returns only what's available; zero actionable alerts returns `[{"priority": "1", "issue": "all_clear", "source_agent": "none", "recommended_action": "Continue monitoring."}]`. Agent status summary: per-agent `{status: "ok"|"alert"|"missing", summary: <one-line factual>}` covering all five Phase 9A–9E agents. Briefing text: multi-line factual paragraph with health score + tier, per-agent status, top priorities, cross-cutting alert count — internal-only, never customer-facing. The five FKs (`customer_success_snapshot`, `rto_snapshot`, `cfo_snapshot`, `data_analyst_snapshot`, `calling_team_leader_snapshot`) are all nullable with `on_delete=SET_NULL` for missing-snapshot tolerance. Every run writes one `AgentRun` (`agent="ceo"`, model `"deterministic_v1"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`) + an `AuditEvent` of kind `ceo_orchestration.snapshot.created`; the task closes with `ceo_orchestration.daily_run.completed`. Kill switch reuses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `ceo_orchestration.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `CustomerSuccessSnapshot` / `RtoRiskSnapshot` / `Call` / `Order` row counts stay constant (Phase 9F is strictly read-only over upstream snapshots — it must not add per-customer or per-order rows). New tests: 33 backend cases covering empty + populated `fetch_latest_snapshots`, score with all missing / healthy / CFO-critical / saturated-alerts (clamps at 0) / cap (≤ 100), tier boundaries (0/19/20/39/40/59/60/79/80/100), alert rollup (empty when all_clear, union + severity sort, `data_gap` injection for missing agent), top-3 selection (happy path / fewer-than-3 / `all_clear` empty path / informational filter), agent status summary (missing vs alert vs ok across the 5 agents), briefing text key sections, happy-path Celery sweep (creates one snapshot + AgentRun + AuditEvent), data-gap path (3 missing agents emit 3 `data_gap` cross-cutting alert entries), kill-switch refusal, sandbox propagation, no-side-effects guard, and build_snapshot all-missing path. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2365 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**. **Tier-2 AI agent layer (Phase 9A through Phase 9F) is now complete.**

- **Phase 9E — Calling Team Leader Agent V1 SHIPPED (call-performance lens; recommendations-only; daily deterministic Celery snapshot; never triggers outbound action).** Call model field check on `apps/calls/models.py` confirmed `Call.agent = CharField(max_length=80)` at line 42 — **per-agent metrics ARE supported by V1**. New `agents.CallingTeamLeaderSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0006_calling_team_leader_snapshot`. New `AgentRun.Agent.CALLING_TEAM_LEADER` enum value + migration `ai_governance.0010_phase9e_add_calling_team_leader_agent`. Service helper `apps.agents.calling_team_leader.service` ships pure functions `compute_call_counts` / `compute_connection_stats_30d` / `compute_avg_duration_30d` / `compute_outcome_breakdown_30d` / `compute_agent_breakdown_30d` / `compute_transcript_backlog` / `detect_anomalies` / `compute_signals` / `build_snapshot`, plus a `_parse_duration_seconds("m:ss"|"mm:ss"|"h:mm:ss")` helper that returns 0 on any garbage input (so a single bad row cannot poison the daily aggregation). Celery task `apps.agents.calling_team_leader.tasks.run_calling_team_leader_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 12:00 IST (env-shiftable; runs after the 08:00 IST Customer Success, 09:00 IST RTO Prevention, 10:00 IST CFO, and 11:00 IST Data Analyst sweeps). Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/calling-team-leader/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 30/page, admin+ only), `snapshots/latest/`, `snapshots/<id>/`. Read-only `/saas-admin` card surfaces call counts, connection rate, avg duration, transcript backlog, outcome breakdown table, agent breakdown table (or "No agent attribution field — per-agent metrics unavailable in V1" if `agent_breakdown` is empty), and active alert pills; no "Trigger Call" / "Reassign Agent" / "Send Coaching Note" / "Run Agent" / "Auto-dial" buttons. "Answered" rule (V1): `Call.status="Completed"`. Outcome breakdown: groups by `Call.status` since there's no separate `outcome` field on the model. Transcript backlog: `Call.created_at < now - 24h` AND no `CallTranscriptLine` rows linked. Agent breakdown: groups by `Call.agent` (CharField), top 10, with per-agent `call_count`, `connection_rate`, `avg_duration_seconds`; the unused `compute_agent_breakdown_30d(has_agent_field=False)` path returns `[]` for forward-compat / test coverage of the `no_agent_attribution_field` informational alert. Anomaly thresholds: `low_connection_rate` when `call_count_30d ≥ 10` AND `connection_rate_30d < 0.30` (small-sample guard); `high_transcript_backlog` when backlog > 20; `no_calls_today` when `call_count_24h == 0` AND `call_count_7d > 0`; `agent_concentration_risk` when top agent's calls > 70% of `call_count_30d` AND `call_count_30d > 10`; `no_agent_attribution_field` (informational only; never blocks `all_clear`) when the agent attribution path was disabled; `all_clear` when no real problem fires. `alert_text` is a short internal rationale stored on the snapshot for operator review; it is **NEVER** a customer-facing message and is **NEVER** sent to anyone. Every snapshot writes one `AgentRun` (`agent="calling_team_leader"`, model `"deterministic_v1"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`) + an `AuditEvent` of kind `calling_team_leader.snapshot.created`; the task closes with `calling_team_leader.daily_run.completed`. Kill switch reuses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `calling_team_leader.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `Call` / `Customer` / `Order` row counts stay constant. New tests: 24 backend cases covering duration parsing (known formats + garbage), call-count window filtering, connection stats (happy + zero), avg duration (happy + no answered calls), outcome breakdown (mixed statuses + empty DB), agent breakdown WITH the field (grouping + ordering) and WITHOUT the field (returns `[]`), transcript backlog (excludes fresh calls + calls with transcripts), every anomaly path (including the `no_agent_attribution_field` informational + `all_clear` healthy combo), happy-path Celery sweep, kill-switch refusal, sandbox propagation, no-side-effects guard, and `build_snapshot` field propagation. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2332 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 9D — Data Analyst Agent V1 SHIPPED (second business-level AI agent — operational / funnel analytics; recommendations-only; daily deterministic Celery snapshot; never triggers outbound action).** New `agents.DataAnalystSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0005_data_analyst_snapshot`. New `AgentRun.Agent.DATA_ANALYST` enum value + migration `ai_governance.0009_phase9d_add_data_analyst_agent`. Service helper `apps.agents.data_analyst.service` (`compute_funnel_counts` / `compute_conversion_rates` / `compute_top_states_30d` / `compute_day_of_week_counts_30d` / `detect_anomalies` / `compute_signals` / `build_snapshot`). Celery task `apps.agents.data_analyst.tasks.run_data_analyst_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 11:00 IST (env-shiftable; runs after the 08:00 IST Customer Success, 09:00 IST RTO Prevention, and 10:00 IST CFO sweeps). Each invocation writes exactly ONE `DataAnalystSnapshot` row plus one `AgentRun` (model `"deterministic_v1"`, provider `"disabled"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`, `agent=AgentRun.Agent.DATA_ANALYST`) and emits one `data_analyst.snapshot.created` AuditEvent + one `data_analyst.daily_run.completed` summary event. Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/data-analyst/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 30/page, admin+ only), `snapshots/latest/` (freshest snapshot + last run timestamp + status), `snapshots/<id>/`. Read-only `/saas-admin` card surfaces funnel counts, conversion rates, top 5 states (state, order count, ₹ revenue), day-of-week mini-grid, and active alert pills; no "Send Report" / "Trigger Funnel Fix" / "Apply Discount" / "Run Agent" / "Auto-rebalance" buttons. Funnel: 30-day `Lead` count, 30-day `Call` count, 30-day `Order` count where `stage ∈ {Confirmed, Dispatched, Out for Delivery, Delivered}` (confirmed-or-beyond), 30-day `Order` count where `stage=Delivered`, and 30-day reorder count. Reorder rule: an in-window order is a reorder unless it is the phone's earliest-ever order; checked by `Order.objects.filter(phone=phone).order_by("created_at").first()` and comparing the earliest order's `created_at` against the 30-day cutoff (if earliest is in-window then `reorder += max(0, in_window - 1)`, otherwise `reorder += in_window`). Conversion rates are simple ratios rounded to 4 decimals with divide-by-zero guards (return 0.0 when denominator is 0). Top states uses `Order.objects.filter(created_at__gte=cutoff).values("state").annotate(order_count=Count("id"), revenue=Sum("amount")).order_by("-order_count")[:n]`. Day-of-week uses `created_at.weekday()` → mon/tue/wed/thu/fri/sat/sun keys with zero-fill. Anomaly thresholds: `conversion_drop` when any of the 4 conversion rates < 0.10 AND the corresponding upstream count > 5 (small-sample guard); `geographic_concentration_shift` when the top state's share of total orders > 0.70 AND top state's order_count > 10; `dead_end_calls` when `call_count_30d > 0` AND `call_to_confirmed_rate < 0.05`; `lead_volume_drop` when `lead_count_30d == 0`; `all_clear` when none of the above fires. `alert_text` is a short factual rationale stored on the snapshot for operator review; it is **NEVER** a customer-facing message and is **NEVER** sent to anyone. Kill switch uses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `data_analyst.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `Lead` / `Call` / `Order` / `Customer` row counts stay constant. New tests: 18 backend cases covering funnel counts (in-window + out-of-window), conversion rates (happy path + divide-by-zero guards), top states (grouping, ordering, n limit), day-of-week bucketing, every anomaly path (including the small-sample false-positive guard), `all_clear`, happy-path Celery sweep, kill-switch refusal, sandbox propagation, no-side-effects guard, and `build_snapshot` propagation. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2308 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 9C — CFO Agent V1 SHIPPED (first business-level AI agent; recommendations-only; daily deterministic Celery financial snapshot; never triggers outbound action).** New `agents.CfoFinancialSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0004_cfo_financial_snapshot`. The existing `AgentRun.Agent.CFO` enum value is reused; no `ai_governance` migration is needed. Service helper `apps.agents.cfo.service` (`compute_rolling_revenue` / `compute_rolling_order_count` / `compute_payment_breakdown` / `compute_aov_30d` / `compute_rto_impact_30d` / `compute_customer_mix_30d` / `detect_anomalies` / `compute_signals` / `build_snapshot`). Celery task `apps.agents.cfo.tasks.run_cfo_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 10:00 IST (env-shiftable; runs after the 08:00 IST Customer Success and 09:00 IST RTO Prevention sweeps). Each invocation writes exactly ONE `CfoFinancialSnapshot` row plus one `AgentRun` (model `"deterministic_v1"`, provider `"disabled"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`, `agent=AgentRun.Agent.CFO`) and emits one `cfo.snapshot.created` AuditEvent + one `cfo.daily_run.completed` summary event. Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/cfo/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 50/page, admin+ only), `snapshots/<id>/`, `latest/` (freshest snapshot + last run timestamp + status). Read-only `/saas-admin` card surfaces revenue 24h/7d/30d, AOV, order counts, payment status breakdown, RTO impact, customer mix, and a flat list of active alert codes; no "Send Report" / "Trigger Refund" / "Apply Discount" / "Run Agent" / "Auto-collect" buttons. Snapshot fields: `revenue_24h`/`7d`/`30d`, `order_count_24h`/`7d`/`30d`, `paid_count`/`partial_count`/`pending_count` + `paid_amount`/`partial_amount`/`pending_amount`, `average_order_value` (30d), `rto_count_30d` + `rto_loss_amount_30d`, `new_customer_count_30d` + `returning_customer_count_30d`, `alerts` JSON list, `alert_text` short rationale (NEVER customer-facing), `agent_run`, `sandbox`, `created_at` / `updated_at`. Anomaly thresholds: `revenue_drop_24h` when `revenue_24h < revenue_7d/7 * 0.5`; `rto_spike` when `rto_count_30d / order_count_30d > 15%`; `high_pending_payments` when `pending_count > paid_count`; `low_order_volume` when `order_count_24h == 0 AND order_count_7d > 0`; `all_clear` when none of the above. Revenue uses `Payment.amount` where `status=Paid`; AOV uses `Order.amount`; RTO loss uses `Order.amount` of orders in `Order.Stage.RTO`. Customer mix bucket logic: a phone is **returning** when it has at least one order *before* the 30-day cutoff AND at least one in the window; **new** when it appears only inside the window; both counts are restricted to phones that also have a Customer row (avoids double-counting lead-only data). Kill switch uses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `cfo.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `Order` / `Customer` / `Payment` row counts stay constant. New tests: 18 backend cases covering rolling-revenue window filtering, rolling-order-count window filtering, payment breakdown counts + ₹ amounts, AOV (empty DB + averaging), RTO impact aggregation (RTO orders only), customer mix bucketing (new vs returning), per-anomaly detection paths, all-clear path, build_snapshot field propagation + sandbox flag, happy-path Celery sweep, kill-switch refusal, sandbox propagation, no-side-effects guard, and empty-DB compute_signals. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2290 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 9B — RTO Prevention Agent V1 SHIPPED (recommendations-only; daily deterministic Celery sweep over in-flight orders; never triggers outbound action).** New `agents.RtoRiskSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0003_rto_risk_snapshot`, new `AgentRun.Agent.RTO_PREVENTION` enum value + migration `ai_governance.0008_phase9b_add_rto_prevention_agent`, service helper `apps.agents.rto_prevention.service` (pure functions `compute_signals` / `compute_lifecycle_stage` / `compute_risk_score` / `compute_risk_tier` / `_build_risk_reasons` / `build_snapshot` / `choose_recommendation`), Celery task `apps.agents.rto_prevention.tasks.run_rto_prevention_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 09:00 IST (env-shiftable; after the 08:00 IST Customer Success sweep). Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/rto-prevention/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 50/page, filter `tier` + `kind` + `stage`, admin+ only), `snapshots/<id>/`, `cohorts/` (tier counts, recommendation counts, stage counts, last run timestamp, top 10 masked critical-tier orders). Read-only `/saas-admin` card lists tier + recommendation cohorts and top critical orders with masked order IDs; no "Call Customer" / "Send WhatsApp" / "Apply Discount" / "Force Dispatch" / "Run Agent" / "Auto-rescue" buttons. Scope: only orders in `Confirmed` / `Dispatched` / `Out for Delivery` stages, created in the last 30 days; orders in `Delivered` / `RTO` / `Cancelled` are explicitly excluded (asserted in tests). Lifecycle stages: `pre_dispatch` (no shipment yet), `in_transit` (shipment exists, no failed attempts), `delivery_at_risk` (shipment exists, NDR/undelivered/reattempt indicator on Delhivery status). Risk tiers: 0–39 `low` → `monitor_only`, 40–59 `medium` → `send_confirmation_reminder`, 60–79 `high` → `send_pre_delivery_call_request`, 80–100 `critical` → `escalate_to_team_lead`. Risk score base 30 + min(rto_count*15, 45) + min(complaint_count_14d*10, 20) + 15 if payment_status≠Paid + 10 if amount>₹5000 + min(days_since_order, 10) − min(delivered_count*5, 20) + 15*failed_attempts, clamped to [0, 100]. Reason codes populated from signals: `high_rto_history` (rto≥1), `recent_complaint` (>0 in last 14d), `cod_payment` (payment_status≠Paid), `high_value_order` (>₹5000), `stale_order` (>14d), `multiple_failed_attempts` (≥1 NDR). `recommendation_text` is a short internal rationale (e.g. "Day 5 post-order, in_transit, tier=high, reasons=[high_rto_history,cod_payment].") and is **NEVER** a customer-facing message; the agent **never** sends it to anyone. Every snapshot writes one `AgentRun` (`agent="rto_prevention"`, model `"deterministic_v1"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`) + an `AuditEvent` of kind `rto_prevention.snapshot.created`; the task closes with `rto_prevention.daily_run.completed`. Kill switch reuses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `rto_prevention.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.queue_template_message` / `send_freeform_text_message`, `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead`, `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment`, or Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `Order` / `Customer` / `Payment` / `Shipment` row counts stay constant. New tests: 27 backend cases covering lifecycle (pre_dispatch / in_transit / delivery_at_risk) — risk score boundaries — tier mapping (39→low, 40→medium, 59→medium, 60→high, 79→high, 80→critical, 100→critical) — reason-code population — recommendation kinds per tier — happy-path Celery sweep that excludes Delivered/RTO/Cancelled — kill-switch refusal — sandbox propagation — no-side-effects guard. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2272 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 9A — Customer Success / Reorder Agent V1 SHIPPED (recommendations-only; daily deterministic Celery sweep; never triggers outbound action).** New `agents.CustomerSuccessSnapshot` model + migration `agents.0002_customer_success_snapshot`, new `AgentRun.Agent.CUSTOMER_SUCCESS` enum value + migration `ai_governance.0007_phase9a_add_customer_success_agent`, service helper `apps.agents.customer_success.service` (pure functions `compute_signals` / `compute_lifecycle_stage` / `compute_score` / `_reorder_candidate` / `_at_risk` / `build_snapshot` / `choose_recommendation`), Celery task `apps.agents.customer_success.tasks.run_customer_success_agent_daily` scheduled via beat at 08:00 IST (env-shiftable). Read-only endpoints under `/api/v1/customer-success/`: `snapshots/` (paginated 50/page, filter `stage` + `kind`, admin+ only), `snapshots/<id>/`, `cohorts/` (stage counts, recommendation counts, reorder + at-risk totals, last run timestamp, top 10 masked reorder candidates). Read-only `/saas-admin` card lists cohort counts + top reorder candidates with masked customer IDs; no "Send" / "Trigger Call" / "Run Agent" / "Execute" / "Auto-dispatch" buttons. Lifecycle stages: `fresh_delivery` (0–2d), `early_usage` (3–7d), `mid_usage` (8–19d), `reorder_window` (20–30d), `late_reorder` (31–45d), `lapsed` (46+d). Reorder candidate when in 20–30d window AND no active Pending/Confirmed/Dispatched order AND no complaint AuditEvent in the last 14 days. At-risk when lapsed-no-reorder OR rto_count ≥ 2 OR recent complaint. Score base 60 + delivered_count*5 (cap 30) + reorder_count*10 (cap 20) − rto_count*10 (cap 20) − 15 if complaint within 14d, clamped to [0, 100]. Recommendation priority: `send_reorder_reminder` → `send_winback_offer` (late/lapsed + at-risk) → `send_usage_reminder` (early_usage) → `monitor_only`. `recommendation_text` is a short internal rationale; it is **NEVER** a customer-facing message and **never** sent to anyone. Every snapshot writes one `AgentRun` (`agent="customer_success"`, model `"deterministic_v1"`, `cost_usd=0`, `dry_run=True`) + an `AuditEvent` of kind `customer_success.snapshot.created`; the task closes with `customer_success.daily_run.completed`. Kill switch uses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 Postgres-safe pattern — `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` ordered by `-pk` wins and triggers `customer_success.daily_run.blocked` (asserted in tests with a second kill-switch row). Sandbox mode propagates: `is_sandbox_enabled()` flips both `snapshot.sandbox=True` and the linked `AgentRun.sandbox_mode=True`. The agent NEVER imports / calls `apps.whatsapp.services.send_*` / `queue_template_message` / `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` / `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment` / Razorpay; asserted by patching all four entrypoints in the safety test and checking `Order` / `Customer` row counts stay constant. New tests: 28 backend cases covering boundary days, score clamping, reorder/at-risk paths, recommendation kinds, happy-path Celery sweep, kill-switch refusal, sandbox propagation, and no-side-effects guard. Verification baseline after the new tests is **2245 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**.

- **Phase 7G-Live — Real Customer Delhivery Dispatch One-Shot Controlled Gate SHIPPED (CLI-only; no `.env.production` edit; no bulk/auto/AI dispatch).** New shipments-owned gate model `Phase7GLiveRealCustomerDispatchGate` + migration `shipments.0005_phase7g_live_real_customer_dispatch_gate` implement `draft → approved → executed/failed/cancelled/rollback_recorded` for exactly one Delhivery live AWB creation against exactly one confirmed customer order (`Order.stage="Confirmed"`). New service helper `apps.shipments.phase7g_live_real_customer_dispatch` plus five CLI commands (`inspect_phase7g_live_real_customer_gate`, `prepare_phase7g_live_real_customer_gate`, `approve_phase7g_live_real_customer_gate`, `execute_phase7g_live_real_customer_dispatch`, `rollback_phase7g_live_real_customer_dispatch`). New `cancel_awb` helper on `apps.shipments.integrations.delhivery_client` calls Delhivery's `/api/p/edit` cancellation endpoint with `cancellation=true`; mock mode short-circuits with a recorded response. New env flag `PHASE7G_LIVE_REAL_CUSTOMER_DISPATCH_ENABLED` defaults locked off and must be passed by runtime env prefix only; execute additionally requires `DELHIVERY_MODE=live` at runtime; `.env.production` is NOT edited. Approval/execute require runtime kill switch enabled, explicit confirm flag, non-empty operator, no prior executed gate for the same order id, an order in stage `Confirmed`, and Director signoff phrases `phase7g_live_gate_id_<ID>`, `target_order_<ORDER_ID>`, `phase7gLiveApproval`, plus structured `BEGIN_UTC`/`END_UTC` validated by `apps.saas.utc_window.validate_within_director_window` (15-minute cap, now inside window). The `_kill_switch_state()` helper uses the Phase 7E-Live-B Hotfix-1 fixed pattern: a `RuntimeKillSwitch(scope="global", enabled=False)` row always wins over the seeded enabled default, ordered by `-pk` for Postgres/SQLite determinism. Execute calls `apps.shipments.services.create_shipment(order=...)` once inside `transaction.atomic()`; locked-False flags `payment_mutation_made`, `order_payment_status_changed`, `whatsapp_sent`, `razorpay_called` are asserted to stay False. Rollback attempts the Delhivery cancellation API for the gate's AWB and records the provider result honestly — Delhivery may refuse if the shipment is already in transit; the gate transitions to `rollback_recorded` either way; the `cancellation_result` JSON is the truthful record. Read-only SaaS endpoint `/api/v1/saas/phase7g-live/gates/` and a `/saas-admin` card list masked gates; there are no approve/execute/rollback buttons. Tests add 9 backend cases; verification baseline after full run is **2217 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**. No real customer dispatch has been executed by this code change; each future execute still needs a separate Director directive and runtime env prefix.

- **Phase 7E-Live-B — Real Customer WhatsApp One-Shot Controlled Send Gate SHIPPED (CLI-only; no `.env.production` edit; no broadcast/campaign/freeform/bulk send).** New WhatsApp-owned gate model `Phase7ELiveBRealCustomerSendGate` + migration `whatsapp.0007_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send_gate` implement draft → approved → executed/failed/cancelled for exactly one approved Phase 5A template (`confirmation_reminder`, `delivery_reminder`, `rto_rescue`, `reorder_reminder`, `payment_reminder`, `usage_explanation`) to exactly one real customer phone. New env flag `PHASE7E_LIVE_B_REAL_CUSTOMER_SEND_ENABLED` defaults locked off and must be passed by runtime env prefix only. CLI commands: `inspect_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate`, `prepare_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate`, `approve_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate`, `execute_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_send`, `cancel_phase7e_live_b_real_customer_gate`. Approval/execute require runtime kill switch enabled, explicit confirm flag, non-empty operator, no prior executed gate, approved template, and Director signoff phrases `phase7e_live_b_gate_id_<ID>`, `target_phone_<last4>`, `template_<name>`, `phase7eLiveBApproval`, plus structured `BEGIN_UTC`/`END_UTC` validated by `validate_within_director_window` (15-minute cap, now inside window). Execute routes through Phase 5A `queue_template_message(..., override_limited_test_mode=True)` for this one CLI call only, preserving consent, approved template, Claim Vault, approval matrix, CAIO, idempotency, and audit gates while bypassing `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE` only for the approved one-shot. Read-only SaaS endpoint `/api/v1/saas/phase7e-live-b/gates/` and `/saas-admin` card list masked gates; there are no approve/execute/cancel buttons. There is **no rollback** because WhatsApp cannot be unsent. Tests add 8 backend cases; expected baseline after full verification is **2208 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**. No real customer send has been executed by this code change; each future execute still needs a separate Director directive and runtime env prefix.

- **Phase 8F Live Execute + Rollback Reading 1 COMPLETED on the VPS (2026-05-14; mechanism proof, no lasting change).** Phase 8F live execute was run for the first time with `attempt_id=1`, `gate_id=1`, `source_phase8e_gate_id=1`, target Order `NRG-20435`, target Payment `PAY-30125`, operator `Prarit Sidana`, and Director signoff window `BEGIN_UTC=2026-05-14T09:32:29Z` to `END_UTC=2026-05-14T09:45:29Z`. Execute result: `ok=True`, `status=executed`; mutation: `Order.NRG-20435.payment_status -> Paid`, `Payment.PAY-30125.status -> Paid`. All locked-False flags (`customer_notification_sent`, `whatsapp_sent`, `courier_called`, `provider_call_attempted`, `shipment_created`) were confirmed still False; no Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi call, no WhatsApp send, no customer notification, no shipment/AWB, and no `Order.state` mutation occurred. Rollback Reading 1 result: `ok=True`, `status=rollback_recorded`, `rollbackId=1`; Order `NRG-20435` was restored to `payment_status="Partial"` and Payment `PAY-30125` was restored to `status="Pending"` (both confirmed by psql). Health endpoint after rollback returned `{"status": "ok"}`. The three `PHASE8F_*` flags were passed by runtime env prefix only; `.env.production` was NOT edited. Multiple placeholder-signoff execute attempts before the final run were correctly refused by the guard (`phase8f_director_signoff_missing_structured_utc_window`, `phase8f_now_outside_director_signoff_utc_window_before_start`); Hotfix-3 recovery restored `attempt.status` between refused runs; no mutation occurred on any refused attempt. Final successful execute used shell variable expansion (`BEGIN=$(date -u -d "-1 minute")`, `END=$(date -u -d "+12 minutes")`) to compute and embed the UTC window. **Phase 8F is now complete as executed + rolled back. Phase 7E-Live-B and Phase 7G-Live remain NOT approved.** Test baseline remains **2200 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**; no code changed in this docs record.
