- **Phase 8F-Hotfix-3 - Attempt-level recovery command for blocked Phase 8F attempt SHIPPED (governance/admin command only; no Order/Payment/provider/WhatsApp/env touch).** Root cause: a prior Phase 8F execute run used placeholder Director signoff (`<FILL>`), failed the structured UTC window precheck before the atomic mutation block, and set attempt id=1 to `blocked`. Because `execute_phase8f_real_customer_controlled_mutation()` refuses any attempt not in `approved_for_one_shot_real_mutation`, future correct signoffs on attempt id=1 would otherwise fail with `phase8f_attempt_status_blocked_not_executable`. The fix adds `python manage.py recover_phase8f_attempt_to_approved` with required `--attempt-id`, `--director-signoff`, `--operator-name`, and `--confirm-phase8f-attempt-recovery`. It requires `PHASE8F_REAL_CUSTOMER_CONTROLLED_MUTATION_GATE_ENABLED=true`, runtime kill switch enabled, the attempt currently `blocked`, the gate currently `approved_for_one_shot_real_customer_mutation`, no executed attempt on the gate, non-empty operator name, explicit confirmation, and signoff phrases `phase8f_attempt_id_<ID>`, `phase8f_gate_id_<ID>`, and `phase8fHotfix3AttemptRecovery`. On success it only flips `attempt.status` from `blocked` to `approved_for_one_shot_real_mutation`, appends `phase8fHotfix3Recovery_recovered_from_blocked` to `attempt.blockers`, and writes a `phase8f.real_mutation.approved` audit event. No UTC window is required because this is governance recovery, not execute. Hotfix-3 was used during the pre-execute journey to restore `attempt.status` between correctly refused placeholder-signoff runs; no mutation occurred on any refused attempt.

- **Test Hygiene Hotfix-1 — Pin integration modes in conftest for VPS-safe full-suite runs SHIPPED (TEST-ONLY fix; no production code, model, migration, service, view, env flag, `.env*` file, or frontend touched).** When the VPS first ran the full `pytest -q` the suite reported ~43 failures (reproduced locally as **49 failures** by exporting `RAZORPAY_MODE=test WHATSAPP_PROVIDER=meta_cloud WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true` and running `pytest -q`). Root cause: tests that don't carry their own `override_settings` inherit `.env.production`'s non-mock integration values — real Razorpay TEST URLs (`rzp.io`) instead of the mock placeholder, real Meta Cloud signature verification rejecting the test fixtures' app-secret HMAC, and the Phase 5F-Gate final-send limited-test-mode allow-list guard refusing every outbound to phones not on `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_ALLOWED_TEST_NUMBERS` (empty on the VPS at suite time). None of these were product defects — purely test-isolation leaks from real `.env.production` values flowing through, and they would have been just as visible on any developer machine that runs `pytest` while the production env vars are set. The fix extends the existing `_force_eager_celery` session-autouse fixture in `backend/tests/conftest.py` to also pin `RAZORPAY_MODE="mock"`, `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER="mock"`, `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=False`, `DELHIVERY_MODE="mock"`, `VAPI_MODE="mock"`, `META_MODE="mock"` for the whole test session via `django.test.utils.override_settings`. Tests that intentionally need a non-mock value already carry their own `override_settings`, which wins over the session pin inside that test — none of them were touched and zero stragglers needed manual cleanup. After the fix: the same faked-`.env.production` command (`RAZORPAY_MODE=test WHATSAPP_PROVIDER=meta_cloud WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true python -m pytest -q`) is all green at **2188 passed**, and the normal `python -m pytest -q` stays at **2188 passed**. `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean, `manage.py check` clean, frontend lint/tests/build green. **Test Hygiene Hotfix-1 did NOT modify any production code, model, migration, service, view, env flag, `.env*` file, or frontend; did NOT execute or roll back anything; did NOT call Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi; did NOT send or queue WhatsApp; did NOT send a customer notification; did NOT mutate any business row.** **`.env.production` posture flagged for Director reconciliation against nd.md §17.** The VPS `.env.production` actually has `RAZORPAY_MODE=test` (NOT `mock`), `WHATSAPP_PROVIDER=meta_cloud` (NOT `mock`), `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true`. The WhatsApp pair appears intentional for the limited live test cohort that powered the Phase 7E-Live-A real send + the Phase 5F-Gate real inbound auto-reply test. However, `RAZORPAY_MODE=test` must be flipped to `live` before any real customer payment collection goes live (the only real provider-side write the platform has ever done is the Phase 6K-B + Phase 7D one-shot Razorpay TEST `order_*` runs, both rolled back). nd.md §17 still lists "stays mock-mode in production" — this entry must be reconciled by the Director. **Do NOT edit `.env.production` without Director sign-off.** Verification baseline unchanged at **2188 backend tests + 82 frontend tests** — the suite is now deterministic on BOTH SQLite (local dev) and Postgres (VPS) after the hotfix. **Phase 8F-Hotfix-1 / -2 follow-up (factual record):** the Phase 8F-Hotfix-1 recovery-approval CLI WAS run on the VPS on 2026-05-14 — Phase 8F gate id=1 is now `status="approved_for_one_shot_real_customer_mutation"`, attempt id=1 was minted (`status="approved_for_one_shot_real_mutation"`, every locked-False flag still False, `executed_at=NULL`), `gate.evidence_json.phase8fHotfix1Recovery` is stamped. At that checkpoint, Phase 8F EXECUTE had not yet run; Order `NRG-20435` was still `payment_status="Partial"`, Payment `PAY-30125` was still `status="Pending"`. Phase 8F-Hotfix-2 made the Phase 8F tests Postgres-safe (40/40 on VPS). Phase 8F live execute on the VPS still requires a separate Director directive AND all three Phase 8F env flags flipped true AND a 15-min structured UTC window. Phase 7E-Live-B (real customer WhatsApp send) and Phase 7G-Live (real customer courier execution) remain **NOT approved**.

- **Phase 8F-Hotfix-2 — Make Phase 8F execute/rollback tests Postgres-safe SHIPPED (test-only fix; no production code touched).** On the VPS (Postgres) the full-file run of `tests/test_phase8f_real_customer_controlled_mutation.py` saw `test_phase8f_execute_happy_path_mutates_only_target_statuses` and `test_phase8f_rollback_restores_old_statuses_no_side_effect` return `ok:False` because `_prep_approve(...)` created a Phase 8E gate but did not return its `pk` — the tests then hardcoded `phase8e_gate_id=1` (and the literal `phase8e_gate_id_1` in inline signoff strings). Postgres `ROLLBACK` does **not** reset `AUTOINCREMENT` sequences between tests in the same file run, so the real Phase 8E gate id is rarely 1 and the execute path's literal-ref check refused with `phase8f_director_signoff_must_reference_phase8e_gate_id`. SQLite effectively resets the sequence so the bug was invisible locally. The fix patches only `backend/tests/test_phase8f_real_customer_controlled_mutation.py`: `_prep_approve` now returns a 5-tuple `(attempt_id, gate_id, phase8e_gate_pk, order, payment)`; every caller unpacks the new `phase8e_gate_id` value; the two happy-path tests pass the dynamic `phase8e_gate_id` into `_structured_signoff`; every refusal test replaces inline `phase8e_gate_id_1` fragments with `f"phase8e_gate_id_{phase8e_gate_id}"`. Test intent, counts, and assertion logic are unchanged. No production code, model, migration, service, env flag, or API was modified. Phase 8F test count unchanged at 40 passed; verification baseline unchanged at **2188 backend tests + 82 frontend tests**, with `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean, `manage.py check` clean, frontend lint/tests/build green. **Phase 8F-Hotfix-1 correction (factual update):** the Phase 8F-Hotfix-1 recovery-approval CLI **WAS run on the VPS on 2026-05-14** — Phase 8F gate id=1 is now `status="approved_for_one_shot_real_customer_mutation"`, attempt id=1 was minted (`status="approved_for_one_shot_real_mutation"`, every locked-False flag still False, `executed_at=NULL`), `gate.evidence_json` carries the sealed `phase8fHotfix1Recovery` block (`recoveredFromMissingEnvApprovalBlock=true`, `recoveredBlocker="PHASE8F_REAL_CUSTOMER_CONTROLLED_MUTATION_GATE_ENABLED_must_be_true"`, `executionStillNotRun=true`, `phase8fHotfix1=true`). **At the Phase 8F-Hotfix-2 checkpoint, Phase 8F EXECUTE had not yet run on the VPS** — `Order.NRG-20435` is still `payment_status="Partial"`, `Payment.PAY-30125` is still `status="Pending"`, no real customer was charged, no provider was called, no notification was sent. Phase 8F live execute on the VPS still requires a separate Director directive AND all three Phase 8F env flags flipped true AND a 15-min structured UTC window referencing the actual Phase 8F gate id 1 / attempt id 1 / Phase 8E gate id 1 / target Order NRG-20435 / target Payment PAY-30125. Phase 7E-Live-B (real customer WhatsApp send) and Phase 7G-Live (real customer courier execution) remain **NOT approved**.

- **Phase 8D-Hotfix-1 — Resolve Phase 8C evidence via rollback record SHIPPED.** On-VPS investigation found Phase 8C attempt id=3 had `status="blocked"` (set by a later post-rollback re-run) even though every other piece of evidence proved a clean executed + rolled_back lifecycle — `executed_at=2026-05-12 11:11:50 UTC`, `recorded_signoff_window_valid=True`, all `order/payment/business_mutation_was_made=True`, every provider/send/courier/notification flag still False; rollback id=1 had `status=rollback_recorded` + `rollback_was_made=True` + `restored_*=Pending`; final Order and Payment both `payment_status="Pending"` / `status="Pending"`. The old Phase 8D resolver filtered Phase 8C attempts on `attempt.status == ROLLED_BACK` only, so it missed attempt id=3 and surfaced `phase8d_source_phase8c_attempt_not_found`. Root cause: the rollback record (not `attempt.status`) is the strongest single source of truth for "Phase 8C executed and rolled back" — a later re-run can flip `attempt.status` after the fact but cannot retroactively forge a rollback record. The new resolver `_resolve_phase8c_evidence_via_rollback(phase8c_gate)` in `apps.payments.phase8d_controlled_mutation_evidence_lock` finds the attempt via the rollback record (`attempt__gate=phase8c_gate` + `rollback.status=rollback_recorded` + `rollback.rollback_was_made=True` + `rollback.restored_order_status="Pending"` + `rollback.restored_payment_status="Pending"`, ordered by `-rolled_back_at`); then `_validate_phase8c_attempt` drops the `status==ROLLED_BACK` blocker but keeps every evidence check (`executed_at` set; `recorded_signoff_window_valid=True`; all `*_mutation_was_made=True`; every provider/send/courier/notification/shipment flag False); `_validate_phase8c_rollback_record` now explicitly asserts `restored_order_status=="Pending"` and `restored_payment_status=="Pending"`; `_validate_final_target_state` now also asserts `target_payment.raw_response.phase8c_sandbox is True` so the live Payment row must still carry the explicit sandbox proof at lock time. `_build_evidence_json` adds 5 top-level normalized fields: `executionEvidenceValid` / `rollbackEvidenceValid` / `attemptStatusAtEvidenceLock` (e.g. `"blocked"`) / `rollbackStatus` (e.g. `"rollback_recorded"`) / `finalDbRestored` — downstream readers consume these canonical signals instead of re-deriving from the nested phase8c snapshot. The readiness counter `eligiblePhase8CGateCount` now joins through `attempts__rollbacks` so a gate is "eligible" only when both `gate.status=rolled_back` AND at least one rollback record satisfies the evidence contract. **Phase 8D-Hotfix-1 did NOT execute Phase 8C, did NOT roll back Phase 8C, did NOT call Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi, did NOT send or queue WhatsApp, did NOT send a customer notification, did NOT create a `Shipment` / AWB / payment link, did NOT capture or refund, did NOT mutate any real `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog` / `WhatsAppMessage` row, did NOT edit any `.env*` file.** No new audit kind, no new model, no new migration, no new CLI command, no new API endpoint — surgical resolver patch only. 10 net new backend tests: preview / prepare / lock all succeed against `attempt.status="blocked"` evidence chain; resolver returns `(None, None)` when the rollback record is missing; readiness counter excludes gates without a valid rollback; `target_payment.raw_response.phase8c_sandbox=False` blocks; `raw_response.phase8c_sandbox` missing blocks; rollback `restored_order_status≠"Pending"` blocks; lock-row `evidence_json` carries all 5 normalized hotfix keys; end-to-end prepare → lock against the recovered evidence chain produces zero row-count drift across every protected business table and leaves target `Order.payment_status="Pending"` / `Payment.status="Pending"`. **2111 backend tests + 79 frontend tests, lint/check/build green.** `makemigrations --check --dry-run` → `No changes detected`. The on-VPS Phase 8C gate id=1 + attempt id=3 + rollback id=1 chain is now Phase 8D lock-eligible — but no Phase 8D lock was prepared during this turn.

- **Phase 8D — Phase 8C Controlled Mutation Evidence Lock SHIPPED (lock-only meta-audit; CLI-only review state changes; never run on the VPS).** New `payments.RazorpayPaymentOrderControlledMutationEvidenceLock` model + migration `payments.0022_phase8d_controlled_mutation_evidence_lock` (pure CreateModel; no `RunPython`). New service `apps.payments.phase8d_controlled_mutation_evidence_lock` snapshots immutable fields off the completed Phase 8C chain: Phase 8C gate (`status=rolled_back`, `dry_run_passed=True`) + Phase 8C attempt (`status=rolled_back`, `executed_at` present, `recorded_signoff_window_valid=True`, `order_mutation_was_made=True`, `payment_mutation_was_made=True`, `business_mutation_was_made=True`, every customer-facing flag still False) + Phase 8C rollback (`status=rollback_recorded`, `rollback_was_made=True`) + Phase 8B → 8A → 7I → 7D source chain into a single composite immutable row. The defensive `assert_phase8d_no_provider_or_business_mutation` guard runs at every transition (8 locked-False snapshot booleans + 11 protected business-row count delta — raises `ValueError` + emits invariant audit). - **Phase 7E-Live-A-Hotfix-3 — Safe retry after no-provider manual rollback before execution SHIPPED.** The retry-eligibility predicate `_retry_eligible` in `apps.payments.razorpay_whatsapp_internal_send` now has two qualifying paths sharing a new pure helper `_no_meta_side_effect` (every Meta-side / business-side / customer-side boolean False AND provider_message_id/status empty): (a) the existing **Hotfix-2 path** — `status in {rollback_recorded, failed}` AND `provider_call_attempted=True` AND `meta_cloud_call_attempted=True` AND the failure reason matches the known pre-hotfix wrapper marker (`Meta Cloud client does not expose send_template_message`); (b) the new **Hotfix-3 path** — `status=rollback_recorded` AND `provider_call_attempted=False` AND `meta_cloud_call_attempted=False` AND `executed_at is None` AND `failed_at is None` (operator rolled back an attempt that never reached the wrapper). Both paths still emit the existing `phase7e.internal_send.retry_prepared` audit kind with the same `retry_sequence` + `previous_attempt_id` payload (no new audit kind added). The retry path remains **database-only**: the lazy `_send_internal_template_via_meta_cloud` wrapper is patched with `mock.MagicMock` and asserted `assert_not_called` in the retry-no-wrapper test; the eleven business / send / courier row counts (`_row_counts()`) stay unchanged across the retry; the locked-False contract on the new attempt row starts intact (`provider_call_attempted=False`, `meta_cloud_call_attempted=False`, `whatsapp_message_created=False`, `whatsapp_message_queued=False`, `customer_notification_sent=False`, `business_mutation_was_made=False`, `real_customer_allowed=False`, `real_customer_phone_used=False`). Refused for any of: `provider_call_attempted=True`, `meta_cloud_call_attempted=True`, `executed_at is not None`, `failed_at is not None` with unknown failure reason, `provider_message_id` set, `whatsapp_message_created/queued=True`, `real_customer_phone_used=True`, `customer_notification_sent=True`, `business_mutation_was_made=True` — manual review only with `nextAction=phase7e_live_attempt_terminal_manual_review_required`. **Hotfix-3 did NOT execute any WhatsApp send, did NOT call Meta Cloud, did NOT send to a real customer, did NOT mutate any business row, did NOT send a customer notification, did NOT touch a real customer phone, did NOT edit any `.env*` file.** The `PHASE7E_LIVE_INTERNAL_WHATSAPP_SEND_ENABLED` env flag remains locked-OFF. The on-VPS Phase 7E-Live attempt id 2 (`rollback_recorded` with all provider flags False, `executed_at=null`, `failed_at=null`) is now retry-eligible — the next operator-driven prepare against that gate would mint `phase7e_live::internal_send::phase7e_gate::1::retry::3` — but no retry was minted during this turn. 13 net new backend tests including a VPS-state end-to-end mirror that walks attempt-1 = wrapper-failure → attempt-2 = no-provider rollback → attempt-3 = fresh retry-prepared row with `_row_counts()` parity + wrapper-`assert_not_called`. **1954 backend tests + 74 frontend tests, lint/check/build green.** `makemigrations --check --dry-run` → `No changes detected`. **Phase 7E-Live-B (real customer WhatsApp send) remains NOT approved.**

When the latest attempt is `rollback_recorded` or `failed` AND `provider_message_id` is empty AND `provider_status` is empty AND every Meta-side boolean is False AND the warnings list contains the **known pre-hotfix wrapper-method marker** (`Meta Cloud client does not expose send_template_message`), prepare mints a fresh retry row with idempotency key `phase7e_live::internal_send::phase7e_gate::<gate>::retry::<N>`. - **Phase 7G-Hotfix-1 — Structured UTC Window Guard for Delhivery execute command SHIPPED.** `execute_phase7g_courier_one_shot` (and the `execute_delhivery_courier_one_shot` CLI) now refuse to dispatch unless the Director sign-off contains literal `BEGIN_UTC=<ISO-Z>` / `END_UTC=<ISO-Z>` markers, parsed window length ≤ 15 minutes (`apps.saas.utc_window.validate_within_director_window`, default `max_window_seconds=900`), window fresh (`window_start ≥ now − 24h`), `END_UTC > BEGIN_UTC`, and `datetime.now(tz=UTC) ∈ [window_start, window_end]`. **Refusal happens before the lazy `_create_awb_via_dedicated_wrapper` import + Delhivery client touch** (asserted in every refusal test with `mock.MagicMock.assert_not_called`). Six Phase 7G blockers surface as safe JSON: `phase7g_director_signoff_missing_structured_utc_window`, `phase7g_director_signoff_malformed_structured_utc_window`, `phase7g_director_signoff_window_too_long_max_15_min`, `phase7g_director_signoff_window_stale_more_than_24h_old`, `phase7g_now_before_director_signoff_utc_window_start`, `phase7g_now_after_director_signoff_utc_window_end`. On a successful execute the parsed window is persisted onto the attempt row via `recorded_signoff_window_valid=True` + `recorded_signoff_window_start_utc` + `recorded_signoff_window_end_utc` — these fields shipped nullable on `RazorpayCourierExecutionAttempt` in Phase 7G migration `payments.0016`, so **no new migration is required**. - **Phase 7D-Hotfix-1 - Structured UTC Window Guard SHIPPED for both Razorpay TEST execute commands.** New `apps.saas.utc_window.validate_within_director_window` (alias `validate_execution_window`, default `max_window_seconds=900`) is the canonical execute-time predicate; both `execute_razorpay_controlled_pilot_test_order` (Phase 7D) and `execute_single_razorpay_test_order` (Phase 6K) now refuse to dispatch unless the Director sign-off contains literal `BEGIN_UTC=<ISO-Z>` / `END_UTC=<ISO-Z>` markers, the parsed window length is `≤ 15 minutes`, the window is fresh (`window_start ≥ now - 24h`), and `datetime.now(tz=UTC)` falls inside `[window_start, window_end]` at runtime. Refusal happens **before the provider boundary** — `_create_order_via_sdk` is asserted `assert_not_called` in every refusal test. Phase 7D blockers (`phase7d_director_signoff_missing_structured_utc_window`, `phase7d_now_outside_director_signoff_utc_window`, `phase7d_director_signoff_window_too_long_max_15_min`, `phase7d_director_signoff_window_stale_more_than_24h_old`, `phase7d_director_signoff_malformed_structured_utc_window`) and Phase 6K blockers (`phase6k_*` mirrors) all surface as safe JSON. Two schema-only migrations: `payments.0014_phase7d_hotfix_director_signoff_window` adds 3 nullable fields to `RazorpayControlledPilotExecutionAttempt` (`recorded_signoff_window_valid: BooleanField(null=True)`, `recorded_signoff_window_start_utc: DateTimeField(null=True)`, `recorded_signoff_window_end_utc: DateTimeField(null=True)`); `saas.0007_phase7d_hotfix_director_signoff_window` adds the same 3 nullable fields to `RuntimeProviderExecutionAttempt`. **No `RunPython` data migration; past rows stay `NULL`.** Historical Phase 7D attempt id 1 (`order_SmThqpK6sc6Dhs`, executed `2026-05-07T12:42:46Z`, rolled back) is **NOT edited** — it remains the canonical legacy free-text row that Phase 7E continues to handle via the `--acknowledge-source-phase7d-window-violation` path. Service-layer wiring: `apps.payments.razorpay_controlled_pilot_execution.execute_phase7d_razorpay_test_order` and `apps.saas.provider_execution.execute_single_razorpay_test_order` parse + validate the structured window before any SDK call; on refusal the attempt row records `blockers` and stays `BLOCKED` with no provider call. The Phase 6K CLI now requires `--director-signoff` (raises `CommandError` if missing). `apps.saas.utc_window` is still **pure** — no DB / settings / env / logging — and is now exported as `parse_director_signoff_window`, `validate_review_window`, `validate_within_director_window`, `validate_execution_window`, `ParsedWindow`, `WindowValidationResult`. **Hotfix-1 did NOT re-run any execute command.** No real Razorpay HTTP request was issued. No WhatsApp send / queue. No Meta Cloud call. No Delhivery call. No shipment / AWB. No payment link / capture / refund. No customer notification. No business mutation. No `.env*` edit. 33 net new backend tests (21 utility additions + 10 Phase 7D guard + 11 Phase 6K guard, minus 9 existing tests adapted to pass structured signoff in fixtures); **1705 backend tests + 68 frontend tests, lint/check/build green.** `makemigrations --check --dry-run` → `No changes detected`. **Phase 7F (Delhivery shipment) / Phase 7E-Live (real customer WhatsApp send) / live customer flow remain NOT approved.** Any future provider-touching command now inherits the structured-window predicate via `apps.saas.utc_window`.

— actual provider execution requires a separate, dated, written Director directive that names the exact attempt id; this is by design.

- **Phase 7B - Controlled Pilot Execution Gate (gate-only) shipped:** Phase 6T Final Phase 6 Audit + Lock is FULL PASS, Phase 7A is planning-only and accepted, and Phase 7B implements the gate-only lifecycle for a future controlled pilot review. New `payments.RazorpayControlledPilotExecutionGate` + `payments.RazorpayControlledPilotGateDryRunRecord` + `payments.RazorpayControlledPilotGateRollbackDryRunRecord` models + migration `payments.0011_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`. New service `apps.payments.razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate` ships 12 functions (`build_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate_contract`, `inspect_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate_readiness`, `validate_phase7b_source_lock_eligibility`, `preview_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `prepare_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `dry_run_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `rollback_dry_run_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `approve_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `reject_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `archive_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gate`, `summarize_phase7b_controlled_pilot_gates`, `assert_phase7b_no_unauthorised_provider_call`). **There is no `execute_*` function in this module.** Nine new CLI commands (`inspect_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate_readiness`, `preview_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `prepare_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `dry_run_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `rollback_dry_run_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `approve_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `reject_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `archive_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gate`, `inspect_razorpay_controlled_pilot_gates`) — **no `execute_*` command exists**. Six read-only GET admin SaaS endpoints under `/api/v1/saas/razorpay/controlled-pilot-gate{,-readiness,-gates,-gates/<id>,-preview,-dry-runs/<gate_id>,-rollback-dry-runs/<gate_id>}/`. **There is no POST API endpoint that prepares, dry-runs, rollback-dry-runs, approves, rejects, archives, or executes a Phase 7B gate.** 13 new audit kinds (`razorpay.controlled_pilot_gate.{readiness_inspected,previewed,prepared,dry_run_passed,dry_run_failed,rollback_dry_run_passed,rollback_dry_run_failed,approved_for_phase7c_review,rejected,archived,blocked,kill_switch_disabled_blocked,invariant_violation_blocked}`); each kind ≤ 64 chars (Phase 6T-hotfix lesson preserved). New env flag `PHASE7_CONTROLLED_PILOT_GATE_ENABLED=false` default; `.env.production` not edited. Approve requires non-empty manual review reason AND `dry_run_passed=true` AND `rollback_dry_run_passed=true`; on success the status flips to `approved_for_future_phase7c_execution_review` only. **Phase 7B never executes a pilot, never calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi (asserted with mock spies), never sends or queues a WhatsApp message, never creates a `WhatsAppMessage` outbound row or a `WhatsAppLifecycleEvent` send trigger, never creates a shipment / AWB, never mutates real `Order` / `Payment` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `WhatsAppMessage` / `WhatsAppConversation` rows. Phase 7B does not validate the live `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` (provider-execution key validation is deferred to Phase 7C+); a planted live key value never appears raw in any command output, API response, or audit payload (asserted in tests). All execution / mutation / provider-enabling flags remain false; provider modes remain safe/mock/test-only as applicable; `DELHIVERY_MODE` stays mock; `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE` may remain true as a safety allow-list guard while WhatsApp send/automation flags remain false; MCP write/provider tools remain disabled.** `/saas-admin` adds a read-only "Razorpay Controlled Pilot Execution Gate" section (`razorpay-controlled-pilot-execution-gate-section`) with the safe-to-start-phase7c-review badge, env-posture block, forbidden-action chip cloud, and explicit CLI-only banner — no Start Pilot / Run Pilot / Execute Pilot / Send WhatsApp / Queue WhatsApp / Notify Customer / Create Shipment / Create AWB / Book Courier / Dispatch Order / Call Delhivery / Call Meta / Call Razorpay / Mark Paid / Capture Payment / Refund / Create Payment Link / Mutate Order / Approve Gate / Reject Gate buttons exist on the page (asserted in vitest). 45 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; **1540 backend tests + 64 frontend tests, lint/check/build green.** `makemigrations --check --dry-run` → `No changes detected`. **Phase 7C / live execution is NOT approved.** Phase 7B's `approved_for_future_phase7c_execution_review` is a status name, not an authorisation; Phase 7C requires a fresh, dated, written Director approval naming a specific Phase 7B gate id.

- **Phase 6T - Final Phase 6 Audit + Lock shipped:** Phase 6S Limited Internal Dispatch Pilot Plan is complete, and Phase 6T adds the final Phase 6N -> 6S audit-lock / decision-gate layer. New `payments.RazorpayPhase6FinalAuditLock` model + migration `payments.0010_razorpayphase6finalauditlock` store a final attestation over Phase 6N, 6O, 6P, 6Q, 6R, and 6S. New service `apps.payments.razorpay_phase6_final_audit_lock`, seven CLI commands, and four read-only admin SaaS endpoints under `/api/v1/saas/razorpay/phase6-final-audit-lock...` expose readiness, preview, lock summaries, and sanitized records. Review state changes are CLI-only and gated by `RAZORPAY_PHASE6_FINAL_AUDIT_LOCK_ENABLED=false` by default. `/saas-admin` adds a read-only "Razorpay Phase 6 Final Audit + Lock" section. **Phase 6T is audit-lock-only: no future controlled pilot execution, no WhatsApp send/queue, no Meta Cloud call, no Delhivery call, no Razorpay call, no shipment/AWB creation, no customer notification, and no real Order / Payment / Shipment / DiscountOfferLog / Customer / Lead mutation happened. Phase 6 is now audit-locked; Phase 7A / future controlled internal pilot execution design is next only after explicit Director approval.** Verification baseline: **1495 backend tests + 62 frontend tests, lint/check/build green.**

- **Phase 6M — Razorpay Webhook Handler (test-mode) shipped:** Phase 6L Razorpay Test Execution Audit + Webhook Readiness Plan + Phase 6M-0 MCP Gateway Foundation are **FULL PASS**. Phase 6M implements the canonical Razorpay test-mode webhook handler designed in the Phase 6L plan. **Handler + persistence + safety only; no business mutation, no customer notification, no Razorpay API call.** New `apps.payments.razorpay_webhooks` service exposes `process_razorpay_webhook(raw_body, headers, request_meta)` with strict gates: env flag → secret presence → signature header presence → HMAC-SHA256 over RAW body with constant-time compare → JSON parse → event classification + safe summary → replay-window check (default 300s) → allowlist + denylist enforcement → `X-Razorpay-Event-Id` idempotency dedup → persist `RazorpayWebhookEvent` row + matching `AuditEvent`. **Signature verification consumes the raw bytes Razorpay sent — never re-serialises the JSON before checking.** New `RazorpayWebhookEvent` model (migration `payments.0004_add_razorpay_webhook_event`) stores per-event environment, signature_present + signature_valid, replay_window_valid, idempotency_status (`first_seen` / `duplicate` / `missing_event_id`), processing_status (`received` / `blocked` / `verified` / `stored` / `duplicate` / `ignored` / `failed`), processing_mode (`test_mode_record_only` in 6M), provider_order_id / provider_payment_id / provider_refund_id, amount_paise + currency, payment_status + order_status, contains list, payload_hash, safe_payload_summary (no raw body, no raw secret, no card / vpa / upi / bank_account / wallet / email / contact / phone / mobile / address fields — sensitive keys scrubbed by `_mask_recursive`), scrubbed_keys list, denied_reason, blockers / warnings, locked safety booleans (`business_mutation_attempted=False`, `business_mutation_was_made=False`, `customer_notification_attempted=False`, `customer_notification_sent=False`, `raw_secret_exposed=False`, `full_pii_exposed=False`), `request_headers_summary` (whitelisted: content-type / user-agent / x-razorpay-event-id; signature is presence-only — never echoed), unique constraint on `(provider, source_event_id)` so duplicates can never insert twice. New `RazorpayTestWebhookView` at `POST /api/webhooks/razorpay/test/` is the public Phase 6M endpoint; the existing `RazorpayWebhookView` at `POST /api/webhooks/razorpay/` (production payment-link path) is unchanged. Nine new audit kinds: `razorpay.webhook.{received,blocked,signature_verified,signature_failed,duplicate_ignored,stored,event_denied,replay_blocked,business_mutation_blocked}`. Four new management commands: `inspect_razorpay_webhook_handler_readiness --json` (read-only readiness report — env flags + counters + typed `nextAction`; expected `safeToReceiveTestWebhooks=false` until env flag flipped), `simulate_razorpay_webhook_event --event <name> [--event-id ...] [--amount-paise 100] [--order-id order_Sks3KPf0vntKhf] [--payment-id ...] [--refund-id ...] [--payment-link-id ...] [--created-at ...] --json` (signs synthetic payload with `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, runs through the same `process_razorpay_webhook` service the public endpoint uses; never calls Razorpay), `inspect_razorpay_webhook_events --json [--limit N] [--event-name ...] [--status ...]` (safe summaries only), `purge_razorpay_webhook_test_events --json [--apply] [--keep-last N]` (defaults to dry-run; refuses to delete any row whose `business_mutation_was_made=True` or `customer_notification_sent=True`). Four new admin/auth-protected DRF endpoints under `/api/v1/saas/razorpay/`: `webhook-handler-readiness/` (admin GET only — POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405), `webhook-events/` (admin GET list — `?limit=N&event_name=&status=`), `webhook-events/<id>/` (admin GET detail), `webhook-events/simulate/` (admin POST — re-uses the management command's payload builder + same service path; never calls Razorpay). New env settings (all default safe): `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_TEST_MODE_ENABLED=false`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_BUSINESS_MUTATION_ENABLED=false`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_NOTIFY_CUSTOMER_ENABLED=false`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_STORE_RAW_PAYLOAD=false`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_ALLOW_TEST_EVENTS_ONLY=true`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_REPLAY_WINDOW_SECONDS=300`, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_ALLOWED_EVENTS` (9 events: payment.authorized / payment.captured / payment.failed / order.paid / refund.created / refund.processed / payment_link.paid / payment_link.cancelled / payment_link.expired), `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_DENIED_EVENTS` (9 events: subscription / dispute / transfer / payout / virtual_account / qr_code / invoice). Frontend `/saas-admin` gains a new **"Razorpay Webhook Handler (Test Mode)"** section: handler-readiness card (test mode enabled / webhook secret present / replay window / allowed-events count + safety-invariants sub-card with `businessMutationEnabled` / `customerNotificationEnabled` / `storeRawPayload` rows + counters sub-card with `businessMutationCount` / `customerNotificationCount` / `rawSecretExposureCount` / `fullPiiExposureCount` — every counter green when zero, red on any non-zero), events table (event id / event / signature valid / idempotency status + duplicate count / processing status / order id / payment id / amount / received-at). New TypeScript types (`SaasRazorpayWebhookHandlerReadiness`, `SaasRazorpayWebhookEventDto`, `SaasRazorpayWebhookEventsResponse`, `SaasRazorpayWebhookSimulationResult`); 3 new api methods (`getSaasRazorpayWebhookHandlerReadiness`, `getSaasRazorpayWebhookEvents`, `simulateSaasRazorpayWebhookEvent`) with deterministic mock fallback. **No "Capture Payment" / "Mark Order Paid" / "Replay Event" / "Send WhatsApp" / "Refund" buttons exist anywhere on the UI.** **Hard rules preserved**: Phase 6M NEVER calls Razorpay; NEVER mutates `Order.status` / `Payment.status` / `Shipment.status` / `DiscountOfferLog`; NEVER creates a Razorpay order / payment link; NEVER captures a payment; NEVER refunds; NEVER sends a WhatsApp; NEVER sends a customer notification; NEVER returns the raw webhook secret / raw signature / raw payload (asserted with fake env values + planted card / email / phone payloads in tests); refuses any event not on the 9-name allowlist; refuses any event on the 9-name denylist; refuses payloads older than the replay window; duplicate `X-Razorpay-Event-Id` returns 200 + `processing_status=duplicate` + increments `duplicate_count` and never re-processes. The Phase 6K Razorpay execution audit (`pex_8f309650e9644cfaae4418f9` → `order_Sks3KPf0vntKhf`) remains untouched; the new handler is wholly independent of the Phase 2B production payment-link receiver. WhatsApp `WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`; broad-automation flags remain LOCKED OFF; campaigns / broadcast remain LOCKED. **42 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; 1283 backend + 48 frontend, all green.** Recommended next phase: **Phase 6N — Razorpay Webhook Business Mutation Sandbox Plan** — the planning-only follow-up that designs how a future Phase 6O may safely flip `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_BUSINESS_MUTATION_ENABLED=true` against synthetic test orders only.
- **Phase 6M-0 — MCP Gateway Foundation + AI Connector Readiness shipped:** Phase 6L Razorpay Test Execution Audit Review + Webhook Readiness Plan is **FULL PASS**. Phase 6M-0 adds the **read-only MCP foundation layer** that prepares the application for future Claude / ChatGPT / Codex / internal MCP-style remote AI clients without enabling any external traffic. New `apps.mcp_gateway` Django app ships **six models** — `McpClientApp` (provider/client registry, default `is_active=False`), `McpAccessPolicy` (per-client/org policy, default `read_only=True`, `allow_write_tools=False`, `allow_provider_tools=False`), `McpToolDefinition` (tool registry, default `read_only=True`, `provider_call_allowed=False`, `business_mutation_allowed=False`, `requires_auth=True`), `McpResourceDefinition` (read-only resources), `McpPromptDefinition` (template-only prompts), `McpToolInvocationLog` (audit row per call: `safe_input_summary` + `safe_output_summary` + `input_hash` + locked safety booleans `provider_call_attempted=False` / `business_mutation_attempted=False` / `raw_secret_exposed=False` / `full_pii_exposed=False`). Migration `0001_initial`; everything backward-compatible. New `apps.mcp_gateway.services` layer ships eight modules: `masking` (mask phones / emails / provider keys / sensitive keys; `detect_full_pii` + `detect_raw_secret` defence-in-depth scans; ISO timestamps no longer false-positive), `schemas` (8-scope read-only enabled vocabulary + 8-scope future-disabled list + 13-tool **forbidden tool list** the executor refuses to dispatch), `default_tools` (10 read-only tools — `system.{get_phase_status,get_health}`, `saas.get_current_org`, `audit.search_events_masked`, `whatsapp.inspect_auto_reply_gate`, `razorpay.{inspect_test_execution_audit,inspect_webhook_readiness,plan_webhook_readiness}`, `dashboard.get_kpis`, `agents.get_agent_status`), `default_resources` (7 resources `nirogidhara://{phase/current-status, razorpay/phase-6k-audit, razorpay/webhook-readiness, whatsapp/safety-status, system/health, docs/runbook-summary, audit/recent-masked}`), `default_prompts` (6 templates `ceo_daily_briefing` / `caio_payment_audit_review` / `razorpay_webhook_readiness_review` / `whatsapp_safety_review` / `phase_deploy_checklist` / `crm_sales_summary_masked`), `registry` (idempotent seeder; refuses any forbidden-tool seed; force-pins read-only/no-provider/no-mutation invariants on update), `audit` (writes one `McpToolInvocationLog` row + matching `AuditEvent` per call; 7 audit kinds `mcp.tool.{call_attempted,call_blocked,call_denied,call_succeeded,call_failed,forbidden_tool_blocked}` + `mcp.registry.seeded`), `auth` (Phase 6M-0 auth shim — internal admin gets the read-only enabled scope set; never grants a write or provider scope), `tool_executor` (single entry `execute_mcp_tool` with seven gates: forbidden-tool refusal → tool lookup + enabled → `MCP_PROVIDER_TOOLS_ENABLED` / `MCP_WRITE_TOOLS_ENABLED` flags → scope check → auth check → mask input + hash → handler dispatch → mask output + truncate → audit log; on success returns `{passed, status, toolName, invocationId, readOnly, providerCallAttempted, businessMutationAttempted, rawSecretExposed, fullPiiExposed, outputTruncated, durationMs, result, blockers, warnings, nextAction}`), `tool_handlers` (every handler is a thin read-only wrapper around an existing safe selector — `apps.saas.context.get_default_organization`, `apps.saas.razorpay_audit_review.*`, `apps.whatsapp.dashboard.get_auto_reply_gate_summary`, `apps.audit.models.AuditEvent.objects` with `mask_payload`, etc — none call external providers, none mutate business records), `readiness` (`get_mcp_gateway_readiness` + `get_mcp_security_posture` selectors; report `mcpEnabled`, `readOnlyMode`, `writeToolsEnabled`, `providerToolsEnabled`, registry counters, exposure counters, blockers, `safeToEnableReadOnlyMcp`, `safeToStartPhase6M`, typed `nextAction`). Five new strictly-read-only / planning-only management commands: `ensure_mcp_defaults --json` (idempotent registry seeder; emits `mcp.registry.seeded` audit row; never enables MCP), `inspect_mcp_gateway_readiness --json`, `list_mcp_tools --json`, `simulate_mcp_tool_call --tool <name> [--input '{...}'] --json` (CLI-only safe simulator; runs through the full executor with `bypass_auth_for_internal=True`), `inspect_mcp_security_posture --json`. Seven new admin/auth-protected DRF endpoints under `/api/v1/mcp/` (`readiness/`, `security-posture/`, `tools/`, `tools/simulate/` (POST admin), `resources/`, `prompts/`, `invocations/`) — POST/PATCH/DELETE on read endpoints return 405; **no public unauthenticated endpoint, no tool-execute endpoint that bypasses the policy gate**. Frontend `/saas-admin` gains a new **"MCP Gateway Readiness"** section: MCP-mode cards (`MCP_ENABLED` / read-only / write tools / provider tools), security posture sub-card (auth required / forbidden tools registered / write tools enabled / provider tools enabled / exposure counters — every counter green when zero), tool table (10 rows: name / category / risk / read-only / provider call allowed / business mutation allowed / scopes), recent invocations table (provider call attempted / business mutation attempted columns — both red if non-zero, green if zero). New TypeScript types (`McpGatewayReadiness`, `McpSecurityPosture`, `McpToolDefinitionDto`, `McpToolsResponse`, `McpResourceDefinitionDto`, `McpResourcesResponse`, `McpPromptDefinitionDto`, `McpPromptsResponse`, `McpToolInvocationDto`, `McpInvocationsResponse`, `McpToolSimulationResult`); 7 new api methods (`getMcpReadiness`, `getMcpSecurityPosture`, `getMcpTools`, `getMcpResources`, `getMcpPrompts`, `getMcpInvocations`, `simulateMcpToolCall`) with deterministic mock fallback. **No "Run Tool" / "Execute MCP" / "Send" / "Go Live" buttons exist anywhere on the UI.** **LOCKED env defaults**: `MCP_ENABLED=false`, `MCP_TRANSPORT=streamable_http`, `MCP_REQUIRE_AUTH=true`, `MCP_READ_ONLY_MODE=true`, `MCP_WRITE_TOOLS_ENABLED=false`, `MCP_PROVIDER_TOOLS_ENABLED=false`, `MCP_AUDIT_ENABLED=true`, `MCP_MASK_PII=true`, `MCP_MAX_TOOL_CALLS_PER_MINUTE=30`, `MCP_MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS=12000`, `MCP_EXPOSE_RESOURCES=false`, `MCP_EXPOSE_PROMPTS=false`, `MCP_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=""`, `MCP_SERVICE_TOKEN=""`, `MCP_JWT_SIGNING_KEY=""`, `MCP_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS=3600`. Missing token / signing key never crashes the app — readiness simply reports the gap. **Hard rules preserved**: forbidden tool list (`razorpay.create_order` / `razorpay.capture_payment` / `razorpay.create_payment_link` / `whatsapp.send_message` / `delhivery.create_shipment` / `vapi.place_call` / `campaign.start` / `payment.execute` / `order.create_live` / `crm.bulk_update` / `system.shell` / `system.sql` / `system.http_fetch`) NEVER seeded, NEVER dispatched (executor refuses on the prefix list); raw secrets / full PII / raw provider responses NEVER stored on `McpToolInvocationLog` (asserted with fake env values + planted full-phone audit rows in tests); the executor refuses any tool whose `provider_call_allowed=True` while `MCP_PROVIDER_TOOLS_ENABLED=false` and any tool whose `business_mutation_allowed=True` while `MCP_WRITE_TOOLS_ENABLED=false` (asserted by mutating a row at runtime + asserting the executor still blocks); `WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`; broad-automation flags remain LOCKED OFF; campaigns / broadcast remain LOCKED. **43 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; 1241 backend + 46 frontend, all green.** Recommended next phase: **Phase 6M — Razorpay Webhook Handler Implementation (test-mode)** — implements the receiver designed in the Phase 6L plan, gated by `PHASE6M_RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_TEST_MODE_ENABLED=true` env flag (default OFF). The MCP foundation here is independent of the Phase 6M Razorpay handler — both can land in parallel.
- **Phase 6L — Razorpay Test Execution Audit Review + Webhook Readiness Plan shipped:** Phase 6K-B (the manual VPS-side Razorpay test-mode `create_order` execution) is **FULL PASS** (`execution_id=pex_8f309650e9644cfaae4418f9`, `provider_object_id=order_Sks3KPf0vntKhf`, `amount_paise=100`, `currency=INR`, `provider_environment=test`, `business_mutation_was_made=false`, `payment_link_created=false`, `payment_captured=false`, `customer_notification_sent=false`, `rollback_status=completed`, `PHASE6K_RAZORPAY_TEST_EXECUTION_ENABLED` rolled back to `false`). Phase 6L adds the **read-only audit-review + webhook readiness planning layer** without making any new Razorpay calls, without creating any payment link, without capturing payment, and without mutating any business record. New `apps.saas.razorpay_audit_review` ships three pure functions: (a) `review_razorpay_test_execution_audit(execution_id)` replays the Phase 6K row against ten required invariants — `providerCallAttempted=True`, `externalCallWasMade=True`, `businessMutationWasMade=False`, `paymentLinkCreated=False`, `paymentCaptured=False`, `customerNotificationSent=False`, `realMoney=False`, `realCustomerDataAllowed=False`, `rollbackStatus=completed`, `providerObjectId` present — plus a defence-in-depth raw-key-leak scan over every linked Phase 6K AuditEvent (kinds `runtime.provider_execution.{prepared,blocked,started,succeeded,failed,rolled_back,archived,invariant_violation_blocked}`); audit events are returned with `payloadKeys` only (never values), `safe_response_summary` is whitelisted to `{id, status, amount, currency, receipt}`, raw provider response NEVER returned, raw secrets NEVER returned. (b) `inspect_razorpay_webhook_readiness()` reports presence-only env state — Razorpay key mode (test/live/unknown/missing) with masked id, `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` PRESENCE BOOLEAN ONLY (never the value), latest succeeded Phase 6K `execution_id` + `provider_object_id` + `rollback_status`, plus a typed `nextAction` ∈ {`ready_to_plan_razorpay_webhook_readiness`, `fix_razorpay_webhook_readiness_blockers`}. (c) `plan_razorpay_webhook_readiness()` returns the canonical Phase 6L policy doc — `endpointDesign` (`POST /api/webhooks/razorpay/test/`, `phase6LRegistration=False`, `phase6MRegistration=True`), `signatureVerificationDesign` (HMAC-SHA256 over raw body via `X-Razorpay-Signature`, constant-time compare, reject on missing header, reject on empty secret), `idempotencyDesign` (`x_razorpay_event_id` primary key + `sha256(rawBody)` fallback, unique constraint, `ignore_with_audit_log; never re-mutate`), `eventAllowlist` (9 events: order.paid, order.notification.delivered/failed, payment.authorized/captured/failed, refund.created/processed/failed), `eventDenylist` (9 events: subscription.*, settlement, virtual_account, qr_code, fund_account, payout.*), `replayProtection` (300s window, reject older), `auditLoggingPlan` (8 future audit kinds, `phase6LAuditMutationAllowed=False`, `storeRawBody=False`, `storePayloadHash=True`, `storePayloadKeysOnly=True`, sensitive-keys-to-scrub list of 13 entries — card / vpa / upi / bank_account / wallet / email / contact / customer_id / customer / phone / mobile / address), `testModeOnlyValidationPlan` (Phase 6M env flag pattern `PHASE6M_RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_TEST_MODE_ENABLED`, default OFF, max 50 events per run, `phase6MEventCanMutateBusinessTables=False`), and a strict `businessMutationPolicy` with every Phase 6L flag `False` (`phase6LAllowOrderUpdate=False`, `phase6LAllowPaymentUpdate=False`, `phase6LAllowShipmentUpdate=False`, `phase6LAllowDiscountOfferUpdate=False`, `phase6LAllowCustomerNotification=False`). Three new strictly-read-only management commands: `inspect_razorpay_test_execution_audit --execution-id <ID> --json`, `inspect_razorpay_webhook_readiness --json`, `plan_razorpay_webhook_readiness --json`. Three new auth-required DRF endpoints under `/api/v1/saas/razorpay/`: `GET /execution-audit/?execution_id=<ID>`, `GET /webhook-readiness/`, `GET /webhook-plan/` — POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405. Frontend `/saas-admin` gains a new **"Razorpay Test Execution Audit + Webhook Readiness"** section: Phase 6K execution audit card (executionId / providerObjectId / status / rollbackStatus / 10 invariant rows green when locked / audit-event count / `rawSecretLeakDetected` flag), Webhook readiness sub-card (key mode / masked key id / webhook secret presence / latest succeeded provider object id / `safeToPlanWebhookReadiness` badge), Webhook plan sub-card (endpoint / signature / replay window / idempotency key / allowlist + denylist tables / mutation policy + sensitive-keys-scrubbed counters). New api methods (`getSaasRazorpayExecutionAudit`, `getSaasRazorpayWebhookReadiness`, `getSaasRazorpayWebhookPlan`) with deterministic mock fallback. **No "Execute Razorpay" / "Register Webhook" / "Capture" / "Send WhatsApp" / "Go Live" buttons exist anywhere on the UI.** **Hard rules preserved**: Phase 6L NEVER calls Razorpay; NEVER creates a payment link; NEVER captures a payment; NEVER sends a WhatsApp; NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Shipment` / `DiscountOfferLog` / `Customer` / `Lead`; NEVER returns the raw `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` / `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET` / `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` value; NEVER returns the raw provider response (only the whitelisted `{id, status, amount, currency, receipt}` summary). The audit review FAILs on any flipped safety boolean, missing rollback, missing provider object id, or raw-secret leak in any linked AuditEvent. `PHASE6K_RAZORPAY_TEST_EXECUTION_ENABLED` stays `false`; `WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`; broad-automation flags remain LOCKED OFF; campaigns / broadcast remain LOCKED. **33 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; 1198 backend + 44 frontend, all green.** Recommended next phase: **Phase 6M — Razorpay Webhook Handler Implementation (test-mode)** — implement the receiver designed in this Phase 6L plan, gated by `PHASE6M_RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_TEST_MODE_ENABLED=true` env flag, default OFF.

- **Phase 6I - Single Internal Live Gate Simulation shipped:** Phase 6H Controlled Runtime Live Audit Gate is **FULL PASS**, and Phase 6I adds the simulation-only rehearsal layer for one internal live-gate flow without enabling live execution. New `RuntimeLiveGateSimulation` records prepared/requested/approved/rejected/simulated/rolled-back simulation state, linked `RuntimeLiveExecutionRequest`, safe payload summary, payload hash, blockers/warnings, gate decision, and explicit safety invariants (`dryRun=true`, `liveExecutionAllowed=false`, `externalCallWillBeMade=false`, `externalCallWasMade=false`, `providerCallAttempted=false`). New `apps.saas.live_gate_simulation` supports only three operations: `razorpay.create_order` (default), `whatsapp.send_text`, and `ai.smoke_test`; every operation delegates to the Phase 6H gate and never calls WhatsApp, Razorpay, PayU, Delhivery, Vapi, NVIDIA, OpenAI, or any provider side-effect endpoint. New commands: `prepare_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`, `request_single_internal_live_gate_approval`, `approve_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`, `reject_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`, `run_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`, `rollback_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`, and `inspect_single_internal_live_gate_simulation`. New admin/auth API surface: `/api/v1/saas/runtime-live-gate/simulations/`, `/simulations/{id}/`, `/simulations/prepare/`, `/simulations/{id}/request-approval/`, `/approve/`, `/reject/`, `/run/`, and `/rollback/`; responses never return raw payloads, raw secrets, full phone numbers, or real customer data. Frontend `/saas-admin` gains **"Single Internal Live Gate Simulation"** with default operation, allowed operations, kill-switch state, simulation table, and safety flags; no provider-execution controls. Global kill switch remains active, runtime stays `env_config`, per-org runtime remains disabled, WhatsApp flags remain untouched/off, campaigns/broadcast/lifecycle/call/rescue/RTO/reorder remain locked/off, and global tenant middleware remains off. Next phase: controlled live readiness review only after an explicit operator gate.
- **Phase 6H - Controlled Runtime Routing Live Audit Gate shipped:** Phase 6G Controlled Runtime Routing Dry Run + AI Provider Routing is **FULL PASS**. Phase 6H adds the central audit/approval/kill-switch gate that every future external side effect must pass before live execution: WhatsApp text/template sends, Razorpay order/payment-link creation, PayU payment creation, Delhivery shipment creation, Vapi outbound calls, customer-facing AI outputs, and future provider-side execution. New `apps.saas.live_gate_policy` is the source of truth for live-gate operations (`whatsapp.send_text`, `whatsapp.send_template`, `razorpay.create_order`, `razorpay.create_payment_link`, `payu.create_payment`, `delhivery.create_shipment`, `vapi.place_call`, `ai.customer_hinglish_chat`, `ai.caio_compliance`, `ai.ceo_planning`, `ai.reports_summary`, `ai.critical_fallback`, `ai.smoke_test`): every Phase 6H policy has `liveAllowedByDefault=false`, `approvalRequired=true`, `idempotencyRequired=true`, `auditRequired=true`, `killSwitchCanBlock=true`, and `allowedInPhase6H=false`. New models `RuntimeLiveGatePolicySnapshot`, `RuntimeLiveExecutionRequest`, and `RuntimeKillSwitch` record policy snapshots, sanitized live-request decisions, approval/rejection state, blockers/warnings, safe payload summaries, payload hashes, and kill-switch state; the default global kill switch is seeded enabled. New `apps.saas.live_gate` evaluates requests without provider calls and always preserves the Phase 6G invariants in Phase 6H: `runtimeSource="env_config"`, `perOrgRuntimeEnabled=false`, `dryRun=true`, `liveExecutionAllowed=false`, `externalCallWillBeMade=false`. Approval in Phase 6H is audit-only and does **not** execute external calls. New commands: `inspect_runtime_live_audit_gate`, `preview_live_gate_decision`, `request_live_execution_approval`, `approve_live_execution_request`, `reject_live_execution_request`, `enable_runtime_kill_switch`, `disable_runtime_kill_switch`. New admin/auth API surface: `/api/v1/saas/runtime-live-gate/`, `/requests/`, `/policies/`, `/kill-switch/`, `/preview/`, `/requests/{id}/approve/`, `/requests/{id}/reject/`; responses never return raw payloads, raw secrets, or full phone numbers. Frontend `/saas-admin` gains **"Controlled Runtime Live Audit Gate"** with global live safety state, operation gate table, approval queue, recent gate audit events, and warnings; no provider execution buttons. **No real WhatsApp/payment/shipment/call/provider side effect occurs in Phase 6H.** WhatsApp flags remain untouched/off, campaigns/broadcast/lifecycle/call/rescue/RTO/reorder remain locked/off, global tenant middleware remains off, and runtime providers still use env/config. **16 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; 1087 backend + 36 frontend, all green.**
- **Phase 6G — Controlled Runtime Routing Dry Run + AI Provider Routing shipped:** Phase 6F per-org runtime routing preview is **FULL PASS**, and Phase 6G adds the **operation-level dry-run engine + NVIDIA-primary AI routing preview**. Live runtime is unchanged: every provider still reads from env / config; this phase only previews per-operation routing for 14 concrete operations (`whatsapp.send_text`, `whatsapp.send_template`, `razorpay.create_order`, `razorpay.create_payment_link`, `payu.create_payment`, `delhivery.create_shipment`, `vapi.place_call`, `openai.agent_completion`, `ai.reports_summary`, `ai.ceo_planning`, `ai.caio_compliance`, `ai.customer_hinglish_chat`, `ai.critical_fallback`, `ai.smoke_test`). New `apps.saas.runtime_operations` is the single source of truth — every operation declares `dryRunAllowed=True` + `liveAllowedInPhase6G=False`, plus its `provider_type`, `side_effect_risk`, `required_secret_refs`, `required_env_keys`, `required_config_keys`, `readiness_notes`, and `next_phase_for_live_execution`. New `apps.saas.ai_runtime_preview` ships the AI task routing table — six logical tasks (`reports_summaries → minimaxai/minimax-m2.7`, `ceo_planning → moonshotai/kimi-k2.6`, `caio_compliance / critical_fallback → mistralai/mistral-medium-3.5-128b`, `hinglish_customer_chat / smoke_test → google/gemma-4-31b-it`) with NVIDIA primary + OpenAI fallback (Anthropic Claude as secondary fallback when configured). Task-wise `max_tokens` resolved from env (`AI_MAX_TOKENS_REPORTS=3000`, `AI_MAX_TOKENS_CEO=2048`, `AI_MAX_TOKENS_COMPLIANCE=1024`, `AI_MAX_TOKENS_CUSTOMER_CHAT=512`, `AI_MAX_TOKENS_SMOKE=32`) with a typed default fallback. New `apps.saas.runtime_dry_run` composes operation taxonomy + Phase 6F provider preview + AI route preview into a single dry-run decision shape — every output declares `runtimeSource="env_config"`, `perOrgRuntimeEnabled=False`, `dryRun=True`, `liveExecutionAllowed=False`, `externalCallWillBeMade=False` (asserted by `validate_dry_run_has_no_side_effects`). Three new strictly-read-only management commands: `python manage.py inspect_controlled_runtime_routing_dry_run [--operation <type>] [--include-ai] [--organization-code <code>] [--json]` (full registry walk), `python manage.py inspect_ai_provider_routing [--json]` (AI runtime + per-task preview), `python manage.py preview_runtime_operation --operation <type> [--organization-code <code>] [--json]` (single-op preview). One operator-only `python manage.py smoke_test_ai_provider_routes [--task <name|all>] [--provider primary|fallback] [--prompt "Reply only OK"] [--timeout 12.0] [--json]` is the ONLY runtime path in Phase 6G that may issue a live NVIDIA / OpenAI request — default prompt `"Reply only OK"` (never customer data), API keys NEVER logged or returned, response truncated to 120 chars, failures captured as audit rows (`ai.provider_smoke_test.completed` / `.failed`) and never crash the server. Three new admin-only DRF endpoints: `GET /api/v1/saas/runtime-dry-run/[?operation=<type>&include_ai=true]`, `GET /api/v1/saas/ai-provider-routing/`, `GET /api/v1/saas/controlled-runtime-readiness/` — POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405; auth required (admin / director / superuser only); response carries no raw secrets and no full phone numbers. Frontend `/saas-admin` gains two new sections — **"Controlled Runtime Routing Dry Run"** (status badge + 14-row operation table with operation type, provider, side-effect risk, setting status, dry-run/live booleans, next-action; warning banner reminds the operator that PayU + Delhivery are deferred and Vapi awaits `phone_number_id` + `webhook_secret`) and **"AI Provider Routing Preview"** (NVIDIA / OpenAI / Anthropic fallback summary, per-task primary / fallback / max-tokens / safety-wrappers-required pill / next-action). Six new TypeScript types (`SaasRuntimeOperationDefinition`, `SaasAiProviderRoutePreview`, `SaasAiProviderRoutingPreview`, `SaasRuntimeDryRunOperationDecision`, `SaasRuntimeDryRunReport`, `SaasControlledRuntimeReadiness`); three new api methods (`getSaasRuntimeDryRun`, `getSaasAiProviderRouting`, `getSaasControlledRuntimeReadiness`) with deterministic mock fallback so the dashboard renders without a backend. **No "Run live" / "Execute" / "Go live" / "Send" buttons anywhere on the UI.** **LOCKED in Phase 6G**: every operation reports `runtimeSource="env_config"`, `perOrgRuntimeEnabled=False`, `liveExecutionAllowed=False`, `externalCallWillBeMade=False`, `dryRun=True` (asserted in tests); raw NVIDIA / OpenAI / Anthropic / Razorpay / Meta / Vapi keys NEVER appear in any preview / command / endpoint output (asserted with fake env values); WhatsApp env flags untouched (`WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`, limited test mode on); broad-automation flags remain LOCKED OFF; campaigns / broadcast remain LOCKED; Postzyo / OpenClaw untouched. PayU + Delhivery surface as deferred-provider warnings (not blockers) so Phase 6H can open without those credentials. Vapi missing `phone_number_id` / `webhook_secret` env vars surface as warning. **56 new backend tests + 2 new frontend tests; 1071 backend + 34 frontend, all green.** Phase 5F (broadcast campaigns) remains LOCKED.
- **Phase 6F — Per-Org Runtime Integration Routing Plan shipped:** Phase 6E SaaS admin + integration-settings foundation is **FULL PASS**, and Phase 6F adds the safe **read-only resolver + preview surface** for future per-org runtime routing. The live runtime is unchanged: every provider still reads from env / config; this phase only previews how a per-org runtime would look without flipping any provider over. **New `apps.saas.integration_runtime` module** ships the resolver vocabulary every Phase 6G+ caller will reach for. **Secret-ref helpers** (`mask_secret_ref`, `validate_secret_ref_format`, `get_secret_ref_status` / `resolve_secret_ref_preview`) accept `ENV:KEY` and `VAULT:path` strings, validate format, and return a structured status — for ENV: refs they probe `os.environ` PRESENCE only (boolean) and never return the raw value; for VAULT: refs they return `planned/not_configured` because Phase 6F does not contact a real vault. Masked-ref shape mirrors the Phase 6E `mask_secret_refs` helper so the frontend can render either source the same way. **Per-provider preview** (`get_provider_runtime_preview`) composes: `integrationSettingExists`, `settingStatus`, `runtimeSource="env_config"` (always), `perOrgRuntimeEnabled=False` (always), `secretRefsPresent` boolean, per-ref `secretRefsResolvablePreview.perRef` map (each entry: `{valid, source, maskedRef, present, canResolveAtRuntime, reason}`), `missingSecretRefs` list (vs the Phase 6E `EXPECTED_SECRET_REFS` table), `configPresent`, `envKeyStatus` map (which env vars the live runtime expects + whether they're set), masked `setting` payload (or null), `blockers`, `warnings`, and a typed `nextAction` ∈ {`keep_runtime_env_config`, `configure_org_integration_settings_before_runtime_routing`, `ready_for_phase_6g_controlled_runtime_routing_dry_run`}. **Combined preview** (`get_all_provider_runtime_previews`) runs the per-provider preview for all six providers (WhatsApp Meta, Razorpay, PayU, Delhivery, Vapi, OpenAI) and derives a `safeToStartPhase6G` boolean (true only when every provider has a setting AND every expected secret ref is present). **New management command** `python manage.py inspect_runtime_integration_routing [--organization-code <code>] [--json]` — strictly read-only diagnostic, no external provider calls, no raw secrets. **New read-only DRF endpoint** `GET /api/v1/saas/runtime-routing-readiness/` — admin-only (`AdminSaasPermission`), POST/PATCH/DELETE return 405; same shape as the CLI. **New idempotent seeder** `python manage.py seed_default_org_integration_refs [--dry-run\|--apply] [--organization-code <code>] [--json]` creates / updates `OrganizationIntegrationSetting` rows for the default org with ENV: secret refs ONLY (`access_token → ENV:META_WA_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `key_secret → ENV:RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET`, etc.). Defaults to `--dry-run`; on `--apply` it writes secret_refs / config / status (status = `configured` when refs exist, `draft` otherwise; `is_active=False` always — runtime stays env/config) and emits one `saas.integration_refs.seeded` audit row per provider per run. NEVER stores or returns raw secret values; idempotent on a stable template (second run reports `unchanged`). **SaaS Admin Panel (`/saas-admin`)** gains a new **"Runtime Integration Routing Preview"** section: status badge ({Phase 6G ready / Phase 6G blocked} based on `global.safeToStartPhase6G`), key-value summary (Runtime source = "Env/config (active)" / Per-org runtime enabled = "false (Phase 6F)" / next action), six provider rows (Provider / Setting / Status / Secret refs / Resolvable / Runtime source columns, all rendered from backend-computed values; never any raw secrets), and a footer warning banner: **"Per-org runtime routing is not active. Runtime still uses env/config."** Three new TypeScript types (`SaasRuntimeRoutingSecretRefStatus`, `SaasRuntimeRoutingProviderPreview`, `SaasRuntimeRoutingReadiness`) + one new api method (`getSaasRuntimeRoutingReadiness`) with deterministic mock fallback. **No activation / enable / send / campaign / provider-flip buttons anywhere on the UI.** **LOCKED in Phase 6F**: `runtimeSource` is `env_config` for every provider (asserted in tests); `runtimeUsesPerOrgSettings` and `perOrgRuntimeEnabled` are `False` everywhere (asserted at the helper layer + endpoint layer + UI render); raw secrets NEVER leave the backend (asserted with a fake env-var value test that confirms the value never appears in the resolver / command / endpoint output / mock fixture); WhatsApp env flags untouched (`WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`, limited test mode on); broad-automation flags remain LOCKED OFF; campaigns / broadcast remain LOCKED; Postzyo / OpenClaw untouched. **24 new backend tests + 3 new frontend tests; 1015 backend + 32 frontend, all green.** Local readiness reports `safeToStartPhase6G=true` after `seed_default_org_integration_refs --apply` runs once; before seeding it reports `false` with a `configure_org_integration_settings_before_runtime_routing` next action. **Phase 5F (broadcast campaigns) remains LOCKED.** Existing logins, dashboards, and APIs continue unchanged.
- **Phase 6E — SaaS Admin + Per-Org Integration Settings Foundation shipped:** Phase 6D org-aware write assignment is now **FULL PASS** and Phase 6E hardens the safe SaaS control layer without changing single-tenant production behavior. New `OrganizationIntegrationSetting` stores per-organization provider readiness for `whatsapp_meta`, `razorpay`, `payu`, `delhivery`, `vapi`, `openai`, and `other` using non-sensitive `config` plus `ENV:` / `VAULT:` `secret_refs` only; raw secrets are rejected and APIs return masked refs/booleans only. Runtime providers still use the existing env/config values — DB-backed per-org provider routing is explicitly deferred to **Phase 6F Per-Org Runtime Integration Routing Plan**. New selectors/commands/API surfaces report SaaS admin overview, org scope readiness, write-path readiness, integration readiness, and safety locks. New read-only `/saas-admin` frontend route shows organization overview, org/write coverage, integration readiness, safety locks, and SaaS audit events with no send/enable/campaign/provider activation controls. `globalTenantFilteringEnabled` remains `false`, org/branch FKs remain nullable, WhatsApp flags remain untouched (`WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`, limited test mode on), campaigns/broadcast/lifecycle/call/rescue/RTO/reorder remain locked/off, and Postzyo/OpenClaw are untouched.
- **Phase 5F-Gate Approved Customer Pilot Readiness shipped:** the current live posture stays intentionally narrow for a tiny approved customer pilot: `WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`, `WHATSAPP_LIVE_META_LIMITED_TEST_MODE=true`, allow-list guard active, campaigns/broadcast locked, lifecycle/call handoff/rescue/RTO/reorder OFF, no unexpected non-allowed sends, and no Order/Payment/Shipment/Discount mutation. This phase adds read-only/prep tooling only. New `WhatsAppPilotCohortMember` references `crm.Customer` and stores masked phone/suffix plus status (`pending/approved/paused/removed`), consent verification, source, approver, daily cap, notes, and metadata. New `apps.whatsapp.pilot` owns `get_whatsapp_pilot_readiness_summary()` plus safe prepare/pause helpers; readiness requires customer existence, granted WhatsApp consent, approved member status, limited-mode phone allow-list membership, daily cap, and no recent safety issue. New commands: `inspect_whatsapp_customer_pilot --json` (read-only), `prepare_whatsapp_customer_pilot_member --phone +91... --name "..." --source approved_customer_pilot --json` (creates/reuses Customer + pilot member only, pending if consent is missing, audit `whatsapp.pilot.member_prepared`), and `pause_whatsapp_customer_pilot_member --phone +91... --reason "..." --json` (pauses only, audit `whatsapp.pilot.member_paused`). New read-only API surface: `/api/v1/whatsapp/monitoring/pilot/` and `/api/v1/whatsapp/monitoring/overview/` include masked pilot readiness. The `/whatsapp-monitoring` page now includes "Approved Customer Pilot Readiness" with counts, blockers, nextAction, masked member table, and daily caps; there are still no send/enable/approve/pause controls. The 4-hour soak was accelerated, not full-duration. Customer pilot requires explicit consent + approval. Campaign/broadcast remains locked. SaaS audit is intentionally lightweight: tenant/org/branch models, per-org feature flags, per-org WhatsApp settings, and audit org/branch context are Phase 7 gaps, not forced into this single-tenant release.
- **What it is:** A full-stack AI Business Operating System for an Ayurvedic medicine D2C company (Nirogidhara Private Limited).
- **Owner / Director:** Prarit Sidana — final authority for high-risk decisions.
- **Stack:** React 18 + Vite + TypeScript (frontend) ↔ Django 5 + DRF (backend), JWT auth, SQLite (dev) / Postgres (prod-ready).
- **Repo layout:** monorepo — `frontend/`, `backend/`, `docs/`.
- **Status today (Phase 1 + 2A + 2B + 2C + 2D + 2E + 3A + 3B + 3C + 3D + 3E + 4A + 4B + 4C + 4D + 4E + 5A-0 + 5A-1 + 5A + 5B + 5C + 5D + 5E + 5E-Hotfix + 5E-Hotfix-2 + 5E-Smoke done):** **16 Django apps** scaffolded (Phase 5A adds `apps.whatsapp`, Phase 5B adds the `WhatsAppInternalNote` model + 6 inbox endpoints inside it), the full Phase 1–4E surface from earlier sessions, plus the **Phase 5A WhatsApp Live Sender Foundation**: 8 new models (`WhatsAppConnection` / `Template` / `Consent` / `Conversation` / `Message` / `MessageAttachment` / `MessageStatusEvent` / `WebhookEvent` / `SendLog`), three providers (`mock` default for tests, `meta_cloud` Nirogidhara-built Graph client, `baileys_dev` dev-only stub that refuses to load when `DEBUG=False AND WHATSAPP_DEV_PROVIDER_ENABLED!=true`), service layer (`queue_template_message` + `send_queued_message`) gating consent + approved-template + Claim Vault + approval matrix + CAIO hard stop + idempotency, Celery `send_whatsapp_message` task with `autoretry_for=ProviderError, retry_backoff=True, retry_jitter=True, max_retries=5`, signed Meta webhook at `/api/webhooks/whatsapp/meta/` (HMAC-SHA256 + replay-window + provider-event-id idempotency + GET handshake), 9 read + 4 write API endpoints under `/api/whatsapp/`, `python manage.py sync_whatsapp_templates` command, frontend Settings → WABA section + read-only `/whatsapp-templates` page + sidebar entry. **Failed sends never mutate Order/Payment/Shipment.** Earlier-phase surface unchanged: Master Event Ledger via signals + explicit service writes, JWT auth + role-based permissions, order state machine, all four gateway integrations with three-mode (mock/test/live) adapters + HMAC-verified webhooks + idempotency, AgentRun foundation + 7 per-agent runtime services + Celery beat at 09:00 + 18:00 IST + provider fallback (OpenAI → Anthropic) + model-wise USD cost tracking + Phase 3D sandbox toggle + versioned prompts (rollback) + per-agent USD budget guards + Governance page + Phase 3E product catalog admin + discount policy (10/20% bands) + ₹499 fixed advance + reward/penalty scoring formula + approval matrix policy + Phase 4B reward/penalty engine (AI agents only, CEO AI net accountability) + Rewards page agent leaderboard + Phase 4C approval-matrix middleware enforcement + Phase 4D Approved Action Execution Layer + Phase 4A Django Channels live `/ws/audit/events/` WebSocket + Phase 4E execution registry expansion (discount.up_to_10 / discount.11_to_20 / ai.sandbox.disable). Phase 5B added the **Inbound WhatsApp Inbox + Customer 360 Timeline**: new `WhatsAppInternalNote` model, six new endpoints (`GET /api/whatsapp/inbox/`, `PATCH /api/whatsapp/conversations/{id}/`, `POST /api/whatsapp/conversations/{id}/{mark-read,send-template}/`, `GET + POST /api/whatsapp/conversations/{id}/notes/`, `GET /api/whatsapp/customers/{customer_id}/timeline/`), six new audit kinds (`whatsapp.conversation.opened/updated/assigned/read`, `whatsapp.internal_note.created`, `whatsapp.template.manual_send_requested`), conversation list filters (`unread=true / assignedTo / q`), conversation serializer extended with `customerName / customerPhone / assignedToUsername` + message serializer with `templateName`. Per-conversation send-template routes through Phase 5A's `queue_template_message`, so consent + approved-template + Claim Vault + approval matrix + CAIO + idempotency gates all stay in force. Frontend wired with automatic mock fallback (21 pages now — WhatsApp Inbox + WhatsApp Templates). The new `/whatsapp-inbox` page is a three-pane layout (filters / conversation list / thread + internal notes + manual template send modal + AI-suggestions-disabled placeholder) with live refresh via Phase 4A `connectAuditEvents` filtered on `whatsapp.*`. Customer 360 grew a WhatsApp tab listing the customer's WhatsApp timeline + AI-disabled placeholder + a one-click link to the inbox. **434 backend tests + 13 frontend tests** all green.
- **Phase 5C — WhatsApp AI Chat Sales Agent shipped:** `apps.whatsapp.ai_orchestration.run_whatsapp_ai_agent` runs on every inbound (Celery `run_whatsapp_ai_agent_for_conversation`); deterministic Hindi/Hinglish/English language detection (`apps.whatsapp.language`); locked Hindi greeting via approved UTILITY template; OpenAI dispatch through `apps.integrations.ai.dispatch`; strict JSON schema validator (`apps.whatsapp.ai_schema`) with blocked-phrase filter; Claim Vault grounding; discount discipline (`apps.whatsapp.discount_policy`) — never upfront, 2–3 push minimum, 50% total cap; order booking from chat via `apps.orders.services.create_order` + ₹499 advance link via `apps.payments.services.create_payment_link`; auto-send rate gates (env-driven). Six new endpoints under `/api/whatsapp/ai/...` and per-conversation `ai-mode / run-ai / ai-runs / handoff / resume-ai`. 18 new audit kinds. Frontend AI Chat Agent panel inside the inbox (mode toggle / language + category / confidence / Run AI / Handoff / Resume) + AI Auto badge + Customer 360 status. **Auto-reply defaults to OFF** (`WHATSAPP_AI_AUTO_REPLY_ENABLED=false`); production flips it true after verification. **35 new tests; 469 backend + 13 frontend, all green.** Hard stops still in force: no medical-emergency replies, no freeform claims, no CAIO send, no shipment from chat.
- **Production deployment scaffold added (Phase 5B-Deploy):** `docker-compose.prod.yml` (six isolated containers — `nirogidhara-db` Postgres + `nirogidhara-redis` + `nirogidhara-backend` Daphne ASGI + `nirogidhara-worker` Celery + `nirogidhara-beat` + `nirogidhara-nginx` serving the Vite SPA), `backend/Dockerfile` + `frontend/Dockerfile`, `deploy/nginx/nirogidhara.conf` (proxies `/api/`, `/admin/`, `/ws/` with WebSocket upgrade), `.env.production.example`, full deploy runbook at `docs/DEPLOYMENT_VPS.md`. Target domain `ai.nirogidhara.com`, host port `18020:80` to avoid colliding with Postzyo / OpenClaw on the same VPS. All `*_MODE` env vars default to `mock` so the first deploy never sends a live message. CSRF_TRUSTED_ORIGINS now env-driven.
- **Phase 5D — Chat-to-Call Handoff + Lifecycle Automation shipped:** new `apps.whatsapp.call_handoff.trigger_vapi_call_from_whatsapp` is the SINGLE entry that may dial Vapi from a WhatsApp conversation; routes through the existing `apps.calls.services.trigger_call_for_lead` Vapi service (never the adapter directly). Idempotent on `(conversation, inbound_message, reason)` via the new `WhatsAppHandoffToCall` model. Safety reasons (medical_emergency / side_effect_complaint / legal_threat / refund_threat) record a `skipped` row for human/doctor pickup — never auto-dial a sales call. Phase 5C's AI orchestrator now opportunistically triggers Vapi for safe handoff reasons (`customer_requested_call`, `low_confidence_repeated`, `ai_handoff_requested`) when `WHATSAPP_CALL_HANDOFF_ENABLED=true`. Operator manual trigger at `POST /api/whatsapp/conversations/{id}/handoff-to-call/` (operations+). AI-booked orders now move directly to the confirmation queue from chat — `book_order_from_decision` calls `apps.orders.services.move_to_confirmation` after order create; failure flips `confirmationMoveFailed=true` in conversation metadata but never loses the order. New `apps.whatsapp.lifecycle.queue_lifecycle_message` + `apps.whatsapp.signals` listen on Order/Payment/Shipment `post_save` and dispatch approved templates (`whatsapp.confirmation_reminder`, `whatsapp.payment_reminder`, `whatsapp.delivery_reminder`, `whatsapp.usage_explanation`, `whatsapp.rto_rescue`) through Phase 5A's `queue_template_message`; idempotent on `lifecycle:{action}:{type}:{id}:{event}`; `usage_explanation` fails closed when Phase 5D Claim Vault coverage shows `missing` / `weak`. New Claim Vault coverage audit: `apps.compliance.coverage` + `python manage.py check_claim_vault_coverage` (exits 1 on missing) + admin-only `GET /api/compliance/claim-coverage/`. Three new endpoints (handoff-to-call, handoffs list, lifecycle-events list), eleven new audit kinds (`whatsapp.handoff.*` + `whatsapp.lifecycle.*` + `whatsapp.ai.order_moved_to_confirmation` + `compliance.claim_coverage.checked`), four new env vars (all default safe — handoff/lifecycle OFF, limited-test-mode ON). Frontend adds a "Call customer" button to the AI Chat panel + `WhatsAppHandoffToCall` / `WhatsAppLifecycleEvent` / `ClaimVaultCoverageReport` types + four new `api` methods. Hard stops still in force: no shipment from chat, no campaigns, no refunds, no ad-budget execution. **29 new backend tests; 498 backend + 13 frontend, all green.**
- **Phase 5E — Rescue Discount + Day-20 Reorder + Default Claims shipped:** new `apps.orders.rescue_discount` is the single source of truth for AI rescue discount math — locked **50% absolute cumulative cap**, per-stage ladder (confirmation 5/10/15, delivery 5/10, RTO 10/15/20 + high-risk step-up, reorder 5), conservative-first selection, automatic clamp to `cap_remaining`. Every offer attempt writes a `DiscountOfferLog` row (offered / accepted / rejected / blocked / skipped / `needs_ceo_review`). Anything above the 0–10 auto band, above the 50% cap, or with no headroom auto-mints an `ApprovalRequest` via two new matrix rows (`discount.rescue.ceo_review`, `discount.above_safe_auto_band`). Customer acceptance applies via `apps.orders.services.apply_order_discount` only; cap is re-validated at accept time, over-cap flips status to `needs_ceo_review` rather than mutating the order. Phase 5C orchestrator now also writes `DiscountOfferLog` rows when the WhatsApp AI proposes a discount, regardless of channel. New lifecycle triggers (`whatsapp.confirmation_rescue_discount`, `whatsapp.delivery_rescue_discount`, `whatsapp.rto_rescue_discount`, `whatsapp.reorder_day20_reminder`) flow through Phase 5A's `queue_template_message`; consent + Claim Vault + matrix + CAIO + idempotency unchanged. Day-20 reorder cadence: `apps.whatsapp.reorder.run_day20_reorder_sweep` covers delivered orders 20–27 days old via `python manage.py run_reorder_day20_sweep` + Celery `run_reorder_day20_sweep_task`. Default Claim Vault seed: `python manage.py seed_default_claims [--reset-demo] [--json]` (idempotent, demo-only) covers eight current categories with conservative non-cure / non-guarantee phrases; demo rows are flagged `version="demo-v1"` and surface in coverage as `risk=demo_ok`. **Defaults stay SAFE** — `WHATSAPP_RESCUE_DISCOUNT_ENABLED=false`, `WHATSAPP_RTO_RESCUE_DISCOUNT_ENABLED=false`, `WHATSAPP_REORDER_DAY20_ENABLED=false`, `DEFAULT_CLAIMS_SEED_DEMO_ONLY=true`. Five new endpoints. Twelve new audit kinds. Frontend adds a Rescue Discount cap card on the AI panel + six new TS types + six new `api` methods. **38 new backend tests; 536 backend + 13 frontend, all green.** Hard stops: never above 50% cumulative; never proactive on reorder Day 20; CAIO never originates an offer; no shipment from chat / no campaigns / no refunds / no ad-budget execution.
