### Phase 15L — Safety Diagnostics Manual Refresh Button (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15I added the Safety Diagnostics mini panel; Phase 15J added the "View details" drawer; Phase 15K cleaned up the session-expiry UX. Phase 15L adds a small **read-only "Refresh status" button** so the Director can manually re-fetch the existing three safety GET endpoints (kill-switch + sandbox + briefing-status) without reloading the whole page. **Phase 15L is intended as the last small Safety Diagnostics UX polish before the Phase 15M release freeze.**
- **Backend (Phase 15L): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit.
- **Provider changes** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx):
  - New exported `SafetyRefreshSource = "initial_load" | "audit_event" | "manual_refresh" | "unknown"` union.
  - `fetchAll` now accepts an optional `source: SafetyRefreshSource = "initial_load"` argument and writes it to a new `lastRefreshSource` state slot just before `setLoading(false)`.
  - The Phase 15G debounced audit-event refresh path now calls `fetchAll("audit_event")`.
  - New `refreshing: boolean` state slot + new `inFlightManualRefresh = useRef<Promise<void> | null>` so concurrent operator clicks coalesce into the same in-flight promise.
  - New `refreshSafetyState()` callback wrapping `fetchAll("manual_refresh")` with `setRefreshing(true)` on entry, `setLastSafetyRefreshAt(new Date().toISOString())` before the fetch, and `setRefreshing(false)` in the `finally` clause. Idempotent under concurrent clicks. NEVER calls a POST/PATCH/DELETE — `fetchAll` only invokes the three Phase 14D/14E/15B GET endpoints already on the provider.
  - Three new `SafetyStateValue` fields: `lastRefreshSource`, `refreshing`, `refreshSafetyState`. Inert hook-outside-provider fallback exposes safe defaults (`"unknown"` / `false` / no-op promise).
- **Panel changes** [`frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsPanel.tsx`](frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsPanel.tsx): new `<Button data-testid="safety-diagnostics-refresh-status">` in the panel header, immediately to the left of "View details". Uses lucide-react `RefreshCw` icon (animates `spin` while `refreshing=true`). Label flips from `Refresh status` → `Refreshing…` when in-flight. Button `disabled={refreshing}` and `aria-label` updates to match. Clicking calls `refreshSafetyState()` from the shared provider — no other side effects.
- **Drawer changes** [`frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsDetailModal.tsx`](frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsDetailModal.tsx): adds a matching `<Button data-testid="safety-diagnostics-detail-refresh">` in the modal footer (left of the existing `Close` button). Same icon, label, disabled, and accessible label semantics as the panel button. Plus: the modal's existing `Refresh source` row now reads `lastRefreshSource` from the provider directly (falling back to the Phase 15J timestamp heuristic only when the provider has no source yet — i.e. the inert hook-outside-provider snapshot). `refreshSourceLabel` extended with `"Manual refresh"` for the new case.
- **Endpoints re-fetched on manual refresh:**
  - `GET /api/v1/saas/runtime-live-gate/kill-switch/` (Phase 14D)
  - `GET /api/ai/sandbox/status/` (Phase 14E)
  - `GET /api/v1/ceo-orchestration/snapshots/sidebar-status/` (Phase 15B)
  All three already owned by the provider's `fetchAll`; Phase 15L re-uses them verbatim. No new backend endpoint added.
- **Loading / disabled behavior.** Click → button immediately renders `disabled` + `Refreshing…` label + spinning icon. Concurrent rapid clicks while the previous wave is in flight return the same promise (verified by the "5 rapid clicks coalesce to 1 GET wave" test). When the `Promise.allSettled` resolves the panel state slots update (last-refresh timestamp, endpoint health pills, refresh-source row) and the button returns to enabled `Refresh status` state. Errors during the manual refresh do NOT throw out of the click handler — `refreshSafetyState`'s `finally` always resets the in-flight ref + `refreshing=false`.
- **Session-expiry behavior during manual refresh.** A 401 from any of the three GETs uses the Phase 15K centralised path: `safeFetch` throws `AuthExpiredError`, dispatches `nirogidhara:auth-cleared`, and fires the deduped global "Session expired" sonner toast exactly once. The provider's three endpoint `*Error` flags flip to `true` for any rejection — including `AuthExpiredError` — so the Safety Diagnostics panel correctly renders `Error` pills for all three rows (verified by the dedicated test that mocks all three GETs to reject with an auth-shaped error and asserts every row says `Error` and **never** `OK`). Phase 15K's per-endpoint `console.error` suppression continues to apply — manual refresh on a 401 never spams the DevTools console.
- **Read-only / no-mutation guarantee.** Phase 15L NEVER calls POST/PATCH/DELETE (verified by asserting `api.postSaasRuntimeLiveGateKillSwitch.mock.calls.length === 0` + `api.postAiSandboxModeAction.mock.calls.length === 0` after click). NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes a new `AuditEvent` from the frontend, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. Both new buttons are `<Button>` instances inside `<Dialog>` / `<section>` regions that only call `refreshSafetyState` — the same GET-only function the provider already exposed.
- **Tests (Phase 15L):** **+10 net new frontend assertions** in [`frontend/src/test/safety_diagnostics_manual_refresh.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/safety_diagnostics_manual_refresh.test.tsx). Panel button (5 — renders next to View details; click re-fires the three GETs exactly once; never invokes a POST/PATCH/DELETE api method; disables + shows `Refreshing…` while in-flight; concurrent rapid clicks coalesce to one wave). Drawer button (4 — renders in footer; click re-fires GETs + flips `Refresh source` row to `Manual refresh`; never POSTs; sensitive-data grep over rendered DOM finds none of `Bearer`, `sk-proj-`, `+91`, `@example.com`, `system_policy`, `instruction_payload`, `raw_payload`, `raw_response`, `payment_url`, `META_WA_TOKEN`, `VAPI_API_KEY`, `Traceback`). Session-expiry (1 — rejected GETs flip all three endpoint statuses to `Error` and never claim `OK`; the provider's auth-aware `console.error` suppression keeps the DevTools clean). One Phase 15I regression assertion updated: the "NO buttons" allow-list test now permits both `safety-diagnostics-view-details` AND `safety-diagnostics-refresh-status` testids; every other `<button>` inside the panel is still forbidden. Full safety + auth cluster (16 files) smoke check passes **181 / 181 tests**.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **275 passed** (Phase 15K baseline 265 + 10 new Phase 15L tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15L status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. Same Topbar Safety Pill + Sync indicator, same Sidebar bottom indicator, same CEO badge, same Phase 15I diagnostics panel + Phase 15J detail drawer + Phase 15K session-expiry UX. Director gains exactly two new chrome elements — a "Refresh status" button in the panel header and another in the drawer footer — that re-fetch the existing three safety GETs on demand. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` and `SandboxState` rows are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.
- **Phase 15 release freeze recommendation:** Phase 15L is the last small Safety Diagnostics UX polish before Phase 15M. The shared `SafetyStateProvider` (Phase 15F) + WebSocket auto-refresh (15G) + sync health pill (15D/E/H) + diagnostics panel/drawer (15I/J) + session-expiry UX (15K) + manual refresh (15L) form a complete read-only safety chrome. Phase 15M should be the release freeze + Director sign-off pass — no further small Safety UI polish phases unless a production blocker or bug is observed.

### Phase 15K — Session Expiry UX Polish (SHIPPED)

- Pre-Phase-15K, when the backend returned HTTP 401 the chrome would surface per-widget technical toasts like *"Sandbox Mode: HTTP 401 — session expired or unauthenticated"* — one per failing safety endpoint (kill-switch + sandbox + briefing), plus matching `console.error` log lines. Phase 15K replaces that spam with **one clean global "Session expired" sonner toast** (deduped at the api layer) plus a passive `SessionExpiredBanner` on the `/login` page so the Director sees exactly why they landed there. The existing Phase 13A `auth-cleared` event + `RequireAuth` redirect to `/login` are preserved verbatim.
- **Backend (Phase 15K): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit. No backend auth policy was touched — the 401 contract from the backend is unchanged; only the frontend's reaction is polished.
- **API client changes** [`frontend/src/services/api.ts`](frontend/src/services/api.ts):
  - New exported `AuthExpiredError` class — subclass of `Error` with `isAuthError = true` + `httpStatus = 401` marker fields. Both `safeFetch` and `safeMutate` now throw this typed error on 401 instead of a raw `Error("HTTP 401 — session expired or unauthenticated")`.
  - New exported `isAuthError(err)` predicate (instanceof + duck-typed `.isAuthError === true`).
  - New module-level dedupe: `notifySessionExpiredOnce()` fires the global `sonner.toast.error("Session expired", { description: "Please sign in again to continue. Safety data may be stale until you sign in.", duration: 8000 })` exactly once per page load. Lazy `import("sonner")` so a non-browser test runner that never mounts the toast surface doesn't error.
  - Test-only `__resetSessionExpiredToastForTest()` helper to reset the dedupe guard between specs.
  - `safeFetch` and `safeMutate` 401 paths now ALL of: clear `localStorage["nirogidhara.jwt"]` (existing Phase 13A behaviour), dispatch `nirogidhara:auth-cleared` (existing), call `notifySessionExpiredOnce()` (new), throw `AuthExpiredError` (new typed). The dev-mode mock fallback in `safeFetch` is short-circuited for auth errors via `if (isAuthError(error)) throw error;` so a 401 never masquerades as a successful mock response. Same guard added to `safeMutate` (which previously fell back to optimistic mock on any error — a 401 now correctly throws instead).
- **Per-widget toast suppression** [`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx`](frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx): the three Settings handlers (`refreshKillSwitch`, `refreshSandbox`, `refreshPromptVersions`) now check `isAuthError(err)` and `return` silently before the existing `toast.error("AI Kill Switch: ${message}")` / `Sandbox Mode: ...` / `Rollback System: ...` calls. Result: no per-widget HTTP 401 toast spam — the single global "Session expired" toast wins. Uses an inline predicate function rather than importing `isAuthError` from `@/services/api` so existing test factories that mock `@/services/api` with only an `api` export continue to work without modification.
- **Provider console noise suppression** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx): the three `console.error("[SafetyStateProvider] kill-switch load failed:", ...)` / sandbox / briefing log sites are now gated behind `if (!isAuthError(...))`. State setters (`setKillSwitchError(true)`, `setBriefingError(classifyBriefingError(err))`, etc.) still fire unconditionally so the Safety Diagnostics panel (Phase 15I/15J) correctly shows `Error` for any failed endpoint after a 401 — it never falsely claims `OK`. Same inline-predicate strategy for mock-factory compatibility.
- **New SessionExpiredBanner** [`frontend/src/components/auth/SessionExpiredBanner.tsx`](frontend/src/components/auth/SessionExpiredBanner.tsx): rendered on `/login` between the page header and the email/password form. Pure presentational `<div role="status" aria-live="polite">` with the warning palette. Reads `useLocation().state.from` — renders ONLY when `RequireAuth` redirected the user with a `from` redirect state (so a direct first-time visit to `/login` shows a clean form with no banner). Body copy: `"Please sign in again to continue. Safety data may be stale until you sign in."` Never renders any technical content (no HTTP code, no error message, no token, no `Bearer`, no `Authorization` header value, no `Traceback`, no response body, no stack trace).
- **Sign-in action.** No new action button was added beyond the existing Login form. The user lands on `/login` via the `RequireAuth` redirect, sees the banner explaining why, and then uses the existing JWT login form to sign in. The next-path is preserved via `react-router`'s `state={{ from: location }}` (Phase 13A pattern); the existing Login handler routes the user back to that path on success.
- **Safety Diagnostics behavior during 401.** Phase 15I panel + Phase 15J detail drawer correctly render `Error` pills for the three endpoint rows on a 401 (because the provider's `*Error` flags flip to `true` for any rejection, including `AuthExpiredError`). The Phase 15I/15J helpers' `buildErrorSummary` then lists `Kill switch endpoint failed` / `Sandbox endpoint failed` / `Briefing endpoint failed` — short sanitised labels that match the existing operator runbook copy. The panel NEVER claims `OK` after a 401.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15K NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows from the frontend, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. The new typed `AuthExpiredError` carries the literal message `"Session expired or unauthenticated"` — no JWT, no `Authorization` header, no raw response body.
- **Tests (Phase 15K):** **+9 net new frontend assertions** in [`frontend/src/test/session_expiry_ux.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/session_expiry_ux.test.tsx). Helpers (2 — `AuthExpiredError` field shape, `isAuthError` recognises both `instanceof` and duck-typed shape, returns false for unrelated errors / null / undefined). `safeFetch` 401 contract (4 — throws `AuthExpiredError`, clears JWT, dispatches `auth-cleared`, fires exactly one global toast across 5 consecutive 401 calls, raw `"HTTP 401"` / `"unauthenticated"` never appears in the toast title or description, dev-mode mock fallback is short-circuited). `SessionExpiredBanner` (3 — renders only with `from` redirect state, renders nothing on direct `/login` visits, never renders `HTTP 401` / `unauthenticated` / `Bearer` / `Authorization` / `Traceback` / `stack` strings). One regression: the Phase 15I panel's "NO buttons" test already had the Phase 15J "View details" allow-list; no new regression assertions needed because the consumer-side `isAuthError` is now an inline function rather than a cross-module import. Existing 15-file safety + auth cluster smoke check passes **171 / 171 tests**.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **265 passed** (Phase 15J baseline 256 + 9 new Phase 15K tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15K status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. Same Topbar Safety Pill + Sync indicator, same Sidebar bottom indicator, same CEO badge, same Phase 15I diagnostics panel, same Phase 15J detail drawer. Director just stops seeing per-widget HTTP 401 toast spam when the JWT expires — replaced by one clean `Session expired` toast and a passive banner on `/login`. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` and `SandboxState` rows are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15J — Safety Diagnostics Detail Drawer (SHIPPED)

Reads the existing `useSafetyState()` snapshot — no new fetches, no new context fields. Opens a local-only `detailOpen` `useState` — no backend method touched. Renders `<SafetyDiagnosticsDetailModal open={detailOpen} onOpenChange={setDetailOpen}/>` at the end of the panel section.
- **Drawer contents (three sections + footer):**
  - **Safety sync** — Status pill (Live / Connecting / Reconnecting / Offline / Unavailable), Last audit event (locale timestamp or `No event seen yet`), Last safety refresh (locale timestamp or `Never`), Refresh source (`Initial load` / `Audit event` / `Unknown`), Reconnect attempts (`Not tracked` — the Phase 4A helper does its own backoff internally; counter exposure is left to a future phase if needed).

Never renders raw `Error:` strings, `Traceback`, HTTP status codes, or response bodies.

### Phase 15I — Safety Diagnostics Mini Panel (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15H added a compact Topbar Safety Sync indicator surfacing the audit-event WebSocket lifecycle. Phase 15I adds a complementary read-only **Safety Diagnostics Mini Panel** on the Settings & Control page so the Director can see, at a glance, both the sync lifecycle AND per-endpoint health (kill-switch / sandbox / briefing) plus the last-event / last-refresh timestamps. Frontend-only, no new fetches — the panel consumes the shared `SafetyStateProvider` (Phase 15F) and the new derived `*Status` fields exposed in this phase.
- **Backend (Phase 15I): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit.
- **Provider changes** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx):
  - New exported type `SafetyEndpointStatus = "loading" | "ok" | "error"`.
  - New pure exported helper `deriveEndpointStatus(hasSnapshot, errored): SafetyEndpointStatus` (error > snapshot > loading cascade).
  - Three new `SafetyStateValue` fields: `killSwitchStatus`, `sandboxStatus`, `briefingStatus`. All three are memoised derivations of the existing snapshot + `*Error` flags — no new fetches issued.
  - Inert hook-outside-provider fallback returns `"loading"` for all three so unrelated component tests render the panel safely without provider setup.
- **New component** [`frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsPanel.tsx`](frontend/src/components/settings/SafetyDiagnosticsPanel.tsx): renders a single `<section data-testid="safety-diagnostics-panel">` with six diagnostics rows. Pure presentational — no fetches of its own, no state, no click handlers. Uses the existing `StatusPill` primitive for OK / Loading / Error / Live / Connecting / Reconnecting / Offline / Unavailable badges. Internal `formatTimestamp` falls back to `"Never"` / `"No event seen yet"` for null ISO timestamps.
- **Settings integration** [`frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx`](frontend/src/pages/Settings.tsx): the panel renders between the existing Rollback History modal block and the AI Action Approval Matrix surface card. Imports the new component; no other Settings logic touched.
- **Final diagnostics rows and labels:**
  - **Safety sync** → `Live` / `Connecting` / `Reconnecting` / `Offline` / `Unavailable` (StatusPill tones: success / info / warning / warning / neutral).
  - **Last safety refresh** → `toLocaleString()` of the `lastSafetyRefreshAt` ISO timestamp, or `Never`.
  - **Last audit event** → `toLocaleString()` of `lastSafetyEventAt`, or `No event seen yet`.
  - **Kill switch endpoint** → `OK` / `Loading` / `Error` (success / info / warning).
  - **Sandbox endpoint** → same tri-state mapping.
  - **Briefing status endpoint** → same tri-state mapping.
- **Read-only / no-action guarantee.** Panel is `<section>` with no buttons, no anchors, no click handlers, no manual-refresh control. Asserted in tests by scanning the rendered DOM for `<button>` and `<a>` (both return null) and by string-grep against the forbidden action labels (`Resume AI`, `Activate kill`, `Enable sandbox`, `Disable sandbox`, `Apply rollback`, `Generate briefing`).
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15I NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows from the frontend, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. Panel rows surface only enum statuses + locale-formatted ISO timestamps.

Helpers (4 assertions — `syncVisual` covers all 5 sync states, `endpointVisual` covers OK/Loading/Error, `formatTimestamp` handles null / invalid / valid ISO, `deriveEndpointStatus` priority cascade). Panel render (8 — six rows render with correct testids; initial state is Connecting + Loading + Never / No event yet; after fetches settle every endpoint shows OK; kill-switch error renders Error while sandbox + briefing stay OK; sync flips to Live on `onStatusChange('live')`; flips through Reconnecting + Offline safely; no buttons / anchors / forbidden action labels; sensitive-data grep finds none of `Bearer `, `sk-proj-`, `+91`, `@example.com`, `system_policy`, `instruction_payload`, `raw_payload`, `raw_response`, `payment_url`, `META_WA_TOKEN`, `VAPI_API_KEY`). Existing Phase 15H sync-health tests, Phase 15G auto-refresh tests, Phase 15F provider tests, Phase 15D/15E pill tests, Phase 14D kill-switch tests, Phase 14E-Hotfix-1 sidebar tests, Phase 15B briefing badge tests, Phase 15C audit timeline tests, Phase 15A rollback history tests all unchanged and still pass.

### Phase 15H — Safety WebSocket Health Indicator (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15G connected the shared safety state to the existing Phase 4A `/ws/audit/events/` WebSocket, but the operator could not see whether the passive safety sync connection was healthy. Phase 15H adds a small read-only Safety Sync indicator in the Topbar that surfaces the WebSocket lifecycle: `connecting` / `live` / `reconnecting` / `offline` / `unavailable`.
- **Backend (Phase 15H): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit. Same Phase 4A WebSocket fan-out and the same three Phase 14D / 14E / 15B endpoints.
- **WebSocket helper reused (not extended):** [`frontend/src/services/realtime.ts`](frontend/src/services/realtime.ts) `connectAuditEvents({onEvent, onStatusChange, onError})` (Phase 4A). The `onStatusChange?: (status: RealtimeStatus) => void` callback was already part of the helper's public API; Phase 15H simply plugs into it from `SafetyStateProvider`. No helper changes required.
- **Provider changes** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx):
  - New exported type `SafetySyncStatus = "connecting" | "live" | "reconnecting" | "offline" | "unavailable"`. First four states are forwarded verbatim from Phase 4A `RealtimeStatus`; `"unavailable"` is a Phase 15H extension for the construction-failure path so the indicator renders a distinct neutral state.
  - New `SafetyStateValue` fields: `safetySyncStatus`, `lastSafetyEventAt`, `lastSafetyRefreshAt` (both timestamps ISO-string-or-null).
  - The Phase 15G `useEffect` now also wires `onStatusChange: (status) => setSafetySyncStatus(status)`, stamps `lastSafetyEventAt` immediately on every allow-listed event, and stamps `lastSafetyRefreshAt` inside the debounced refresh just before `fetchAll()` fires. Construction-failure `catch` sets status to `"unavailable"`. Effect-cleanup setter belt-and-braces sets status to `"offline"` so a React 18 StrictMode dev double-mount cannot leak a stale `"live"`.
  - Inert hook-outside-provider fallback returns `safetySyncStatus: "unavailable"` so isolated component tests render the indicator safely without setup.
- **Indicator helper** [`frontend/src/utils/safetySyncIndicator.ts`](frontend/src/utils/safetySyncIndicator.ts): pure `computeSafetySyncIndicator(status)` returns `{label, compactLabel, tone, className, tooltip, dataStatus}`. Tone mapping: `live → success`, `connecting → info`, `reconnecting → warning`, `offline → warning`, `unavailable → neutral`. Tooltip always ends with the literal phrase `Read-only indicator.` so the operator knows clicking does nothing.
- **UI placement** [`frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx): the indicator renders directly after the Phase 15D/15E Safety Pill and before the existing `Live` indicator. Visible at `md+` widths (same breakpoint convention as the Safety Pill). Renders as `<span role="status">` with `cursor-default` — no button, no anchor, no click handler. `shrink-0` so the Topbar `min-w-0` flex layout (Phase 15E) compresses other elements first. Full label `Sync: Live` at `xl+`, compact label `Live` below `xl`. lucide-react `Radio` icon (3.5px square, semantic for a passive stream indicator).
- **Final labels:**
  - xl+ widths: `Sync: Live` / `Sync: Connecting` / `Sync: Reconnecting` / `Sync: Offline` / `Sync: Unavailable`
  - md/lg widths: `Live` / `Connect` / `Reconnect` / `Offline` / `Unavail`
  - Tooltip / `aria-label` always carries the long-form sentence regardless of which label is visible.
- **Error / reconnect behavior.** Stream errors are non-fatal — Phase 15G's `console.warn` path is preserved. Reconnect is handled entirely by the existing Phase 4A helper (exponential backoff: 1s, 2s, 4s, 8s, 15s, 30s capped). Phase 15H NEVER adds polling or retry logic. If the WebSocket cannot open at all (e.g. `WebSocket` missing from the runtime), the construction-failure `catch` flips status to `"unavailable"` and the chrome keeps working on initial GETs.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15H NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows from the frontend, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. The indicator surfaces only the WebSocket lifecycle enum + two ISO timestamps the operator already has access to.
- **Tests (Phase 15H):** **+18 net new frontend assertions** in [`frontend/src/test/safety_sync_health.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/safety_sync_health.test.tsx). Pure helper (5 assertions — one per `SafetySyncStatus` mapping including the `"Read-only indicator."` tooltip suffix). Provider lifecycle (6 — initial `connecting`, `onStatusChange` flips through `live`/`reconnecting`/`offline`, construction-failure sets `unavailable`, matching event stamps both timestamps after debounce, non-matching event stamps nothing, unmount calls `controller.close()`). Topbar render (7 — testid + role=status + data-safety-sync-status on mount; updates to live; updates to reconnecting/offline; construction-failure renders unavailable neutral; read-only span with no button/anchor; full+compact labels with `hidden xl:inline` / `xl:hidden` classes; co-renders with Phase 15D Safety Pill). Existing Phase 15F provider tests, Phase 15G auto-refresh tests, Phase 15D/15E pill tests, Phase 15A/15B/15C feature tests all unchanged and still pass.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **228 passed** (Phase 15G baseline 210 + 18 new Phase 15H tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15H status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. Same labels, same colour palette, same priority cascade. Director gains a single new chrome element — the Safety Sync pill — that exposes whether the existing Phase 15G auto-refresh is actually live. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` and `SandboxState` rows are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15G — Safety State Auto-Refresh on Audit Events (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15F consolidated the three safety GETs (kill-switch / sandbox / sidebar-status) into a single `SafetyStateProvider`. Phase 15G connects that shared provider to the existing Phase 4A `/ws/audit/events/` WebSocket so the Topbar pill, Sidebar bottom indicator, and CEO Briefing badge automatically refresh when an allow-listed audit event lands — no polling, no extra HTTP traffic, no new backend.
- **Backend (Phase 15G): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit. Same Phase 4A WebSocket fan-out (`apps.audit.realtime.publish_audit_event` + `/ws/audit/events/` Channels route) and the same three Phase 14D / 14E / 15B endpoints.
- **Event stream reused:** [`frontend/src/services/realtime.ts`](frontend/src/services/realtime.ts) `connectAuditEvents({onEvent, onError})` (Phase 4A). The helper already wraps reconnect with exponential backoff and swallows all errors so a transient WebSocket failure never escapes to the UI.
- **Allow-list (3 prefixes, exact match by `event.kind.startsWith`):**
  - `runtime.kill_switch.` — Phase 6H audit kinds (`runtime.kill_switch.enabled` / `runtime.kill_switch.disabled`). Backs the Phase 14D Topbar AI Paused control + Phase 15D Topbar Safety Pill.
  - `ai.sandbox.` — Phase 3D audit kinds (`ai.sandbox.enabled` / `ai.sandbox.disabled`). Backs the Phase 14E Sandbox Mode UI + Phase 15D Topbar Safety Pill.
  - `ceo_orchestration.snapshot.` — Phase 9F audit kinds (e.g. `ceo_orchestration.snapshot.created`). Backs the Phase 15B Sidebar Director Briefing badge + Phase 15D Topbar Safety Pill briefing token.
  - **Anything else is ignored** — the live activity feed keeps flowing for other consumers (Audit Timeline page, Governance page) without driving any extra safety GETs.
- **Provider changes** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx): exports `SAFETY_REFRESH_EVENT_PREFIXES`, `SAFETY_REFRESH_DEBOUNCE_MS`, and a pure `shouldRefreshOnEvent(event)` helper. Adds a single `useEffect` that calls `connectAuditEvents({onEvent, onError})` once on provider mount. Matching events arm a single debounced `setTimeout(fetchAll, 750ms)`; subsequent matches inside the window cancel + re-arm the same timer so a burst of N events lands as exactly one `fetchAll()`. Unmount calls `controller.close()` AND clears the pending timer so a late event never fires after the provider tears down. Stream construction errors are caught and `console.warn`-ed without crashing the chrome — initial GETs still drive the first paint.
- **Debounce / coalescing.** `SAFETY_REFRESH_DEBOUNCE_MS = 750ms`. Verified in the new tests by dispatching 5 allow-listed events back-to-back and asserting exactly +1 call per endpoint (not +5).
- **Cleanup.** Unmount tears down the subscription via `controller.close()` (Phase 4A's `closedByCaller=true` path — no reconnect attempt) and clears the pending debounce timer. Verified in `test_unmount_calls_controller_close_and_cancels_pending_debounce` which dispatches a matching event, unmounts before the debounce elapses, advances fake timers past the window, and asserts no late `fetchAll()` fires.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15G NEVER calls a POST/PATCH/DELETE on any api method (event listener only invokes `safety.refresh()` which only reads). NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER creates an `AuditEvent` from the frontend (the WS is read-only — frames are consumed, never published), NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. The WS frame format the helper consumes (`{type:"audit.event", event:{id, kind, tone, icon, text, payload?}}`) is the same shape already sanitised by Phase 4A's `apps.audit.realtime.publish_audit_event`.
- **Docs sync (Docs Consistency Audit).** As part of Phase 15G the canonical doc set was audited for "current baseline" drift after Phase 14B through Phase 15F shipped. Findings + actions:
  - `nd.md` was current through Phase 15F — Phase 15G entry added above Phase 15F (this block). No historical content rewritten.
  - `docs/RUNBOOK.md` was current through Phase 15F (Topbar safety pill + responsive + shared-state subsections) — added a "Safety State Auto-Refresh on Audit Events (Phase 15G)" subsection.
  - `docs/BACKEND_API.md` has Phase 15A/15B/15C endpoint rows — no backend endpoint added in Phase 15F or 15G, so no change required.
  - `AGENTS.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `README.md` had stale "Current baseline" sections (Phase 12D / Phase 14A respectively). Each gained a one-paragraph **"Current operational baseline (post Phase 15G)"** note at the very top that defers to `nd.md` as the source of truth for current state while preserving the historical narrative below. No historical content was rewritten.
  - `docs/MASTER_BLUEPRINT_V2.md`, `docs/FUTURE_BACKEND_PLAN.md`, `docs/DEPLOYMENT_VPS.md`, `docs/FRONTEND_AUDIT.md`, `docs/WHATSAPP_INTEGRATION_PLAN.md`, and the Phase 7 plan documents (`docs/PHASE_7D_HOTFIX_1_PLAN.md`, `docs/PHASE_7E_PLAN.md`, `docs/PHASE_7F_PLAN.md`, `docs/PHASE_7G_PLAN.md`) are intentionally left as historical / strategic records — they do not claim to be the current operational baseline and `nd.md` already wins in the project convention (`§"Strategic mirror"` rule at the top of `nd.md`).
- **Tests (Phase 15G):** **+11 net new frontend assertions** in [`frontend/src/test/safety_auto_refresh.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/safety_auto_refresh.test.tsx). Helper allow-list (3 assertions): exports the documented 3-tuple of prefixes; matches kill-switch / sandbox / ceo_orchestration.snapshot kinds; never matches unrelated kinds, empty string, undefined `kind`, or `null`/`undefined` events. Subscription lifecycle (3): subscribes exactly once on mount, unmount calls `controller.close()` AND cancels pending debounce so no late refresh fires after fake timers advance past the window, stream construction error is swallowed without crashing. Refresh on matching events (5): kill-switch / sandbox / ceo_orchestration.snapshot each trigger exactly one refresh after debounce elapses; non-matching kinds (`order.created`, `whatsapp.message.sent`, `payment.received`) trigger zero refreshes even with the timer advanced 3× the debounce window; burst of 5 matching events inside the debounce window coalesces to exactly +1 call per endpoint (not +5). The realtime helper is mocked end-to-end so the test never opens a real WebSocket; the mock captures the `onEvent`/`onError` callbacks so the test drives the provider deterministically with fake timers (`toFake: ["setTimeout", "clearTimeout"]` only — microtasks stay real so the initial `Promise.allSettled` flushes correctly). Existing 10 Phase 15F provider tests still pass unchanged.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **210 passed** (Phase 15F baseline 199 + 11 new Phase 15G tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15G status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. Same labels, same colour palette, same priority cascade — but now when a Director hits the AI Kill Switch confirmation from any browser tab, every open tab's Topbar pill + Sidebar bottom + CEO badge updates automatically within ~750ms without a page refresh. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` and `SandboxState` rows are untouched; the only side effect of Phase 15G's listener is the existing `fetchAll()` GET wave. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15F — Shared Safety State Hook (SHIPPED)

- Phase 14D / 14E / 15B / 15D / 15E each independently fetched one or more of the three safety-related endpoints (`kill-switch`, `sandbox/status`, `ceo-orchestration/snapshots/sidebar-status`). After Phase 15D the chrome layout effectively issued **six GETs per AppLayout mount** (3 from the Topbar, 3 from the Sidebar) because both components owned their own state and ran identical effects. Phase 15F consolidates the reads into a single shared provider so Topbar + Sidebar consume one consistent snapshot — same labels, same logic, half the network noise.
- **Backend (Phase 15F): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit. Same three Phase 14D / 14E / 15B endpoints are still the source of truth.
- **Frontend (Phase 15F):**
  - **New context** [`frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx`](frontend/src/context/SafetyStateContext.tsx) exports `SafetyStateProvider` (the data owner) and `useSafetyState()` (the consumer hook). The provider runs `Promise.allSettled([killSwitch, sandbox, briefing])` once on mount, classifies briefing errors into Phase 15B's three buckets (`auth` / `permission` / `generic`), and memoises the computed `sidebar` (via `computeSafetyStatus`) and `topbar` (via `computeTopbarSafetySummary`) views so consumers get identical derived state without re-deriving. The hook returns an inert "loading" fallback when consumed outside the provider — no throw — so unrelated component tests don't have to set up the provider.
  - **AppLayout** [`frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx) wraps the whole shell in `<SafetyStateProvider>` so all chrome consumers see one snapshot.
  - **Topbar refactor** [`frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx) drops its own three `useState` blocks + two `useEffect`s and consumes `useSafetyState()` instead. `aiPaused` is still derived from `killSwitch.aiExecutionBlocked ?? .enabled`, the Safety Pill still renders via the helper-provided `topbar` view (full + compact labels intact), and the KillSwitchModal success callback now calls the provider's `setKillSwitch(next)` so the chrome updates without a refetch round-trip.
  - **Sidebar refactor** [`frontend/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx) drops its own three `useState` blocks + one `useEffect` and consumes `useSafetyState()` instead. The Phase 14E-Hotfix-1 priority cascade (`kill-switch paused → sandbox → all-normal`) and the Phase 15B briefing badge logic are unchanged — both are now driven by the same `sidebar` view + `briefing` + `briefingError` fields the provider exposes.
- **Deduplication outcome.** On a fresh AppLayout mount, the network panel now shows exactly one GET per safety endpoint (down from two). Verified via `tests/safety_state_provider.test.tsx` — `Promise.allSettled` is invoked once, each `api.getSaasRuntimeLiveGateKillSwitch / getAiSandboxModeStatus / getDirectorBriefingSidebarStatus` mock records exactly one call regardless of how many consumers mount inside the provider.
- **Loading / error behaviour preserved:**
  - Topbar pill: still shows `Safety: Checking…` while any fetch pending, never claims green on partial failure, shows `Safety: State unavailable` when all three errored. Full label at `xl+`, compact at `md/lg`.
  - Sidebar bottom indicator: still shows `AI paused by kill switch` (warning) when kill switch active, `Sandbox mode active` (info) when AI running + sandbox ON, `All systems normal` (success) when both green, `Safety state unavailable` (neutral) when any fetch errored.
  - CEO Briefing nav badge: still shows `READY` / `STALE` / `CRIT` / `—` / `auth` / `—` / `…` with the same colour palette Phase 15B introduced.
- **Refresh policy.** No polling, no `setInterval`. The provider exposes a `refresh()` callback so a future caller (Phase 15G WebSocket sweep, for instance) can re-fetch all three on demand. `setKillSwitch(next)` lets the KillSwitchModal optimistically update the snapshot without forcing a refetch.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15F NEVER calls a POST/PATCH/DELETE on any api method (`postSaasRuntimeLiveGateKillSwitch` / `postAiSandboxModeAction` are explicitly asserted `not.called` in the provider test), NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads.
- **Tests (Phase 15F):** **+10 net new frontend assertions** in [`frontend/src/test/safety_state_provider.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/safety_state_provider.test.tsx): one-fetch-per-endpoint contract, dual-consumer shared-snapshot contract, loading state, briefing error classification (auth / permission / generic), kill-switch-failure-doesn't-claim-normal, `refresh()` re-fetches each endpoint once, `setKillSwitch()` updates state without re-fetch, hook-outside-provider returns inert fallback (does NOT throw), read-only POST/PATCH/DELETE invariant. Existing tests touched: `topbar_safety_summary.test.tsx`, `safety_status.test.tsx`, `director_briefing_badge.test.tsx`, `kill_switch.test.tsx` — each render helper now wraps `<Topbar>` / `<Sidebar>` in `<SafetyStateProvider>` so the existing mocks drive the provider's fetches. No test logic changed; counts unchanged. Phase 15D / 15E topbar tests untouched. Phase 15C audit timeline + Phase 15A rollback history tests untouched.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **199 passed** (Phase 15E baseline 189 + 10 new Phase 15F tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15F status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. Same labels, same colour palette, same priority cascade, same tooltip text. Director sees exactly what they saw before — but the network tab now shows half the safety-related GETs per page mount, and any future safety chrome (badge / dashboard / sidebar widget) can subscribe to the same provider without spawning new fetches. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15E — Topbar Responsive Overflow Polish (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15D's Topbar Safety Compact Pill added a long `whitespace-nowrap` label (`Safety: AI Paused · Sandbox OFF · Briefing STALE`, ~46 chars) on top of the existing chrome (search input + OrgBadge + Live + Ask CEO AI + Bell + AI Paused + user profile + Logout). The combined intrinsic width pushed the Topbar past common desktop widths (1280–1366px), producing a body-level horizontal scrollbar that was visible most prominently on `/settings`. Phase 15E is a **frontend-only responsive polish** that fixes the overflow without removing any safety control.
- **Root cause confirmed:** Topbar inner flex container had no `min-w-0`, none of its `whitespace-nowrap` children could shrink, and the AppLayout right-content wrapper had no horizontal-overflow guard, so a wide Topbar overflowed all the way to `<body>` and surfaced as a page-level scrollbar.
- **Backend (Phase 15E): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit.
- **Frontend (Phase 15E):**
  - **Helper extended** [`frontend/src/utils/topbarSafetySummary.ts`](frontend/src/utils/topbarSafetySummary.ts): adds a `compactLabel` field to `TopbarSafetySummary`. The compact label drops the "Safety:" prefix, abbreviates `Sandbox ON/OFF` → `SBOX ON/OFF`, and drops the leading "Briefing" word so the briefing status appears bare (`STALE`, `READY`, `CRIT`, `—`). Pure-helper change; no fetch, no DOM, no React.
  - **Topbar** [`frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx): inner flex container now carries `min-w-0` so children can shrink; the search wrapper now also carries `min-w-0 shrink` (collapses gracefully at narrower desktop widths instead of forcing overflow); the Safety Pill renders BOTH labels — full `<span className="hidden xl:inline">{label}</span>` at `xl+` (≥1280px) and compact `<span className="xl:hidden">{compactLabel}</span>` below `xl` — and the pill itself gains `shrink-0` so a sibling shrinkable element compresses first. `aria-label` and `title` always carry the full long-form tooltip regardless of which visual label is shown.
  - **AppLayout** [`frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/AppLayout.tsx): the right-content wrapper (the div with the `lg:pl-[260px]` / `lg:pl-[72px]` offset) now carries `overflow-x-clip` (modern, non-scrolling clip that does NOT establish a new scroll context and does NOT break sticky positioning of the Topbar / Sidebar). `<main>` now also carries `min-w-0` alongside the existing `max-w-[1600px]` so a wide child element (e.g. a future data table) cannot push the layout out — wide tables can still scroll inside their own card by adding `overflow-x-auto` on that card. New `data-testid="app-layout-content"` attribute for structural tests.
- **Final visual behaviour:**
  - Widths ≥ 1280px (xl): full text `Safety: AI Paused · Sandbox OFF · Briefing STALE` (unchanged from Phase 15D).
  - Widths 768px–1279px (md / lg): compact text `AI Paused · SBOX OFF · STALE` — same tone colour, same icon, same `aria-label` carrying the full posture.
  - Widths below 768px: pill hidden (same as Phase 15D — full posture still visible via Sidebar bottom indicator + Settings cards on mobile).
  - All other Topbar controls (existing `AI Paused` button, Live indicator, Ask CEO AI, Bell, user profile, Logout) unchanged in placement and behaviour.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15E NEVER mutates business tables, NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER calls any provider (Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter), NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. The pill remains a `<span role="status">` with `cursor-default` — functionally inert.
- **Tests (Phase 15E):** **7 net new frontend assertions** distributed across two test files. (a) [`frontend/src/test/topbar_safety_summary.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/topbar_safety_summary.test.tsx) gains: `compactLabel` returned in the loading + AI-paused + unavailable cases; new test "compactLabel for all 3-source states drops 'Safety:' prefix and abbreviates Sandbox/Briefing" (4 states asserted); new test "pill has shrink-0 so it cannot be squeezed out of the Topbar"; new test "both full and compact labels render, responsive classes pick which is visible" (asserts `hidden xl:inline` on full + `xl:hidden` on compact). (b) New file [`frontend/src/test/app_layout_overflow.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/app_layout_overflow.test.tsx) with 4 structural assertions: `data-testid="app-layout-content"` wrapper has `overflow-x-clip`; sidebar padding offset preserved; `<main>` carries `min-w-0` alongside `max-w-[1600px]`; the page body still renders inside the layout (no regression).
- **JSDOM limitation:** JSDOM does not implement real layout / scroll measurement, so we assert structural classes rather than measure `document.documentElement.scrollWidth`. Visual confirmation that the body-level horizontal scrollbar is gone requires manual browser validation at 1366px / 1280px widths after VPS deploy.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **189 passed** (Phase 15D baseline 182 + 7 net new Phase 15E assertions across two files). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15E status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. The Topbar Safety Pill still renders for every Director session on `md+` widths; it just now uses a compact label between 768px and 1279px so the Topbar fits without overflow. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15D — Topbar Safety Compact Pill (SHIPPED)

- Phase 14D / 14E / 14E-Hotfix-1 / 15B already surfaced kill-switch / sandbox / briefing state on individual chrome elements; Phase 15D adds a single compact **read-only Topbar pill** so the Director sees the combined safety posture at a glance without scrolling the Sidebar or opening Settings.
- **Backend (Phase 15D): NONE.** No new endpoint, no migration, no model change, no `.env.production` edit. Three existing read-only endpoints are reused: `GET /api/v1/saas/runtime-live-gate/kill-switch/` (Phase 14D), `GET /api/ai/sandbox/status/` (Phase 14E), `GET /api/v1/ceo-orchestration/snapshots/sidebar-status/` (Phase 15B).
- **Frontend (Phase 15D):**
  - **New helper** [`frontend/src/utils/topbarSafetySummary.ts`](frontend/src/utils/topbarSafetySummary.ts) — pure function `computeTopbarSafetySummary({killSwitch, sandbox, briefing, ...errors})` returning `{label, tone, className, tooltip, dataStatus}`. No fetches, no React deps; callers pass the snapshots they already fetched.
  - **Pill placement** in [`frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Topbar.tsx) right after the `OrgBadge` and before the existing `Live` indicator. Visible on `md+` widths so mobile chrome stays uncluttered. Renders as a `<span role="status">` — no button, no anchor, no click handler.
  - **Status priority cascade** (highest first): kill switch paused → warning amber; briefing critical → danger red; partial unavailability or briefing stale → warning amber; sandbox ON → info blue; all-normal → success green; total-unavailability → neutral grey. The pill NEVER claims all-green when any required fetch errored.
  - **Pill text patterns:** "Safety: Checking…" (loading), "Safety: AI Paused · Sandbox OFF · Briefing STALE" (current production posture), "Safety: AI Running · Sandbox ON · Briefing READY" (sandbox active), "Safety: State unavailable" (all three fetches failed), "Safety: AI Running · Sandbox ? · Briefing READY" (partial fail — sandbox unknown).
  - **Tooltip / `aria-label`** carries the long-form per-source breakdown (`Kill Switch: Paused (AI execution blocked). Sandbox: OFF. Briefing: STALE (older than 36h). Read-only summary.`) so screen-reader users get the whole posture, and ends with the literal phrase `Read-only summary` so the Director knows clicking does nothing.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15D NEVER mutates business tables (`Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `WhatsAppMessage` / `Call` / `DiscountOfferLog` / `WhatsAppLifecycleEvent` / `WorkflowStep` / `RescueAttempt`), NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes new `AuditEvent` rows, NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER generates a CEO briefing, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phones / customer PII / prompt bodies / provider payloads / hidden reasoning / raw audit payloads. The pill is `<span role="status">` with `cursor-default` — it is functionally inert.
- **Tests (Phase 15D):** **19 new frontend tests** in [`frontend/src/test/topbar_safety_summary.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/topbar_safety_summary.test.tsx): pure helper (loading / AI paused / sandbox on info / all-normal success / briefing critical danger / briefing stale warning / partial unavailability / kill-switch alone errored / full unavailability / briefing missing not escalated / TONE_CLASS shape) + Topbar render (loading state, AI paused with stale briefing, AI running with sandbox on, total unavailability, partial fetch error never claims all-green, existing AI Paused indicator co-renders, pill is read-only `<span>` with `role="status"`, aria-label / title contain long-form tooltip). Phase 14D regression: `frontend/src/test/kill_switch.test.tsx` extended its `vi.mock("@/services/api")` to include the two new method stubs (`getAiSandboxModeStatus`, `getDirectorBriefingSidebarStatus`) so the new pill never throws on `undefined.then()` — Phase 14D test count unchanged at 9 passed.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests unchanged (no backend code touched); frontend tests **182 passed** (Phase 15C baseline 163 + 19 new Phase 15D tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15D status post-shipping:** the production safety surfaces are NOT changed by this implementation. The new pill renders sanitised data from the same three read-only endpoints already in production. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation. Director's current production view becomes: `Safety: AI Paused · Sandbox OFF · Briefing STALE` (warning tone) in the Topbar, with the existing AI Paused button, Sidebar bottom indicator, and CEO briefing badge unchanged.

### Phase 15C — Audit Timeline Page (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15A gave the Director a focused read-only window into prompt rollback history; Phase 15B added the Sidebar Director Briefing badge. Phase 15C adds the matching **standalone Audit Timeline page** at `/operations/audit-timeline` so the Director can review state changes across the whole Master Event Ledger — filtered by kind / tone / category / text / date range — without ever exposing sensitive body content and without ever mutating state.
- **Backend (Phase 15C):**
  - **New module** [`backend/apps/audit/views.py`](backend/apps/audit/views.py) with `AuditTimelineView` (`GET /api/audit/timeline/`).
  - **New module** [`backend/apps/audit/urls.py`](backend/apps/audit/urls.py); registered in [`backend/config/urls.py`](backend/config/urls.py) under `api/audit/`.
  - **Permission class** `_AdminAndUpAuditTimeline` (admin / director / owner / superuser only). Viewer and anonymous return 403 / 401.
  - **Filters:** `kind` (exact), `tone` (success/info/warning/danger; invalid → 400), `category` (safety / rollback / ai_governance / whatsapp / payments / orders / delivery / auth_system / other — invalid → 400), `q` (case-insensitive substring against `text` only; payload bodies are NEVER substring-searched), `date_from` / `date_to` (ISO 8601), `limit` (default 50, hard-capped at 200), `offset`.
  - **Category mapping** is prefix-derived in pure Python via `categorize_kind()`; rollback wins over safety where they overlap (`ai.prompt_version.rolled_back` is rollback first). Delivery is checked before payments so `razorpay.courier_execution.*` lands under delivery, not payments. Unknown kinds safely fall through to `other`.
  - **Sanitisation contract.** The endpoint NEVER returns the raw `AuditEvent.payload`. The serialiser slices via a 70-entry allow-list (phase / source / actor / agent / IDs / stage / status / counts / tier / labels / boolean flags / SHA-256 hashes), with a separate 50-entry hard-reject list (token, access_token, refresh_token, verify_token, app_secret, secret, api_key, METAA_WA_TOKEN, VAPI_API_KEY, phone, customer_phone, email, customer_email, address, card, vpa, upi, raw_response, raw_payload, raw_signature, gateway_reference_id, payment_url, system_policy, role_prompt, instruction_payload, messages, provider_payload, transcript, reply_text, inbound_text, customer_name, director_signoff, metadata, evidence_json, briefing_text, …). String values are truncated defensively at 200 chars with an ellipsis. Non-dict payloads return `{}`. The 405 contract is explicit: POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE return 405 even though DRF would default to 405 anyway, so the read-only intent is unambiguous in code review.
  - **Pagination semantics:** `categories=` filter is applied in Python after a windowed DB fetch (`min((limit+offset)*4, MAX_LIMIT*8)`); other filters are pure DB. Secondary order-by `-id` so SQLite millisecond ties resolve deterministically (Phase 15A precedent).
- **Frontend (Phase 15C):**
  - **New page** [`frontend/src/pages/AuditTimeline.tsx`](frontend/src/pages/AuditTimeline.tsx) with header + filter bar + timeline table + empty/loading/error states + pagination footer. No "Send / Approve / Execute / Submit / Resume AI / Mutate / Kill / Toggle / Apply Rollback / Generate Briefing / Confirm / Go Live" controls anywhere (asserted in tests).
  - **New route** `/operations/audit-timeline` in [`frontend/src/App.tsx`](frontend/src/App.tsx).
  - **New Sidebar nav item** under the Governance group in [`frontend/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx`](frontend/src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx) (lucide-react `ScrollText` icon).
  - **New TS types** `AuditTimelineTone`, `AuditTimelineCategory`, `AuditTimelineItem`, `AuditTimelineResponse`, `AuditTimelineFilters` in [`frontend/src/types/domain.ts`](frontend/src/types/domain.ts).
  - **New api method** `getAuditTimeline(filters)` in [`frontend/src/services/api.ts`](frontend/src/services/api.ts) with deterministic dev mock fallback.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15C NEVER mutates business tables (`Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `WhatsAppMessage` / `Call` / `DiscountOfferLog`), NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates an `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes a new `AuditEvent` (the endpoint is pure SELECT + computed category), NEVER changes `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / OpenAI / Anthropic / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER exposes secrets / tokens / verify tokens / app secrets / full phone numbers / full customer names / full prompt bodies / hidden reasoning / provider payloads / medical content. Verified in `test_defensive_safety_no_provider_no_mutation` by patching `queue_template_message` + `send_freeform_text_message` + `trigger_call_for_lead` + `create_shipment` and exercising every filter path, plus snapshotting `audit / customer / lead / order / payment` row counts before/after every request and asserting identity. Verified in `test_sanitisation_drops_forbidden_keys` by inserting a poisoned payload with **24 forbidden values** (leaked-jwt-token / leaked-app-secret / +919999999999 / leaked@example.com / 12 Some Lane / 4111111111111111 / Real Customer Name / Razorpay key / Razorpay URL / system_policy body / role_prompt body / instruction_payload blob / messages array / transcript / reply_text / customer_name / director_signoff / metadata / evidence_json / etc) and grepping the full response JSON for each forbidden value.
- **Tests (Phase 15C):** **27 new backend tests** in [`backend/tests/test_phase15c_audit_timeline.py`](backend/tests/test_phase15c_audit_timeline.py): auth (anon / viewer blocked, admin / director allowed), empty timeline, newest-first ordering across 10 seeded kinds, response shape allow-list, kind / tone / category / q / date_range filters with happy paths + invalid → 400, pagination (limit / offset / hard-cap at 200 / zero-falls-back-to-default / garbage-falls-back), sanitisation of 24 forbidden payload values, long-string truncation to ≤ 200 chars, non-dict payload → `{}`, write-verb 405 contract for POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE, defensive safety across every filter path, and a pure-function `categorize_kind` unit test mapping 22 representative kinds across all 9 categories. **14 new frontend tests** in [`frontend/src/test/audit_timeline.test.tsx`](frontend/src/test/audit_timeline.test.tsx): header + filter bar render, loading / empty / error / populated states, sanitised payload rendering, no forbidden-value DOM leak, no Send/Approve/Execute/Mutate/Kill/Toggle/Apply/Resume/Confirm/Go-Live buttons, category / tone / kind / q filter wiring to api, Clear filters resets every input + offset, Next/Previous pagination drives `offset`, Previous disabled on first page, Sidebar nav item links to `/operations/audit-timeline`.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests **2832 collected** (Phase 15B baseline 2805 + 27 new Phase 15C tests); frontend tests **163 passed** (Phase 15B baseline 149 + 14 new Phase 15C tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15C status post-shipping:** the production `AuditEvent` rows are NOT changed by this implementation. The new endpoint reads existing rows only; the new page renders sanitised data only. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger, no provider call, no Celery enqueue, no business mutation.

### Phase 15B — Sidebar Director Daily Briefing Badge (SHIPPED)

- Phase 15A gave the Director read-only audit visibility for past prompt rollbacks. Phase 15B adds the matching **at-a-glance Director Briefing status badge** on the Sidebar so the Director knows whether the latest CEO AI orchestration snapshot is ready, stale, critical, or missing without having to click into the CEO AI Briefing page.
- **Discovery findings (no assumptions, verified directly):**
  - **Source:** `apps.agents.ceo_orchestration.models.CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` (Phase 9F). Fields used: `snapshot_at`, `business_health_score`, `health_tier` (Phase 9F `HealthTier` enum: critical / poor / fair / good / excellent). The existing daily Celery task (`run_ceo_orchestration_agent_daily`) writes one row per day at 13:00 IST.
  - **Pre-existing read endpoint:** `GET /api/v1/ceo-orchestration/snapshots/latest/` (Phase 9F). Returns the full snapshot **including `briefingText`** — the multi-line internal Director briefing body. Too heavy + too revealing for a Sidebar fetch that fires on every mount; Phase 15B adds a slimmer dedicated endpoint instead of widening Sidebar consumption of the heavy one.
  - **Frontend CEO page route:** `/ceo-ai` (already registered in `App.tsx`; consumed via the existing Sidebar nav item with `Sparkles` icon in the AI Layer group). The badge attaches to that nav item — no new route added.
- **Backend endpoint added (Phase 15B):** `GET /api/v1/ceo-orchestration/snapshots/sidebar-status/` — new `CeoOrchestrationSidebarStatusView` at [`backend/apps/agents/ceo_orchestration/views.py`](backend/apps/agents/ceo_orchestration/views.py). Permission `_AdminPermission` (admin/director/owner/superuser only — same class as the Phase 9F sibling endpoints); viewer + anonymous refused. POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE return 405 (asserted in tests).
- **Slim allow-list response shape:** the endpoint returns exactly 8 keys — `status` ∈ {`"ready"`, `"stale"`, `"critical"`, `"missing"`}, `label` (short human-readable), `latestSnapshotId`, `latestSnapshotAt` (ISO-8601), `ageMinutes`, `healthScore` (0–100), `tier` (Phase 9F HealthTier), `targetRoute` (always `"/ceo-ai"`). The heavy fields the Phase 9F `/snapshots/latest/` endpoint exposes — `briefingText`, `crossCuttingAlerts`, `top3Priorities`, `agentStatusSummary`, `alerts`, `agentRunId`, `cfo_snapshot_id`, etc. — are **deliberately absent** from the sidebar response. Tested explicitly with a poisoned snapshot whose `briefing_text`, `cross_cutting_alerts`, `top_3_priorities`, `agent_status_summary`, and `alerts` are all stuffed with marker strings; every one of those markers is asserted absent from the response JSON.
- **Status precedence:** computed entirely from existing model fields (no new fields, no migration):
  1. `health_tier == "critical"` → `status="critical"`, badge text `"crit"`, red tone, regardless of age.
  2. `(now - snapshot_at) >= 36h` → `status="stale"`, badge text `"stale"`, amber tone.
  3. Otherwise → `status="ready"`, badge text `"ready"`, green tone.
  4. No snapshot exists → `status="missing"`, badge text `"—"`, neutral grey tone.
  5. Loading / 401 / 403 / generic error → badge shows `"…"`, `"auth"`, `"—"`, `"—"` respectively with neutral grey tone; **never** "ready". The 36h threshold matches Phase 9F's daily 13:00 IST cadence — anything older means we missed >1 full sweep.
- **Frontend wiring:**
  - New types `DirectorBriefingStatus` + `DirectorBriefingSidebarStatus` in [`frontend/src/types/domain.ts`](frontend/src/types/domain.ts).
  - New api method `api.getDirectorBriefingSidebarStatus()` in [`frontend/src/services/api.ts`](frontend/src/services/api.ts). Deterministic optimistic fallback returns `status="missing"` (NEVER "ready") so dev mode + offline never claim a ready briefing when the backend is unreachable.
  - Sidebar fetches the briefing status on mount alongside the existing Phase 14D kill-switch + Phase 14E sandbox state fetches (3 parallel GETs; all admin-gated; all read-only). New private helper `computeBriefingBadge` at the bottom of `Sidebar.tsx` maps the 4 backend statuses + 3 client-side states (loading / auth-error / permission-error / generic-error) to a 4-key visual contract (`shortLabel`, `title`, `className`, `dataStatus`).
  - Badge is rendered as a small `<span>` with `data-testid="sidebar-briefing-badge"` + `data-briefing-status` attribute (for e2e visibility) **next to** the existing "CEO AI Briefing" nav item — not as a separate button. The surrounding `NavLink` owns navigation to `/ceo-ai`; the badge is a passive label. No new clickable surface added.
  - Hidden when the sidebar is collapsed (the icon-only collapsed view doesn't have horizontal space; the safety indicator at the bottom also hides on collapse — same pattern).
- **No raw briefing body in the UI.** The badge renders only the short label + a `title` tooltip carrying age + health score. No prompt body, no priorities, no cross-cutting alerts, no agent status summary surface anywhere in the Sidebar.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15B NEVER triggers a new orchestration run, NEVER enqueues a Celery task, NEVER mutates `CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` / `PromptVersion` / any business table, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / WhatsApp / OpenAI / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter, NEVER creates an `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER writes an `AuditEvent` (the new endpoint is pure SELECT + computed status), NEVER edits `.env.production`, NEVER touches `RuntimeKillSwitch` / `SandboxState` state. Verified by a defensive integration test that patches `apps.agents.ceo_orchestration.tasks.run_ceo_orchestration_agent_daily` + 4 outbound provider entrypoints and asserts `assert_not_called` across a full GET, plus snapshots all 10 model row counts (including `CeoOrchestrationSnapshot`) before/after and asserts identity.
- **Existing surfaces preserved:**
  - Topbar AI Kill Switch (Phase 14D) — unchanged.
  - Settings AI Kill Switch / Sandbox Mode / Rollback System / Rollback History cards (Phase 14D + 14E + 14F + 15A) — unchanged.
  - Sidebar bottom safety indicator (Phase 14E-Hotfix-1) — unchanged. The existing safety_status test mock was extended with `getDirectorBriefingSidebarStatus` so the Sidebar's new third api fetch doesn't crash any existing Sidebar render test.
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests **2805 collected** (Phase 15A baseline 2795 + 10 new Phase 15B tests); regression batch across Phase 3D + 4C + 14D + 14E + 14F + 15A + 15B = 116 passed; frontend tests **149 passed** (Phase 15A baseline 138 + 11 new Phase 15B tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15B status post-shipping:** the production `CeoOrchestrationSnapshot` rows are NOT changed by this implementation. The new endpoint reads the existing rows; the Sidebar badge renders a 4-character status pill driven by the existing daily Celery sweep. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. No briefing generation, no orchestration trigger.

### Phase 15A — Rollback History View (SHIPPED)

- Phase 14F gave the Director a typed-phrase + reason-gated way to roll back AI prompt versions from Settings. Phase 15A adds the matching **read-only audit visibility surface** so the Director can review past rollbacks — who, what, when, why — without leaving the Settings page and without any risk of accidentally executing a new rollback.
- **Discovery findings (no assumptions, verified directly):**
  - **Audit model:** `apps.audit.AuditEvent` at [`backend/apps/audit/models.py:6`](backend/apps/audit/models.py#L6). Fields: `occurred_at`, `icon`, `text`, `tone`, `kind`, `payload`, `organization`. Indexed on `(-occurred_at, kind)` for cheap kind-filtered lookups.
  - **No pre-existing REST endpoint for audit events.** The audit app ships only `models`, `signals`, `realtime`, `consumers` (WebSocket fanout). The dashboard activity feed (`/api/dashboard/activity/`) surfaces raw `AuditEvent` rows but does not filter by `kind`, and exposes too much raw payload for the Phase 15A use case. Phase 15A adds a new dedicated, sanitised endpoint instead of widening that surface.
  - **Audit kinds covered:** exactly two — `prompt_version.rollback.ui_changed` (Phase 14F UI source) + `ai.prompt_version.rolled_back` (Phase 3D service, also the backing row of every Phase 14F UI flip). Other audit kinds (`ai.prompt_version.activated`, `ai.sandbox.enabled`, `runtime.kill_switch.ui_changed`, etc.) are deliberately excluded so unrelated audit payloads never leak into this surface.
- **Backend endpoint added (Phase 15A):** `GET /api/ai/prompt-versions/rollback-history/` — new `PromptVersionRollbackHistoryView` in [`backend/apps/ai_governance/views.py`](backend/apps/ai_governance/views.py). Permission `_AdminAndUpAlways` (admin/director-only); viewer + anonymous refused. Query params: `agent` (filter), `kind` (filter — must be one of the two allow-listed kinds; 400 otherwise), `limit` (default 50, hard-capped at 200 so a malformed value cannot drain the audit table), `offset` (pagination). Response shape: `{items, count, limit, offset, kindsIncluded}` where every `items[]` row is composed from the **allow-list slice** of the underlying audit payload — see "data safety" below.
- **Data safety contract.** The endpoint defines an explicit allow-list of payload keys (`phase, source, action, actor, agent, previous_active_version_id, previous_version_id, previous_version, previous_version_label, target_version_id, target_version, target_version_label, reason, by, matrix_action, matrix_status`). Every other key in the raw payload is silently dropped by `_safe_payload_slice` BEFORE the row is serialised. Tested explicitly with a poisoned audit row that stuffs `system_policy`, `role_prompt`, `instruction_payload`, `razorpay_key_secret`, `meta_wa_token`, `vapi_api_key`, `openai_api_key`, `phone`, `email` into the payload — all of those are scrubbed and asserted absent from the JSON response. Phase 14F UI rows and Phase 3D service rows use slightly different payload schemas (UI uses `previous_active_version_id` + `actor`; service uses `previous_version_id` + `by`); the serializer normalises both into the same camelCase fields so the frontend renders uniformly.
- **Deterministic ordering.** Rows sorted by `(-occurred_at, -id)`. The secondary `-id` tiebreaker matters on SQLite (test DB) where `auto_now_add` timestamps can collide in the same millisecond, and on production bursts where two audit rows might share a timestamp. Operators always see "the most recent insertion first" regardless of resolution limits.
- **Audit ledger consistency improvement (small bonus).** Added `prompt_version.rollback.ui_changed` to [`apps/audit/signals.py::ICON_BY_KIND`](backend/apps/audit/signals.py) (Phase 14F shipped without registering its icon — every fanout used the generic `activity` icon). Phase 15A registers it as `rotate-ccw` matching the legacy `ai.prompt_version.rolled_back` icon, so the audit-event WebSocket fanout finally surfaces the correct visual for UI-driven rollbacks.
- **Frontend wiring (Settings + modal):**
  - New `frontend/src/components/RollbackHistoryModal.tsx` renders a 6-column table (Time / Agent / Change / Reason / Actor / Source). Loading / empty / error states each have a dedicated `data-testid` (`rollback-history-loading`, `rollback-history-empty`, `rollback-history-error`) for e2e visibility. The error state has a `data-error-status` attribute (`"401"`, `"403"`, or `""`) so the operator + tests can distinguish authentication failures from generic outages with distinct copy: HTTP 401 → "Session expired or unauthenticated."; HTTP 403 → "You do not have permission to view rollback history."; anything else → "Rollback history unavailable.". Agent filter dropdown surfaces only agents that actually have rollback history in the current response, narrowing the next refetch via the `?agent=` query param.
  - Settings page card gains a secondary **"View rollback history"** ghost-variant button next to the Phase 14F "Choose rollback target…" outline button. The history button is **always enabled** (an operator may want to review past rollbacks even when no current candidates exist), but the modal is lazy-mounted so the list endpoint is only hit when the operator opens it.
  - **No prompt body rendered.** The modal renders only safe metadata (`agent`, `version` labels, `actor`, `reason`, `source`, `summary`). `systemPolicy` / `rolePrompt` / `instructionPayload` are not read by the modal — even if a future API change accidentally surfaced them, the modal would silently ignore them. Tested with a poisoned mock that stuffs `DO-NOT-RENDER-POISONED-PROMPT-BODY` into the response.
- **Defensive safety contract.** Phase 15A NEVER mutates `PromptVersion`, NEVER creates `ApprovalRequest`, NEVER invokes `rollback_prompt_version`, NEVER calls Razorpay / Meta Cloud / Delhivery / Vapi / WhatsApp / OpenAI / NVIDIA / NIM / OpenRouter, NEVER mutates `Order` / `Payment` / `Customer` / `Lead` / `Shipment` / `WhatsAppMessage` / `Call` / `DiscountOfferLog`, NEVER touches `RuntimeKillSwitch` or `SandboxState` state, NEVER edits `.env.production`. Verified by a defensive integration test that patches `apps.ai_governance.prompt_versions.rollback_prompt_version`, `queue_template_message`, `send_freeform_text_message`, `trigger_call_for_lead`, `create_shipment` and asserts `assert_not_called` across a GET, plus snapshots all 9 model row counts (including `PromptVersion`) before/after and asserts identity. POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE on the endpoint all return 405 (asserted in tests).
- **Verification baseline:** backend tests **2795 collected** (Phase 14F baseline 2783 + 12 new Phase 15A tests); regression batch across Phase 3D + 4C + 14D + 14E + 14F + 15A = 106 passed; frontend tests **138 passed** (Phase 14F baseline 127 + 11 new Phase 15A tests). `makemigrations --check --dry-run` clean; `manage.py check` clean; lint 0 errors; build clean.
- **Phase 15A status post-shipping:** the production `PromptVersion` rows were NOT changed by this implementation. The new endpoint and the new modal are strictly read-only. The Director can open the modal, filter by agent, refresh — none of those actions write anything anywhere. Production `RuntimeKillSwitch` ("AI Paused") and `SandboxState` ("Sandbox OFF") states are untouched. The Phase 14F rollback execution flow remains the only path that can write a rollback event; Phase 15A only reveals them.

### Phase 14F — Rollback System UI Wiring (SHIPPED)

- Closes the third and final Phase 14B audit gap: the Settings page "Rollback System" card was a mock `toast.success("Rolled back to v3.2")` button. Phase 14F wires it to the canonical `apps.ai_governance.prompt_versions.rollback_prompt_version` service via a typed-phrase + reason-gated POST on a new dedicated UI endpoint — no new model, no new migration, no parallel rollback concept.
- **Discovery findings (no assumptions, verified directly):**
  - **Model:** `apps.ai_governance.models.PromptVersion` at [`backend/apps/ai_governance/models.py:138`](backend/apps/ai_governance/models.py#L138). Fields include `id`, `agent` (FK-like to `AgentRun.Agent`), `version`, `title`, `system_policy`, `role_prompt`, `instruction_payload`, `is_active`, `status` (draft / sandbox / active / rolled_back / archived), `created_by`, `metadata`, `created_at`, `activated_at`, `rolled_back_at`, `rollback_reason`. A partial unique constraint enforces **one active version per agent**.
  - **Service:** `apps.ai_governance.prompt_versions.rollback_prompt_version(target_version_id, reason, by_user)` at [`backend/apps/ai_governance/prompt_versions.py:118`](backend/apps/ai_governance/prompt_versions.py#L118). Direct mutation under `@transaction.atomic`: flips the current active row → `is_active=False, status=ROLLED_BACK, rolled_back_at=now, rollback_reason=reason`; flips the target row → `is_active=True, status=ACTIVE, activated_at=now`. Writes `ai.prompt_version.rolled_back` audit row automatically.
  - **Existing Phase 3D endpoint preserved:** `POST /api/ai/prompt-versions/<pk>/rollback/` (legacy) keeps working unchanged — the Governance page consumes it.
  - **Matrix policy:** `ai.prompt_version.activate` covers both activate AND rollback per the description "Activate or rollback an AI prompt version". Mode = `approval_required`; approver = `admin`. The endpoint permission already requires admin/director (`_AdminAndUpAlways`), so the role gate is effectively the matrix gate. The activate view records `mark_auto_approved` for the matrix audit trail; the legacy rollback view does not. Phase 14F closes this gap on the new UI endpoint.
